# nUIget 2.0 parity audit — 1.18.9 (`0457c75`) vs `labs/2.0` (2026-10-16)

Produced by a 134-agent read-only code audit (seven areas, three-lens refuters on the high/medium claims, completeness critic) after the owner's hands-on test of `nuiget-v2.0.0.vsix`. Nothing was rendered by the audit; the owner's 36 screenshots (16 of 1.18.9, 20 of 2.0) are the visual evidence and a reference set of 1.18.9 screenshots lives in `docs/ux/reference-1.18.9/`.

Verification column: **OK** = survived three independent refuters, **KILLED** = refuted by at least two, **unverified** = low severity or beyond the per-area cap (treat as plausible, confirm while implementing). Corrections from the refuters are included; they refine citations and severity, never widen scope.

The headline numbers double-count: the icon gap is filed seven times (MI1, MU1, MU2, MB2, DP1, DP2, DP5), the comfortable-row layout five times (MI3, MU11, MB1, MH5, DP7). The plan's P9 items de-duplicate them.

## Main panel — Installed tab (rows, sections, counts, selection, bulk actions, empty/loading states, transitive section)

### MI1 — Installed rows render no package icon (not even the bundled default) although the host sends one and Row accepts it

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Every installed row showed a 32 px icon: `pkg.iconUrl` with fallback to the bundled default (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:186-192; App.css:92-104). The host sent `installedPackages` at once and `installedPackagesMetadata` with iconUrl/verified/authors as a second phase (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:303-335); App.tsx merged it into the rows (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:443-466; all-projects at :1070-1105). The default icon came from `data-package-icon` on the root (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:1397,1409; App.tsx:22).
- 2.0: `InstalledProjectSection.tsx` passes no `icon` prop to `Row` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136) although `Row` renders `icon` in comfortable density (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx:37,97) and the slice already holds `metadata.get(id).iconUrl` from `transitive/metadata` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/installed.tsx:66-87; enricher sets it as a `data:` URI at origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/enricher.ts:56,68-70). The host still emits `data-package-icon` on the root with a CSP that allows it (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/services.ts:106-108,112), but no 2.0 webview file reads `dataset`/`package-icon` (grep over src/webview: no hit); `BrowseList.tsx:111` shows an icon only for `data:` URIs and nothing otherwise. Contradicts docs/ux/interaction-model.md:11 ("Comfortable rows (icons, description, badges)") and ADR-006 ("the webview shows the bundled default icon", docs/adr/006-icons-and-csp.md:18).
- Fix: 1) Add a tiny shared helper (e.g. `src/webview/shared/defaultPackageIcon.ts`) that reads `document.getElementById(rootId)?.dataset.packageIcon` once (both roots: `root` and the sidebar root id) and exports the URI. 2) In InstalledProjectSection pass `icon={<img className="nuiget-row-icon-img" src={metadata.get(id.toLowerCase())?.iconUrl ?? defaultPackageIcon} alt="" onError={fallback to default} />}`; reuse the same in BrowseList (replace the `startsWith('data:')` guard with the default fallback) so both comfortable lists agree. 3) Add `.nuiget-row-icon-img { width/height: var(--nuiget-icon-size-lg); object-fit: contain }` in primitives.css (move `.nuiget-browse-icon` from main.css to it). Do NOT add a new icon component: `src/test/lint/webview-icons-pure.test.ts` asserts exactly 26 memo icons. Keep every class `nuiget-` prefixed and no colour literal (webview-stylesheets/webview-colours lint).
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, `src/webview/main/main.css`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx`
- Test: InstalledTab.test.tsx: after answering `transitive/metadata` with `{ id:'Serilog', version:'3.0.0', iconUrl:'data:image/png;base64,AAA' }` (pattern at origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/installed.test.tsx:186), assert `within(optionOf('Serilog')).container.querySelector('.nuiget-row-icon img')` has `src` starting with `data:`; a row without metadata shows `src` equal to the root's `data-package-icon` (set `document.getElementById('root').dataset.packageIcon` in the test). Row.test.tsx: keep the existing comfortable/compact icon test green.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/installed src/webview/shared/primitives && npm run lint && npm test && node esbuild.mjs --production --analyze`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands as written; three precision notes for the fix phase: (1) `InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124` DOES pass `description={metadata.get(id)?.description}` to Row — the parent brief's "description never reaches the Installed rows" is wrong for the main panel; only `icon`, `verified`, and `authors` are dropped. (2) The P5.4 ruling (origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/STATUS.md:2229) specified "icon from the slice's metadata when present" only for `TransitiveSection` rows and said nothing about `InstalledProjectSection`; the `icon={` grep shows `TransitiveSection.tsx` does not pass it either, so the same gap covers transitive rows and the ruling omission is the likely origin. (3) `InstalledPackage.iconUrl/verified/authors` exist in the protocol (domain.ts:63-65) but no `src/core` writer sets them on `installed/get` results (the only `iconUrl` writers are `Catalog.ts:259-260` for `PackageUpdate` and the enricher); the sole icon path for installed rows is the separate `transitive/metadata` ask, so the fix is a webview-only change: pass `icon` from `metadata.get(id)?.iconUrl` (data: URI) with a shared default-icon helper that reads `#root[data-package-icon]`, plus a test in `InstalledTab.test.tsx`.
  - Claim stands as written; two sharpenings. (1) The only documented 2.0 rule that could be mistaken for intent is "icons are `data:` URIs only" (ARCHITECTURE.md:613, BrowseList.test.tsx:80, ADR-006:19-23 CSP). That rule governs remote icon URLs, not the bundled default: ADR-006:18 explicitly intends the default and the CSP's `img-src ${webview.cspSource}` admits the `asWebviewUri` of resources/package-icon.png that services.ts:106 already emits. So the root cause is feature-not-rendered on two independent paths: (a) `metadata.get(id).iconUrl` never passed to Row, and (b) the default icon never read from the root dataset — (b) alone would already give every comfortable row an icon, as in 1.x. (2) The same omission applies to UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 (no `icon` prop, while ARCHITECTURE.md:430 says updates rows are "enriched (icon, authors, verification)"); that is a sibling gap outside MI1's scope.
  - Severity high stands; the claim is right but incomplete in four ways the fix phase should carry:

1. The gap is upstream of the feature code. The P5.4 implementation spec (origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/STATUS.md:2229) prescribes "icon from the slice's metadata when present" only for TransitiveSection rows, which are `density="compact"` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/TransitiveSection.tsx:77) where Row discards `icon` anyway (Row.tsx:97), and says nothing about icons for InstalledProjectSection, the comfortable list that could render them. The execution-plan row P5.4 (origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/2026-10-11-nuiget-2.0-execution-plan.md:277) names no icon acceptance either, and no UI test asserts one (git grep -i icon over src/test/ui/v2 hits only theme.ui.test.ts:105, unrelated). Root cause is therefore "feature-not-rendered" plus "spec omission"; the fix must add an acceptance line (interaction-model §4 or the STATUS row) and a test, or it regresses silently.

2. The same gap exists in both Updates lists: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx:181-185 and AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-114 pass no `icon` to comfortable rows. And BrowseList shows an icon only for `data:` URIs with no default fallback (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:111), contradicting ADR-006:18. One shared `packageIcon(iconUrl?)` helper (reads the root dataset once, returns `<img className="nuiget-package-icon" src={iconUrl ?? default} alt="" onError=fallback>`) plus moving `.nuiget-browse-icon` (main.css:200-204) into primitives.css as `.nuiget-package-icon` fixes Installed, Updates, All-Projects Updates and Browse in one ≤1-day item. Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main src/webview/shared` with a new InstalledTab.test.tsx assertion that each `package-row-*` contains an `img` whose `src` is the metadata `data:` URI when present and the dataset default otherwise; `npm run package:vsix`.

3. Scope precision: the 2.0 Installed row is not literally "plain text". InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124 passes `description` from metadata and :129-132 passes CPM/Vulnerable badges, so within MI1 the icon is the isolated missing visual. This narrows the claim without lowering severity: the 32px image was the largest element in every 1.x row.

4. The 1.x details panel never showed the icon (git grep package-icon|iconUrl over 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/PackageDetailsPanel.tsx: no hit) and 2.0's does not either (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsInfo.tsx has only WarningIcon), so the fix should not add one there as "parity".

### MI2 — Installed rows have no authors line and no verified badge although the slice holds both

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Each row had a muted authors line with a VerifiedIcon glyph when the prefix is reserved (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:235-243; App.css:559-575). Data came from the two-phase `installedPackagesMetadata` enrichment (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:318-324; App.tsx:443-466).
- 2.0: `PackageMetadataLite` carries `authors` and `verified` (origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/domain.ts:189-197), the enricher fills them (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/enricher.ts:59-64), the installed slice stores them by lower-cased id (installed.tsx:77-86) and the All Projects `metadata` chunk does too (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/allProjects.tsx:119-123; Catalog.ts:341-359). InstalledProjectSection reads only `description` and `vulnerabilities` from that map (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:41-42,124,131) and renders no authors and no Verified badge; sibling lists do show Verified (BrowseList.tsx:114, UpdatesTab.tsx:191). `VerifiedIcon` exists (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/icons.tsx:151).
- Fix: In `renderRow`, read `const lite = metadata.get(entry.id.toLowerCase())`; add `{lite?.verified ? <Badge label="Verified" tone="info" title="The package id prefix is reserved by its owner" /> : null}` to `badges` (same wording as UpdatesTab.tsx:191), and pass the authors to the row's secondary line: with the MI3 layout, `meta={lite?.authors ? `by ${lite.authors}` : undefined}`; until MI3 lands, prepend `by ${authors}` to `description` with ` · ` as BrowseList.tsx:109 does. Read everything from the slice's `metadata` map, never from `entry.authors`/`entry.verified`: `rows.ts` never sets those fields (see notes).
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`
- Test: InstalledTab.test.tsx: answer `transitive/metadata` with `{ id:'Serilog', version:'3.0.0', authors:'Serilog Contributors', verified:true }`; assert `optionOf('Serilog')` has accessible description matching /reserved by its owner/ and the row text contains 'Serilog Contributors'. Add an All Projects variant driven by a `{ kind:'metadata' }` chunk (pattern in AllProjectsView.test.tsx).
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/installed && npm run lint && npm test`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; three precision notes for the fix item, none of which weakens it. (1) 1.x nested the Verified glyph INSIDE the `{pkg.authors && ...}` block (InstalledTab.tsx:235-243), so a verified package with no authors string showed no badge; 2.0's siblings render `<Badge label="Verified" tone="info">` independently of authors (BrowseList.tsx:114, UpdatesTab.tsx:191) — the fix should follow the 2.0 pattern, not replicate 1.x's gating. (2) 2.0 does surface Authors for the SELECTED package as a fact row in the details pane (DetailsInfo.tsx:25-26), but never `verified`; the gap is row-level (and the Verified badge is absent from the installed path entirely). (3) 2.0's `InstalledProjectSection` is the single row renderer for both single-project and All-Projects (`grouped`) modes, and both slices already hold the data, so one change there (read `metadata.get(id)?.authors`/`?.verified`, add a Verified `Badge` beside CPM/Vulnerable and fold authors into the `description` string the way BrowseList.tsx:109 does, or add an authors slot to Row) closes the gap in both modes.
  - The claim stands, but its two halves have different strength and it misses the fix shape:

- Verified badge (firm, keep high): data, Badge primitive and VerifiedIcon all exist; sibling comfortable lists render it; §1 "badges" covers it. Pure feature-not-rendered in InstalledProjectSection.tsx:129-132.

- Authors line (parity loss, not a documented decision — but weaker): interaction-model §1 enumerates row content as "icons, description, badges" without authors, and §7 places authors in the Details tab (2.0 does show them there: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsInfo.tsx:25-26). So the design doc neither mandates nor forbids authors on rows. Note also that 2.0 dropped authors from Updates rows too (UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 renders name/version/badges/actions only; 1.x showed them at 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:703-708), while Browse folds them into the description string as `by <authors> · …` (BrowseList.tsx:109). The drop is consistent across Installed and Updates and undocumented — the owner may ratify it as a deliberate trim, but nothing on the branch does; if ratified, §1 must say so per the doc's own rule.

- Fix shape: `Row` has no authors slot (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx:28-49: name, version, versionTitle, description, icon, badges, status, busy, disabled, actions, density). The ≤1-day fix should follow the BrowseList precedent (compose authors into `description`, add `<Badge label="Verified">` to `badges`) in InstalledProjectSection.tsx and UpdatesTab.tsx, with a test in InstalledTab.test.tsx asserting the badge and `by <authors>` from a metadata entry — not a new primitive prop.
  - Split MI2: high holds narrowly for the Verified badge (information gone for installed packages everywhere in 2.0, owner named it, inconsistent with BrowseList/UpdatesTab); medium for the authors line (relocated to the Details tab per interaction-model §7, which is also where 1.x showed it). Root cause stays feature-not-rendered; no host, protocol, slice or primitive change is needed. Fix (<= 1 hour, one file + one test): in `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx` read `const meta = metadata.get(entry.id.toLowerCase())` and (1) mirror `BrowseList.tsx:109`: `description={[meta?.authors ? \`by ${meta.authors}\` : '', meta?.description].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')}`; (2) mirror `UpdatesTab.tsx:191` inside `badges`: `{meta?.verified ? <Badge label="Verified" tone="info" title="The package id prefix is reserved by its owner" /> : null}`. Test: extend the `transitive/metadata` response at `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx:234-236` with `authors: 'Serilog Contributors', verified: true` on one entry and assert the row shows text matching `/by Serilog Contributors/` and a `Verified` badge, and that an entry without `verified` shows none; add the same to `AllProjectsView.test.tsx` for the `metadata` chunk. Gate: `npm run test:frontend` (then `npm test`). Do not regress what 2.0 does better: single-line row keeps the virtualised `Listbox`, `aria-describedby` carries badges+description (`Row.tsx:62,93,102-108`), description already shows on installed rows (1.x did not), and muted text switches to `inherit` on selected/current rows for contrast (`primitives.css:168-175`).

### MI3 — Comfortable row is a single 44 px line: name is squeezed by the right-aligned description, which can overflow the row (by reading, not rendered)

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `css` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: A stacked 66 px row: icon column, then name + inline badges, version line, authors line (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:42,183-247; App.css:70-130, 123-128), with a bottom border per row and a left accent on the selected row (App.css:70-90).
- 2.0: `Row` lays everything on one flex line: `.nuiget-row-option` is `display:flex; align-items:center` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:90-100); `.nuiget-row-title` has `min-width:0` and `.nuiget-row-name` ellipsizes (143-154); `.nuiget-row-desc` sits at `margin-inline-start:auto` with `min-width:0` (177-183) and `.nuiget-row-description` is `white-space:nowrap` with no `overflow:hidden` (156-162), so a long description (passed at InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124) is a flex child with default `min-width:auto` that paints past its container while the name shrinks toward nothing. Row height is 44 px (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/tokens.css:26; Listbox.tsx:8 mirrors it). The CSS claim is from reading the stylesheet, not from a render. docs/ux/interaction-model.md:11 promises description in comfortable rows; it does not promise one line.
- Fix: Give comfortable rows a stacked anatomy: `.nuiget-row--comfortable .nuiget-row-option` becomes icon + a `.nuiget-row-text` column (title line: name, version, badges; second line: `description`/`meta` ellipsized with `overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis`) + actions; keep `.nuiget-row-desc` right-aligned only in compact rows. Raise `--nuiget-row-height-comfortable` (e.g. 56px, two lines of 1.4 line-height plus padding) and the mirror in Listbox.tsx:8 together; add `border-block-end: 1px solid var(--nuiget-color-border)` to comfortable rows if the owner wants 1.x separators. Add an optional `meta?: string` prop to Row for the authors line (used by MI2) so `description` stays the package description. Constraints: no px font sizes, every class `nuiget-`, duration tokens only, 24 px targets (src/test/lint/webview-stylesheets.test.ts:78-101), Chromium-114 CSS only (webview-css-baseline.test.ts), no colour literals. Update interaction-model.md §1 density cell in the same change (docs:check gates).
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, `src/webview/shared/tokens.css`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: Row.test.tsx: a comfortable row with a 400-character description renders `.nuiget-row-name` with the full id (toHaveTextContent) and `.nuiget-row-description` has `overflow:hidden`/ellipsis via computed class; a compact row still hides the description (existing test at Row.test.tsx:122). Listbox.bench.ts must still pass with the new height. Add a theme.ui.test.ts screenshot assertion only if the UI job already captures the Installed tab.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/primitives src/webview/main && npx vitest run --project backend src/test/lint && npm run docs:check && npm test && node esbuild.mjs --production --analyze`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The claim stands; four precisions. (1) Clipping boundary: the leaked description text is not clipped by `.nuiget-row-desc`, `.nuiget-row-option` or `.nuiget-row` (none set overflow), crosses the `.nuiget-row-actions` slot (primitives.css:201-206; the Uninstall icon becomes visible on hover and overlaps the text), and is caught by `.nuiget-listbox { overflow:auto }` (primitives.css:55-59) via the absolutely positioned row inside the `position:relative` sizer (:62-65,:73-80) — so the visible symptom is a horizontal scrollbar on the installed/browse list plus a name cell squeezed toward zero (both `.nuiget-row-title` and `.nuiget-row-desc` have `min-width:0` and default `flex:0 1 auto`, so they shrink in proportion to content width; the version span is nowrap with min-width:auto and does not shrink, so the name goes first). (2) "44 px" is `min-height` (primitives.css:86-88) and Listbox measures real height (Listbox.tsx:96); it is 44 only because every span is nowrap on one flex line — a stacked fix changes measured height and the `ROW_HEIGHT` mirror at Listbox.tsx:8 must follow. (3) Root cause is CSS plus primitive structure: Row.tsx:97-108 emits a single flat flex sequence with no stacked/two-line variant, so the fix touches Row.tsx and primitives.css, not CSS alone. (4) Scope is wider than Installed: BrowseList.tsx:109, TransitiveSection.tsx:78 and AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx:129 pass the same `description` prop in comfortable density, so all four main-panel lists inherit the overflow. Minor 1.x precision: 66 px is the virtualizer estimate (InstalledTab.tsx:42,:470-477); rows are measured (:1181 etc.) and a row with an authors line is taller; 1.x `.package-name` wraps rather than truncates (App.css:112-116), consistent with "stacked, not squeezed".
  - The claim stands; three precision edits. (a) 1.x showed no description in main-panel rows at all (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:503-505 only merges `description` into metadata; InstalledTab.tsx:183-246 renders icon, name, badges, version, authors). The description in 2.0 rows is new and is what interaction-model.md:11 asks for, so the fix must keep it (stack or clamp it), not remove it; the fix phase should not regress it. (b) The worst case is the browse list, not the installed list: BrowseList.tsx:109 concatenates authors, downloads and description into one nowrap string. (c) The P3.2 ruling (STATUS.md:2023) fixes the DOM order (title span, then desc span holding badges, description, status) and says secondary text is "visually hidden when the density has no room"; a fix should therefore be CSS-only on `.nuiget-row-option`/`.nuiget-row-desc`/`.nuiget-row-description` (wrap to a second line under the name, or `overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis` plus `min-width:0` on the description span, with the title given `flex-shrink:0` or a `flex-basis` floor), not a DOM change. The 44px is `min-height` and the Listbox measures real row height (`measureRef`, Row.tsx:77; estimate at Listbox.tsx:8 and the ROW_HEIGHT-equals-token assertion from P5.11), so a two-line row needs only the token/estimate updated. Severity high is fair as a reading of the CSS; it is unconfirmed by a render, as the claim itself says.
  - Severity medium, and split the claim in two. MI3a (medium, CSS): long descriptions squeeze the name and overflow the row in comfortable density; visible mainly in Browse results (every row) and in Installed once metadata arrives; never in Updates. Fix in primitives.css only: `.nuiget-row-title { flex-shrink: 0; max-width: 60% }` (or similar) and `.nuiget-row-description { min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis }`; gate with a v2 UI test asserting `scrollWidth <= clientWidth` on `installed-list`/browse list with a 500-char description fixture, since jsdom cannot measure layout. Under an hour. MI3b (low, design): the one-line 44 px row versus 1.x's stacked icon/name+badges/version/authors row is the P3.2 ruling (STATUS.md:2019-2025) and is not contradicted by interaction-model.md:11; restoring a two-line comfortable row is an owner design decision (Row markup plus `--nuiget-row-height-comfortable`), not a parity defect. The high-severity symptoms the owner saw (no icons, authors, verified badge, description) belong to the data-flow claims, not to this CSS one.

### MI4 — Transitive rows are inert and their 'Required by' text is visually hidden; no transitive details, no icon/authors/verified

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: Transitive rows had a 24 px icon, version and authors with the verified glyph (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:1452-1480; App.css:3023-3075). Clicking a row opened a details view with Version, Authors (+verified) and a Required-by block, grouped per project in All Projects (InstalledTab.tsx:900-975). Metadata (iconUrl/verified/authors) was fetched per framework (`getTransitiveMetadata`, InstalledTab.tsx:644-660) and for the All Projects aggregate (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:484-503; App.tsx:1158-1180,1206-1215). All Projects rows also showed '· N projects' (InstalledTab.tsx:1575-1580).
- 2.0: Single project: `TransitiveSection.tsx` renders `Row density="compact"` with `description="Required by: …"` and no `onSelect`/`onActivate` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/TransitiveSection.tsx:64-84); compact rows clip `.nuiget-row-description` to 1 px (primitives.css:185-193), so the chain exists only for assistive technology. The transitive slice fetches lite metadata (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/transitive.tsx:65-85) but the section reads only `vulnerabilities` (TransitiveSection.tsx:79). All Projects: `AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx:114-132` is the same (compact, inert, description hidden), and the stream never asks `transitive/metadata` at all (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/allProjects.tsx:237-263). Compact rows in the main panel contradict docs/ux/interaction-model.md:11-12 (compact is the sidebar's density).
- Fix: Step A (visible rows, ≤0.5 day): switch both transitive Listboxes/Rows to `density="comfortable"`; pass `icon` (metadata iconUrl or default, as MI1), `meta` = authors (MI3 slot) and a Verified badge from `transitive.metadata.get(metadataKey(id, version))`; keep `Required by: …` as the description (now visible); in All Projects keep `describeOrigins` and add a `· N projects` suffix. In allProjects.tsx add a `transitive/metadata` ask for the unique `{id, version}` pairs of the aggregated chunks (mirror transitive.tsx:65-85, abort on new chunks) and expose `transitive.metadata`. Step B (details, ≤0.5 day): give the Listboxes `onSelect`/`onActivate` that call `details.select({ packageId, version, origin:'installed', projectPath })` (origin type at details.tsx:16 already covers it) so the details pane loads metadata/README for a transitive package; add a `requiredBy?: string[]` or `origins` field to `DetailsSelection` and render a 'Required by' block in DetailsPane (grouped per project in All Projects, 'No traceable top-level package' for empty roots, as 1.x :927-965). Update interaction-model.md §7 and §8 for the new state in the same PR.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/installed/TransitiveSection.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/allProjects.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/AllProjectsView.test.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: InstalledTab.test.tsx: expand the transitive section, answer `transitive/get` and `transitive/metadata`; assert the row's visible text contains 'Required by: Serilog' (toBeVisible), the Verified badge, and that clicking the row asks `package/metadata` for that id/version and the details pane shows a 'Required by' list. AllProjectsView.test.tsx: after two project chunks, assert exactly one `transitive/metadata` request carrying the deduplicated pairs, and the row text 'MyLib · 2 projects'.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features src/webview/shared/store && npm run docs:check && npm test`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The claim is right; only its single-label root cause ("feature-not-rendered") needs splitting into two work items with different gates:
(a) Single project: the data is already fetched and stored (transitive.tsx:79-80 keeps the whole PackageMetadataLite, including iconUrl/verified/authors). Root cause is feature-not-rendered plus the primitive's compact density (Row.tsx:97 drops `icon` under compact; primitives.css:186-193 hides `description`). Fix is rendering only: switch to comfortable density, pass icon/authors/verified, wire `onSelect` to a details path.
(b) All Projects: root cause is store-not-fetching AND feature-not-rendered — allProjects.tsx:237-263 never requests `transitive/metadata` and has no slot for transitive metadata, so the store needs a new request (mirroring 1.x's `getAllProjectsTransitiveMetadata` fan-out over unique id@version pairs, App.tsx:1206-1226) before anything can render. Also lost in All Projects: the `· N projects` count (1.x InstalledTab.tsx:1577-1580) and the per-project grouped Required-by details (1.x 931-953).
(c) Details path: the details slice's `origin` union (details.tsx:16) has no transitive value, so a transitive details view requires a slice extension, not just a click handler. The interaction-model §7 (lines 117-125) also does not describe a transitive details variant, so the doc should gain one when the fix lands.
  - Claim stands; two refinements. (1) Framing: 1.x never showed 'Required by' inline on the row (0457c75:InstalledTab.tsx:1452-1480 is icon/name/version/authors); the chain lived in the details view (:926-974). 2.0 moved it into the row's description and then hid it via compact density, so it is now visible nowhere (not 'hidden where 1.x showed it'). (2) Root cause is composite, not just feature-not-rendered: (a) density choice in both feature files (compact CSS hides the description, Row.tsx:97 blocks the icon); (b) TransitiveSection.tsx:79 reads only `vulnerabilities` from a slice that already carries iconUrl/verified/authors — not rendered; (c) allProjects.tsx never requests transitive/metadata — data not fetched for All Projects; (d) no onSelect/onActivate/details wiring — never built. The P5.4 ruling's 'compact-or-comfortable' latitude is the only text that could be read as sanctioning compact, but it is a plan ruling, not interaction-model.md or an ADR, it conflicts with §1, and its own 'icon from the slice's metadata' instruction is impossible under compact. Severity high is justified; density alone could be presented to the owner as a choice, but hidden chain, missing icon/authors/verified and inert rows are undocumented regressions.
  - Keep MI4 but split its severity; the claim lumps three gaps under one "high". MI4a (high, as claimed): "Required by" invisible in both sections and no path to any details (no onSelect/onActivate/onContextMenu). Fix cost is far below a day for the single-project half: `density="comfortable"` on the Listbox and Row in TransitiveSection.tsx:68,76 makes the description render (BrowseList.tsx:109-110 already does this), plus an `onSelect`/`onActivate` that opens a transitive details view or at least a `title` tooltip; the test gate must assert visible text (`getByText('Required by: Serilog')`), since the accessible-description assertion at InstalledTab.test.tsx:223 is what let this ship. MI4b (medium, not high): icon/authors/verified absent from transitive rows — the data is already in `transitive.metadata` (enricher.ts:59-69), so this is a rendering-only change in TransitiveSection.tsx; and the dedicated details view with Version/Authors/Required-by drops to medium for single project once the inline text is visible. MI4c (medium): All Projects never asks `transitive/metadata` (allProjects.tsx:237-263), so aggregate rows cannot show icon/authors/verified; note that a density flip alone is not enough there — `describeOrigins` (AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx:49-59) emits one "Project: required by …" clause per project joined by "; " as a single `white-space: nowrap` string (primitives.css:155-159) with no overflow rule on the description, so the fix item must verify truncation and likely needs a per-project details view or tooltip (which is why 1.x grouped origins in the details panel). Two framing corrections: do not say compact rows "contradict the P5.4 ruling" (it allowed compact-or-comfortable; it is the ruling's description+icon requirement that compact cannot deliver), and keep the "no verified glyph" point scoped to transitive rows — no verified glyph was located anywhere in 2.0's main panel, so it is not a transitive-specific omission.

### MI5 — SDK (implicit) packages carry no badge and look selectable; selection refusal is only announced

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Implicit rows showed an 'SDK' badge with a tooltip (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:196-198; App.css:156-168) and their checkbox was disabled with a title (InstalledTab.tsx:1238-1246).
- 2.0: Badges are CPM and Vulnerable only (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx:129-132); `Row` draws the decorative check mark for every row in a multi-selectable list (Row.tsx:96; Listbox.tsx:234) with no disabled state for a non-removable row; toggling an implicit row only calls `announce(whyNotRemovable(...))` (InstalledTab.tsx:114-119) — nothing on screen says why, apart from the trash button being absent on hover. Also no badge for `declaration:'props'` rows, which are equally non-removable (rules.ts:35-37).
- Fix: Add `{entry.isImplicit ? <Badge label="SDK" title="Added by the .NET SDK; cannot be uninstalled" /> : null}` and `{entry.declaration === 'props' ? <Badge label="props" title="Declared in an imported props file" /> : null}` to the row badges. Add an optional `selectable?: boolean` (default true) to `Row`: when false, render the `.nuiget-row-check` with a `nuiget-row-check--off` class (muted border via `--nuiget-color-fg-muted`, no colour literal) so the user sees the row cannot join a bulk action; the Listbox already refuses toggles for `aria-disabled` rows, so also set `aria-disabled` only for unresolved rows as today and let the owner decide if implicit rows should be `aria-disabled` (they keep Enter → details).
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx`
- Test: InstalledTab.test.tsx: a row with `isImplicit:true` has accessible description /Added by the .NET SDK/ and its check mark has the off class; Space on it leaves `uninstall-selected-button` disabled and the live region says the reason (existing announce). Row.test.tsx: `selectable={false}` renders the off class and no axe violation.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/installed src/webview/shared/primitives && npm test`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The gap is real; four precision corrections to the claim text and root cause: (1) "no disabled state for a non-removable row" is imprecise. `Row` has `disabled` -> `aria-disabled` (Row.tsx:91) and `Listbox` honours it for Space/Enter/Delete/check-click (Listbox.tsx:138, 151-164, 208-210); the feature just does not set it for implicit/props rows. But `disabled={readOnly}` is not the fix: it hides the whole actions column (Row.tsx:110), blocks `onActivate` so Enter would no longer open details (Listbox.tsx:151; InstalledProjectSection.tsx:108), and still draws the check box (Row.tsx:96 ignores `disabled`). Root cause should be split: badge = feature-not-rendered; non-selectable affordance = primitive lacks a per-row "current-able and activatable but not bulk-selectable" state (e.g. a per-row `checkable`/`selectable: false` override in `RowPlacement`/`RowProps`). (2) "nothing on screen says why, apart from the trash button being absent on hover" misses the details pane: once the row is current, DetailsPane.tsx:199-200 disables Uninstall with `title={removalBlocked}`, which is parity with 1.x PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:231-232. Accurate statement: nothing on the row itself says why. (3) Tooltip parity needs a primitive change: 2.0 `Badge` renders `title` as visually-hidden AT text, not a hover tooltip (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Badge.tsx:15-24), so adding `<Badge label="SDK" title=... />` alone would still lack the hover explanation 1.x had (same is true today for the CPM badge). The fix item must touch `Badge` (or set a real `title` attribute). (4) The `declaration: 'props'` badge note is a 2.0 internal-consistency observation, not a 1.x parity gap: 1.x `NuGetTypes.ts` has no `declaration` field (git grep declaration 0457c75 -- src/services/NuGetTypes.ts: no hits). Keep it, but label it as such.
  - Claim is partly right; narrow it as follows. (a) "nothing on screen says why" overstates: once the row is selected, the details pane's Uninstall button is disabled with `title={removalBlocked}` carrying the rules.ts sentence (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.tsx:109,198-200), matching 1.x PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:231-232. The gap is confined to the list row: no badge, a drawn check mark, no disabled look. (b) Not a regression and must be preserved: 2.0 excludes non-removable rows from Select all (InstalledTab.tsx:98-100 filters by `whyNotRemovable`), as 1.x did for implicit rows (0457c75 InstalledTab.tsx:458,483,536,594), and 2.0 extends non-removability to props/unresolved/unsupported rows with per-case reasons (rules.ts:25-39); the sidebar never marked implicit rows in 1.x either (`git grep -i implicit 0457c75 -- src/webview/sidebar` is empty), so the sidebar part (ProjectPackages.tsx:111-114) is not a 1.x parity gap. (c) The `declaration:'props'` sub-claim is not a 1.x parity gap at all — 1.x had no `declaration` concept (`git grep declaration 0457c75 -- src/webview/app/types.ts src/services/NuGetTypes.ts` is empty); it is a 2.0-internal consistency gap only (severity low). (d) Fix constraints the claim misses: do NOT reuse `disabled` for implicit/props rows — a disabled row ignores Enter (Listbox.tsx:151), which in the main panel is "select and show details" (interaction-model §5), and Row.tsx:110 drops the actions container; instead add a Badge (e.g. "SDK" / "props" with the rules.ts sentence as title) via InstalledProjectSection.tsx:129-132 and suppress the check mark for rows where `whyNotRemovable` is set (Row/Listbox need a per-row "not selectable for bulk" flag; today `checkable` is list-wide, Listbox.tsx:234). Because §4 is a closed vocabulary and interaction-model.md:4-5 requires doc and code to change together, the work item must add an "SDK-implicit / props-declared" row to §4 in the same commit. Severity medium is fair for the implicit-row part; the props part is low.
  - Severity: LOW (polish), not medium. The mechanism stands and should still be fixed, with two cautions for the fix phase: (a) do NOT fix it by passing `disabled={readOnly}` — Listbox.tsx:149-153 also gates Enter→onActivate on `aria-disabled`, so SDK rows would lose keyboard Enter-to-details, and `disabled` does not hide the check slot anyway (Row.tsx:96 draws it whenever `checkable`; primitives.css:139-141 only mutes the name). Instead add `<Badge label="SDK" title="Added by the .NET SDK; cannot be uninstalled" />` and `<Badge label="props" title=...>` next to CPM at InstalledProjectSection.tsx:129-132 (one line each) and give Row a `selectable={false}`-style prop that suppresses the check span (a primitive change, not CSS). (b) The one non-cosmetic residual is that the refusal on Space / check-click / Ctrl+click is inaudible to sighted users (LiveStatus.tsx:29 is visually hidden) — keep that in the work item so it is not dropped. Reframe the 1.x comparison: 1.x only showed the SDK badge for projects with zero `<PackageReference>` elements (fallback path, NuGetProjectService.ts:329-334 vs :339-399); 2.0 surfaces implicit rows for all projects, which is new information rather than a regression. The props part is 2.0-internal, not a parity gap. Confidence is medium, not high, because the silent no-op is a genuine UX defect and the repo cannot tell how common zero-PackageReference (bare netstandard2.0 / fresh F#) projects are among users.

### MI6 — Floating/range versions collapse to one value: the declared pattern, the Sync/Ruler badges, 'always latest' and '(run restore)' are gone

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Rows showed the pattern and the resolved version together (`10.* (10.2.0)`), an italic '(run restore)' when unresolved, `* (always latest: X)` for wildcards, and a Sync (floating) or Ruler (range) badge with tooltips (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:199-204,217-231; App.css:147-154,260-268).
- 2.0: `shownVersion()` returns only `resolvedVersion ?? version` for floating/range and gives no hint that the declaration is a pattern (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/installed/rules.ts:13-19); the row passes no badge for `versionType`/`isAlwaysLatest` (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136) although `InstalledPackage` carries `versionType`, `floatingPrefix`, `isAlwaysLatest` (domain.ts:53-55) and `SyncIcon`/`RulerIcon` exist (icons.tsx:107,115). Interaction model §4 defines Unresolved and CPM cells but says nothing about floating/range.
- Fix: Extend rules.ts with `versionCell(entry): { text: string; title?: string }`: floating/range → `${entry.version} (${entry.resolvedVersion ?? 'run restore'})` with title 'Floating version pattern' / 'Version range'; `isAlwaysLatest` → `* (always latest${resolved ? `: ${resolved}` : ''})`; else current behaviour. Use it for `version`/`versionTitle` in both surfaces (sidebar keeps the compact text, so it may prefer `shownVersion` and only the title). Add `{entry.versionType === 'floating' ? <Badge label="floating" title="Floating version pattern" /> : entry.versionType === 'range' ? <Badge label="range" title="Version range" /> : null}` to the main-panel badges (text badge; no new icon component needed). Record the cell wording in interaction-model.md §4.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/installed/rules.ts`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/ProjectPackages.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: rules unit test (SidebarView.test.tsx holds the rules tests today, or a new rules.test.ts): `versionCell` for `{version:'10.*', resolvedVersion:'10.2.0'}` → '10.* (10.2.0)'; without resolvedVersion → '10.* (run restore)'; `isAlwaysLatest` → '* (always latest: 13.0.1)'. InstalledTab.test.tsx: a floating row shows that text and the 'floating' badge in its accessible description.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview && npm run docs:check && npm test`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands but narrow and sharpen it: (a) Scope is main panel only (Installed rows + details "Installed:" cell). The 1.x sidebar also collapsed floating/range to `resolvedVersion || version` with no badge (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1530), so the 2.0 sidebar is not a regression here and should not be listed. (b) Replace "gives no hint that the declaration is a pattern" with: when a floating/range row is resolved, 2.0 shows only the resolved version and nothing marks it as a pattern; when unresolved, 2.0 shows the raw pattern (`10.*`) without the "(run restore)" hint (rules.ts:18; SidebarView.test.tsx:465). (c) Note that 2.0's details pane already shows the text "A floating or ranged version is changed in the project file." (DetailsPane.tsx:117-118,231) as the only hint, but drops 1.x's Floating/Range badge + pattern → resolved display in the details "Installed:" cell (0457c75:PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:212-221) — add that as a second regression site. (d) Add that `isAlwaysLatest`/`floatingPrefix` are populated by the 2.0 host (src/core/project/rows.ts:16-26) and read by no webview code, so the fix is purely in shared/installed/rules.ts (return a ReactNode or a `{ shown, pattern, hint }` triple) + InstalledProjectSection.tsx badges (SyncIcon/RulerIcon) + DetailsPane.tsx installed cell, with the interaction model §4 gaining a "Floating / Range" row. Severity medium is appropriate.
  - The claim is right that the main-panel Installed row lost the pattern display and badges and that nothing in the design docs decides it, but it overstates two things. (a) "the declared pattern ... [is] gone": the pattern is not gone from 2.0, only from the row. origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.tsx:194 renders `${entry.version} → ${installedVersion}` (e.g. "10.* → 10.2.0") in the details header for floating/range, and :108/:118 disable the primary button with "A floating or ranged version is changed in the project file"; ARCHITECTURE.md:639 documents that button rule. The gap is row-level only. (b) The sidebar row is 1.x parity, not a regression: the 1.x sidebar showed only `pkg.resolvedVersion || pkg.version` (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1486,1530,1600) and had no Sync/Ruler badge or "(run restore)" text (grep of 0457c75 src/webview/sidebar finds none); 2.0's shownVersion reproduces that exactly and §1 "compact rows" covers it (SidebarView.test.tsx:171,461-468 pin it). So MI6 should be scoped to the main panel's comfortable Installed rows, root cause "feature-not-rendered: the comfortable list inherited the sidebar's compact version rule via the shared rules.ts lift". Fix must be density-aware: keep shownVersion's contract for compact (SidebarView.test.tsx:461-468 pins it) and add a comfortable-only version cell + badge in InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136 (pattern + "(resolved)" / "(run restore)", "* (always latest: X)", Sync/Ruler Badge with title), with an RTL test in InstalledTab.test.tsx asserting the option's accessible name contains both "10.*" and "10.2.0" and a floating badge; gate `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/installed`. Severity medium stands (the row's only action is Uninstall; the details pane already blocks pinning a floating reference). Not verified and out of MI6's scope: the main Updates tab has no floating/versionType handling (grep of src/webview/main/features/updates finds versionType only in a test fixture), and 2.0's isUpdateCandidate (VersionRange.ts:283-284) does make a resolved floating spec an update candidate, which 1.x filtered out; whether the Updates row then offers to pin a floating package belongs to an Updates-area claim.
  - Keep MI6 at medium, but correct its wording and scope: (1) the declared pattern and resolved version are NOT gone, they survive in the details pane (`10.* → 10.2.0`, DetailsPane.tsx:194) and the pattern still shows in unresolved rows (rules.ts:19); what is truly gone is the row-level Sync/Ruler badge, the italic "(run restore)" hint and the "* (always latest: X)" phrasing; (2) the gap is main-panel-only: the 1.x sidebar never showed the pattern or badges (PackageRow.tsx:87-89), so the 2.0 sidebar is parity, not regression; (3) the fix is small TSX, not CSS: have `shownVersion()` (or the two render sites) return `<>{version} <span>({resolvedVersion})</span></>` for floating/range, pass `versionTitle`, and add a text `Badge` "floating"/"range" (or a tiny icon prop on Badge); add a floating-row test in InstalledProjectSection/SidebarView tests and a §4 "Floating / range" row to docs/ux/interaction-model.md so the next rewrite does not drop it; (4) fold in one details-pane fix: for a floating spec without `resolvedVersion`, DetailsPane.tsx:106+194 renders `10.* → 10.*` — show "(run restore)" or omit the arrow; (5) file the pin-on-update behaviour (2.0 lists floating/range packages as updatable and `ops/update` would pin them; 1.x excluded them) as a separate Updates-area finding rather than using it to escalate MI6.

### MI7 — Vulnerability signal is a generic 'Vulnerable' badge: no severity colour or count on the row, no Installed-tab vulnerability badge

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: The row badge carried the highest severity as a class (`vuln-Low` … `vuln-Critical`, coloured) with a WarningIcon and a count tooltip (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:205-215; App.css:170-192). The Installed tab button showed a severity-coloured count of vulnerable packages next to the installed count (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2163-2178,2953-2960).
- 2.0: `isVulnerable` reduces the metadata to a boolean and renders `Badge label="Vulnerable" tone="error"` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx:41-42,131); Badge tones only colour the border (primitives.css:276-290). `MainShell` puts a count only on the Updates tab (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:130-135). `PackageVulnerability.severity` is available in the lite metadata (domain.ts:136-141,196).
- Fix: Compute the highest severity from `lite.vulnerabilities` and render `Badge label={`${severity} vulnerability` or `N vulnerabilities`} tone={severity === 'Low' ? 'info' : severity === 'Moderate' ? 'warning' : 'error'} title=...`. In MainShell derive `vulnerableCount` from `installedSlice.metadata` (single project) or `all.metadata` (All Projects) and show it as a second `Badge` on the Installed tab (extend `TabItem` with an optional `extra?: { count: number; tone: BadgeTone; title: string }`, rendered after the count badge; keep it part of the tab's name). Tokens only; no colour literals.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Tabs.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx`
- Test: InstalledTab.test.tsx: metadata with `[{severity:'Critical', advisoryUrl}]` yields a badge whose accessible description matches /Critical/; MainShell.test.tsx: with two vulnerable rows the Installed tab's accessible name contains '2'.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main && npm test`

### MI8 — The installed count is shown nowhere in the main panel (no 'Direct packages (N)' header, no Installed-tab count)

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Single project had a collapsible 'Direct packages (N)' / '(N of M)' header (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:1267-1280; App.css:2707-2741) and the Installed tab button carried the count (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2953). All Projects headers showed `(N)` when collapsed (InstalledTab.tsx:1203-1215).
- 2.0: The single-project list deliberately has no header (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx:150-158, citing §4 — which only governs where the provisional badge goes, docs/ux/interaction-model.md:60) and `MainShell` gives a count to the Updates tab only (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:135). All Projects headers do show `count` (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:170). The sidebar shows the count on its section header (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/InstalledSection.tsx:132). Under a filter, 1.x showed 'N of M'; 2.0 shows nothing.
- Fix: In MainShell set `count` on the Installed tab: single project → `installedSlice.result?.packages.length` when `status === 'ready'`; All Projects → sum over `all.chunks` once `all.status === 'ready'` (same guard shape as `allCount` at MainShell.tsx:133). Under an `@installed`/`@vulnerable` filter, render a one-line `nuiget-installed-status` note 'N of M packages' above the single-project list (reuse the existing `.nuiget-installed-status` container, InstalledProjectSection.tsx:155).
- Files: `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`
- Test: MainShell.test.tsx: after `installed/get` answers three packages the Installed tab has accessible name 'Installed 3'; InstalledTab.test.tsx: typing `@installed ser` shows '1 of 3 packages'.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main && npm test`

### MI9 — Single-project transitive section is closed and silent until clicked; per-framework headers with counts and TFM badges are gone

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Transitive data was prefetched 500 ms after the installed list arrived (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:744-760) and rendered one collapsible header per target framework: 'Transitive packages (N)' plus a TFM badge, metadata prefetched per framework (InstalledTab.tsx:1413-1427,756-790; App.css:2905-2949). A missing assets file showed an explanatory block with a 'Restore Project' button (InstalledTab.tsx:1374-1388).
- 2.0: One section 'Transitive packages' with no count; nothing is asked until the user expands it (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/TransitiveSection.tsx:19-23,34-40; transitive.tsx:36-50); several frameworks become plain `h3` headings without counts (TransitiveSection.tsx:60-66) although `SectionHeader` supports `count` (SectionHeader.tsx:20). The section is capped at half the pane (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/main.css:155-160). The interaction model mandates lazy loading only for All Projects (docs/ux/interaction-model.md:51), so the single-project laziness is a choice, not a requirement; the restore banner is equivalent (TransitiveSection.tsx:49-54).
- Fix: In TransitiveProvider, enable the resource automatically ~500 ms after the installed slice is `ready` for the same project (a `setTimeout` in an effect on `useInstalled().status`/`projectPath`, cleared on change), keeping `enable()` for an earlier manual expand; the section stays collapsed but its header gets `count` = total packages across frameworks (or per-framework `SectionHeader`s with `count` when several, replacing the `h3`s). Keep the 50 % cap. Document the prefetch in interaction-model.md §7/§4.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/store/transitive.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/TransitiveSection.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/transitive.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: transitive.test.tsx: with fake timers, after `installed/get` answers, `transitive/get` is asked after 500 ms without `enable()`; switching project cancels the pending ask. InstalledTab.test.tsx: the collapsed header reads 'Transitive packages 12'.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/store src/webview/main/features/installed && npm run docs:check && npm test`

### MI10 — Bulk toolbar lost Collapse all / Expand all and the icon-button style; Uninstall is no longer a danger button

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Toolbar with icon buttons: Select all (CheckAllIcon, `active` state), separator, Collapse all / Expand all (project sections in All Projects; direct + transitive sections in single project), and a red `btn-danger` 'Uninstall Selected (N)' (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:1055-1137; App.css:320-327,465-500).
- 2.0: Two text buttons, 'Select all' (`aria-pressed`) and 'Uninstall selected (N)', no collapse/expand (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.tsx:200-207). `CheckAllIcon`, `CollapseAllIcon`, `ExpandAllIcon` still exist (icons.tsx:213,221,231). Each project section keeps its own `expanded` state (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:69), so nothing can collapse them all.
- Fix: Lift `expanded` to InstalledTab as `collapsed: ReadonlySet<string>` passed to each section (controlled, default expanded); add two `IconButton`s (CollapseAllIcon/ExpandAllIcon, labels 'Collapse all'/'Expand all') to the Toolbar, shown only in All Projects (single project has one list and the transitive section; include the transitive section in the toggle if the owner wants 1.x parity). Keep the text 'Uninstall selected (N)' button but give it a `nuiget-button--danger` class backed by a new token `--nuiget-color-button-danger-bg: var(--vscode-button-background)` only if a VS Code danger colour exists (none standard; otherwise keep the primary style and skip). Add the new test ids to interaction-model.md §8.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: InstalledTab.test.tsx (All Projects): 'Collapse all' sets every `section-header-project-*` to `aria-expanded=false`; 'Expand all' restores them; the Toolbar keeps one tab stop.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/installed && npm run docs:check && npm test`

### MI11 — Hover prefetch of versions/metadata on rows is gone

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Hovering an installed row for 150 ms prefetched its versions and metadata so the details panel opened instantly (`useHoverPrefetch`, 0457c75:src/webview/app/hooks/useHoverPrefetch.ts:1-40; wired at InstalledTab.tsx:1236-1237,1331-1332).
- 2.0: No `onMouseEnter`, `prefetch` or `prewarm` use anywhere in src/webview (grep over origin/labs/2.0 src/webview: no hit); the details slice asks `package/metadata`/`package/versions` only on `select` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx). The protocol has `package/prewarm` (messages.ts:143) but it warms a source, not a package.
- Fix: Add an optional `onHover?: (item: T) => void` to Listbox, fired from a single debounced (150 ms) `onMouseOver` on the list root (resolve the row via `data-index`, as `indexOf` does); expose `prefetch({ packageId, version, sourceUrl })` from the details actions that issues `package/versions` and `package/metadata` through the bus with the same keys the selection would use (the coordinator caches by resource key, so the later `select` is a cache hit). Wire it in InstalledProjectSection (and BrowseList/UpdatesTab if wanted).
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/details.test.tsx`
- Test: details.test.tsx: `prefetch` sends exactly one `package/metadata` and one `package/versions` and a following `select` of the same id/version sends none; Listbox.test.tsx: hovering a row for 150 ms (fake timers) calls `onHover` once.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared && npm test`

### MI12 — All Projects project headers are uppercase sidebar-style; count badge always shown; no hover background

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `css` · Estimate: 0.25 d
- 1.18.9: Project headers were normal-case 13 px/500 buttons with a hover background and the count only when collapsed (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:1203-1215; App.css:2707-2741,2744-2748); workspace-folder headers were the uppercase ones (App.css:538-558).
- 2.0: `SectionHeader` is shared with the sidebar and sets `text-transform: uppercase`, `font-size: sm`, weight 600 and no hover rule (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:313-346); project names (case-sensitive identifiers) therefore render as e.g. 'MYAPP.WEB' in the main panel, and the count badge is shown whether expanded or not (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:170; SectionHeader.tsx:48). The folder heading is uppercase too (main.css:483-489), so two levels look alike.
- Fix: Add an optional `appearance?: 'section' | 'group'` to SectionHeader (default 'section' keeps the sidebar look); 'group' adds `nuiget-section-header--group` with `text-transform: none`, `font-size: var(--nuiget-font-size-md)`, `font-weight: 600`, and `:hover { background: var(--nuiget-color-list-hover-bg) }` on the toggle. Use 'group' for the main-panel project sections (InstalledProjectSection, AllProjectsUpdates). Tokens only; keep the 24 px target.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/SectionHeader.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, `src/webview/main/main.css`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/SectionHeader.test.tsx`
- Test: SectionHeader.test.tsx: `appearance="group"` adds the class and keeps the heading/button semantics and axe clean.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/primitives && npx vitest run --project backend src/test/lint && npm test`

**Better in 2.0 (do not regress):**
- Listbox keyboard/ARIA model: one roving tab stop, `aria-multiselectable`, `aria-current` (details row) distinct from `aria-selected` (bulk set), Space/Ctrl+click toggle, Enter, Delete, Shift+F10/ContextMenu, Home/End, focus follows a vanished row (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx:57-247; interaction-model §5) — 1.x had a hand-rolled key handler and native checkboxes with no listbox semantics.
- Per-row pointer Uninstall action (trash IconButton, hidden from AT, `row-action-uninstall`) in addition to the Delete key (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:133-135); 1.x had only the key.
- In-panel BulkUninstallDialog that lists exactly the packages per project with Confirm/Cancel (BulkUninstallDialog.tsx; interaction-model §5) instead of 1.x's host-side modal round trip (`confirmBulkRemove` → `bulkRemoveConfirmed`).
- Select all is scoped to removable, non-busy rows under the current filter, and a selected row that leaves the list is pruned from the bulk set (InstalledTab.tsx:97-112,102-108).
- Rows stay in place and show a busy spinner plus status text ('Uninstalling') from the request until the host's next list; no optimistic removal, partial outcomes reconcile from the authoritative list (InstalledTab.tsx:40-42; InstalledProjectSection.tsx:74-82,126-128; harness scenario 'bulk remove partial failure reconciles').
- `whyNotRemovable` gives a sentence for implicit, props-declared, unresolved and unsupported rows, announced through the live region (rules.ts:25-39).
- Provisional/authoritative fidelity with the 'provisional' badge, Unsupported banner (packages.config, non-SDK, sdk-too-old read-only list), CPM badge and `unresolved` version cell with tooltip (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:91-96,114-128,144-149; interaction-model §4) — none existed in 1.x.
- `@vulnerable` distinguishes 'no vulnerable packages' from 'vulnerability data is not available yet' (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:48-53); 1.x showed an empty list in both cases.
- Per-project error Banner with Retry in All Projects and for a failed single-project read (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:85-88,96); 1.x only appended '— failed to load' to the header.
- No blanking: revalidation keeps rows with `aria-busy`, the provisional chunk is shown while the evaluation runs, stale answers are dropped by generation (installed.tsx:45-60; interaction-model §4).
- Icons are fetched host-side with auth/policy and delivered as `data:` URIs under a hardened CSP (ADR-006; services.ts:109-116), so private-feed icons can work once MI1 renders them; 1.x blocked them by CSP.
- Design tokens with light/dark/high-contrast/forced-colours/reduced-motion in one file and lint gates for colour literals, px font sizes, 24 px targets and Chromium-114 CSS (tokens.css; src/test/lint/webview-*.test.ts).
- Workspace-folder grouping with heading levels (FolderGroups) and the aggregated All Projects transitive section with the 'not restored' banner naming the projects and one Restore action (AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx:104-108).

**Notes:**
- Field matrix for the Installed tab (host → slice → row): host `installed/get` returns `InstalledPackage` rows built by `rows.ts` with id, version, resolvedVersion, versionType, floatingPrefix, isAlwaysLatest, declaration, definedIn, targetFrameworks, isImplicit only (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/project/rows.ts:16-41; Catalog.ts:186-225). The `iconUrl`/`verified`/`authors`/`vulnerabilities` fields declared on `InstalledPackage` (domain.ts:63-66) are never set by the host in 2.0 — dead fields. The only carrier of icon/verified/authors/description/vulnerabilities is `PackageMetadataLite` (domain.ts:189-197): single project via the webview's own `transitive/metadata` ask (installed.tsx:66-87 → Catalog.transitiveMetadata, Catalog.ts:285-289), All Projects via the `{kind:'metadata'}` chunk (Catalog.ts:341-359 → allProjects.tsx:119-123). Both slices keep all five fields by lower-cased id. The rows render only `description` and a boolean from `vulnerabilities` (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124,131). Fixes must read from the slice's `metadata` map, never from `entry.iconUrl` etc. Downloads are not in the lite metadata and 1.x installed rows did not show them either — no gap.
- The computed task's claim that `description` never reaches Installed rows is not what the code says: it is passed at InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124. The owner's 'no description' observation is not explained by the wiring; candidate causes (unverified): the enrichment returned nothing on that machine (feeds unreachable or `transitive/metadata` erroring, which the slice treats as no metadata, installed.tsx:85), or the single-line comfortable row of MI3 made it unreadable.
- Single-project lite metadata is resolved from `sourceDirectory(services)` — the selected project's directory, else the first workspace folder (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/handlers.ts:29-36,289) — because `transitive/metadata` carries no `projectPath`; All Projects uses each project's own feed context (`forProject`, Catalog.ts:350). Under ALL_PROJECTS the main panel's transitive slice is inactive, so this only matters if the session's selected project differs from the list being enriched; not a user-visible gap today, but a protocol wrinkle a fixer should know before adding more asks.
- Nothing was rendered or screenshotted in this audit; every CSS claim (MI3, MI12, the compact clipping in MI4) is from reading the stylesheets, not from a browser. Row heights: 1.x 66 px (InstalledTab.tsx:42), 2.0 44 px (tokens.css:26, mirrored in Listbox.tsx:8 — change both together).
- Fix constraints that gate every item: `src/test/lint/webview-icons-pure.test.ts` asserts exactly 26 `React.memo` icons (reuse VerifiedIcon/SyncIcon/RulerIcon/CollapseAllIcon/ExpandAllIcon; adding one means updating that count); `webview-stylesheets.test.ts` requires `.nuiget-` class prefix, no px font sizes, duration tokens, 24 px pointer targets; `webview-colours.test.ts` forbids colour literals outside tokens.css; `webview-css-baseline.test.ts` forbids CSS newer than Chromium 114; `dist/webview.js` ceiling is 577 766 bytes (esbuild.mjs:24); any behaviour or test-id change must update docs/ux/interaction-model.md in the same PR (`npm run docs:check` and `interaction-model-testids.test.ts`).
- Skeleton of 3 plain rows (Skeleton.tsx:14) is what interaction-model §4 prescribes (docs/ux/interaction-model.md:58); plainer than 1.x's 8 icon-shaped rows (InstalledTab.tsx:49-63) but not a contradiction — not listed as a gap.
- Out of this area and not examined: the Updates tab (which also renders no `icon`, UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 — MI1's default-icon helper should be reused there), the details pane header, the main header toggles, the sidebar, and the CI failure the owner mentioned.

## Main panel Updates tab and All Projects updates (rows, badges, grouping, toolbar, dialogs, bulk progress, tab badge)

### MU1 — Single-project Updates rows show no package icon and no author line although the host sends both

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Each update row was a card: checkbox, 32px icon with default-icon fallback (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:689-695, fallback at 612-613), name, 'v{installed} -> v{latest}' line, and an authors line with the VerifiedIcon (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:696-710). Styles: 12px/16px padding, 32px icon, muted authors (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:70-104, 559-575).
- 2.0: ProjectUpdates passes only name, version, badges (CPM + Verified) and actions to Row (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx:182-197); no icon= and no description=. The data is there: the host's final answer attaches iconUrl, verified and authors to every update (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:254-268) and the slice exposes it as rows (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/updates.tsx:15-19, 87). Row accepts icon and description in comfortable density (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx:35-37, 97, 105) and BrowseList already renders both (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:109-111). The Verified badge is rendered (UpdatesTab.tsx:192). Streamed 'found' chunks are bare by design (Catalog.ts:228-230), so icon/authors can only appear once the answer lands. Contradicts interaction-model §1 'Comfortable rows (icons, description, badges)' (origin/labs/2.0:docs/ux/interaction-model.md:11).
- Fix: In ProjectUpdates renderRow add icon={update.iconUrl?.startsWith('data:') ? <img className="nuiget-package-icon" src={update.iconUrl} alt="" /> : <PackageIconPlaceholder />} and description={update.authors ? `by ${update.authors}` : undefined}. Generalise .nuiget-browse-icon (main.css:200-204) to .nuiget-package-icon (keep the old class as alias) and add a same-size placeholder (inline SVG from shared/primitives/icons.tsx) so rows without an icon stay aligned, as the 1.x default icon did (2.0 resolver returns undefined when none: origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/icons.ts:192,211). Extract a renderUpdateRow helper to share with MU2.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx`, `src/webview/main/main.css`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.test.tsx`
- Test: Extend 'lists the updates with their versions, counts them on the tab and passes axe' (UpdatesTab.test.tsx:59): give one fixture update iconUrl 'data:image/png;base64,...' and authors; assert an img exists inside package-row-serilog, the row's description reads 'by <authors>', a row without iconUrl renders the placeholder, and axe still passes.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim is correct; three precision notes for the fix item:
1. "attaches iconUrl, verified and authors to every update" is slightly overstated: Catalog.ts:259-267 copies each field only when the enricher returned it, and enricher.ts:5-6/74-80 leaves an entry bare on failure or cancellation. Say "to every update the enricher could resolve".
2. 1.x nested the VerifiedIcon inside the `{pkg.authors && ...}` block (0457c75:UpdatesTab.tsx:703-707), so Verified appeared only when authors were present. 2.0 renders the Verified badge independently (UpdatesTab.tsx:192) — a 2.0 improvement; the fix should keep the badge independent and add `description={update.authors ? \`by ${update.authors}\` : undefined}` alongside it, not re-nest.
3. 2.0 icons are `data:` URIs produced host-side (icons.ts:211-216) under a CSP of `img-src ${cspSource} data:` (host/services.ts:113). The Updates fix must mirror BrowseList.tsx:111 (`iconUrl?.startsWith('data:')` guard + `.nuiget-browse-icon` class), not 1.x's URL `<img onError>` fallback; a default-icon fallback for rows without an icon would be a separate decision since BrowseList currently renders none either. Also note that 1.x streamed rows already carried icon/authors (NuGetPackageService.ts:2096 fires `onUpdateFound` after enrichment), whereas 2.0 streamed `found` chunks are bare by design — so even after the fix, icons/authors appear only when the final answer lands.
  - The claim stands; refinements only. (1) ARCHITECTURE.md:582-583 ("installed → latest as each row's version, the CPM and Verified badges") describes the shipped shape and is not a design decision: interaction-model.md:5 makes the interaction model the authority, and no ADR, ruling or plan row decides against or defers icons/authors on main-panel rows (2.0.1 deferrals are P7.4, N64, N19, N81 residue only). This is a bug/parity gap, not an owner decision. (2) The gap is wider than stated: ADR-006:18 promises a bundled default icon when resolution fails; the host supplies it as data-package-icon (services.ts:106-108) but nothing in src/webview reads it, so neither the resolved icon nor the documented fallback appears on Updates rows (BrowseList shows resolved data: icons only, no fallback either). The fix should wire the fallback too. (3) Sibling gap outside MU1's single-project scope: AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-122 renders only the CPM badge (no Verified, no icon, no authors) although the allProjects slice stores the icons chunk (allProjects.tsx:38-39, :205). (4) Severity "high" matches the task's definition (owner called it broken); root cause "feature-not-rendered" is correct — data reaches the slice (updates.tsx:87) and the primitive supports it.
  - Severity high stands. Refinements to the claim's proposed fix, not its severity:

(a) No default-icon fallback exists anywhere in 2.0 (grep for defaultPackageIcon/placeholder in src/webview is empty); BrowseList.tsx:111 renders no icon unless iconUrl is a `data:` URI. Wiring `icon=` from `update.iconUrl` alone would indent some rows by 24px and not others. The fix needs a shared default package icon in shared/primitives (1.x had one: 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:612-613) so rows align; apply it to BrowseList and Installed too.

(b) Icons and authors arrive late by design: `updates/get` chunks are bare (origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/messages.ts:124; Catalog.ts:228-230), so rows pop from text to icon when the answer lands, whereas 1.x streamed enriched rows (NuGetPackageService.ts:2082-2098). Acceptable for the one-day item; a follow-up could stream an `icons` chunk as `allProjects/updates` already does (messages.ts:130).

(c) The author line alone would be medium (11px at 0.7 opacity in 1.x, App.css:563-567, and still shown in the Details pane); it is the icon loss that drops the row to text. Icon size will be 24px (`--nuiget-icon-size-lg`, origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/tokens.css:31) versus 32px in 1.x; cosmetic.

(d) Add assertions in UpdatesTab.test.tsx that a row with iconUrl/authors in the host answer renders `.nuiget-row-icon` and "by <authors>" in `.nuiget-row-description`; gate with `npm run test:frontend`.

### MU2 — All Projects Updates rows render only the CPM badge: no icon, no Verified badge, no authors; the icons chunk is never consumed

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: All-projects rows showed checkbox, icon and name + version arrow (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:601-623). Icons arrived in a background phase: NuGetPanel sent allProjectsIcons (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:1086-1097) and App.tsx merged them into the rows (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:1241-1252). Authors/verified were absent because PackageUpdateMinimal lacked them (0457c75:src/webview/app/types.ts:128-134).
- 2.0: ProjectUpdatesSection passes badges={central ? <Badge label="CPM"/> : undefined} and nothing else beyond name/version/actions (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-121). Yet the project chunk's rows are the enriched PackageUpdate: allProjectsUpdates emits result.updates from this.updates (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:376-381), which attaches iconUrl/verified/authors (Catalog.ts:256-268); chunk type origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/messages.ts:67-73, row type origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/domain.ts:96-104. The slice additionally builds an icons map from the 'icons' chunk (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/allProjects.tsx:38-39, 205) that no file under src/webview/main/features/updates/ reads (git grep for icons|iconUrl|authors there returns nothing). Net: 2.0 lost the icons 1.x had and never surfaced the Verified/authors it now has data for.
- Fix: Use the shared renderUpdateRow helper from MU1 in ProjectUpdatesSection, reading update.iconUrl / update.authors / update.verified from the chunk row (not from the icons map). Add the Verified badge next to CPM. Leave the redundant 'icons' chunk and allProjects.updates.icons in place for now; removing them is a separate protocol change (Catalog.ts:382-390, messages.ts:130, allProjects.tsx:205) gated by npm run check-types.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/updates/AllProjectsUpdates.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.test.tsx`
- Test: In 'All Projects: one section per project, selections across them become ops/bulkUpdateAllProjects' (UpdatesTab.test.tsx:231) give a chunk row iconUrl, authors and verified: true; assert inside updates-list-<project> an img, the text 'by <authors>' and a 'Verified' badge; the axe test at :279 still passes.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim MU2 is substantively correct; keep it at high severity. Refinements for the fix item: (1) The claim's grep omitted `verified`; widening it finds origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx:191, where the SINGLE-project Updates row already renders `<Badge label="Verified" tone="info" .../>` from `update.verified`. So "never surfaced Verified" is true only for the All Projects rows; the fix is to mirror UpdatesTab.tsx:189-192 into AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:118 (and add the same to neither path for `authors`, which no 2.0 Updates row shows). Note the single-project row also passes no `icon`, so the icon gap exists in both Updates paths, not only All Projects. (2) 1.x always rendered an icon slot (default icon when iconUrl missing, UpdatesTab.tsx:610-614); 2.0's Row renders no slot when `icon` is undefined (Row.tsx:97) and 2.0 ships no placeholder icon asset, so the fix must decide on a placeholder (or accept uneven rows) — BrowseList.tsx:111 passes `undefined` for non-`data:` URLs. (3) iconUrl arrives twice in 2.0 (on each `PackageUpdate` in the `project` chunk, Catalog.ts:381, and again in the `icons` chunk, Catalog.ts:382-389); the fix should read `update.iconUrl` directly (same as the Verified pattern) and may leave the slice's `icons` map unused or remove it, rather than reconcile both. (4) Cite the enrichment block as Catalog.ts:254-269 (the `searchEnricher.enrich` call is line 254), not 256-268.
  - The claim stands as a regression/omission, not a design decision.
