Precision corrections: (a) "the icons chunk is never consumed" should read "stored by the slice (allProjects.tsx:205) and exposed as `updates.icons` (allProjects.tsx:304) but never rendered"; (b) line cites: Catalog.ts enrichment is :259-267 (claim: 256-268) and the `project` chunk emit is :378 with the `icons` chunk at :379-387 (claim: 376-381); (c) the icons chunk is redundant in 2.0 because the `project` chunk rows already carry `iconUrl` — the fix should read `update.iconUrl` first; (d) scope: the single-project UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 also renders no icon and no authors (it does render Verified at :191), while 1.x single-project rows showed icon + authors + Verified (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:609-614, 703-708), so the icon/authors loss is Updates-tab-wide; one fix should cover both consumers. (e) The only documented "no icons" decision is the sidebar's compact density (interaction-model.md §1), which does not apply to this main-panel view.
  - Downgrade MU2 to medium as scoped (All Projects Updates rows lost the 32px package icon 1.x showed; rows otherwise functionally complete). Split the claim: (a) icon loss belongs to one cross-cutting item covering every Installed/Updates row in the main panel (single-project UpdatesTab, InstalledProjectSection, AllProjectsUpdates, transitive sections) plus the unused data-package-icon default (ADR-006), which is the thing the owner perceived as "broken" and may carry the high; (b) missing Verified badge/authors in All Projects Updates is low: not a 1.x regression, only an inconsistency with UpdatesTab.tsx:191; (c) the unread icons chunk is redundant with per-row iconUrl, not a data-loss bug. Fix item: AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-121 add icon (data: URI, default-icon fallback read from document.getElementById('root').dataset.packageIcon) and the Verified badge; fixed-width .nuiget-row-icon in primitives.css; new AllProjectsUpdates.test.tsx asserting .nuiget-row-icon img[src^="data:"] and the Verified badge; gate: npm run test:frontend && npm run check-types && npm run lint.

### MU3 — During Update All only the package of the current step stays busy; the other confirmed rows drop their spinner and re-enter the Update all count (statically traced, not executed)

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: While updatingAll the toolbar read 'Updating...' and was disabled, and every checkbox was disabled (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:528-535, 604-607, 681-688); state reset on bulkUpdateResult / bulkUpdateAllProjectsResult (376-387).
- 2.0: The ops slice marks every requested package busy from the call (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/ops.tsx:185 with touchedBy at 103-128), and the host's first report names the full set (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/ops/PackageWorkflows.ts:186). Each step then reports packageIds: [request.packageId], projectPaths: [request.projectPath] (PackageWorkflows.ts:195), and the reducer merges by spread { ...previous, ...progress } (ops.tsx:63-76), so packageIds narrows to the one current package; busyPackages reads only that (ops.tsx:131-141). Rows outside the current step lose aria-busy and the spinner (Row busy at UpdatesTab.tsx:179, 188) and re-enter candidates (UpdatesTab.tsx:82), so 'Update all (N)' counts them again and they can be re-submitted mid-bulk. Contradicts interaction-model §4 'Pending op: Row gets a spinner ... until the terminal outcome' (origin/labs/2.0:docs/ux/interaction-model.md:63). Existing tests cover a single op only (UpdatesTab.test.tsx:83-93; ops.test.tsx:52-72). Static trace: not executed in this audit.
- Fix: In the 'progress' branch of opsReducer merge projectPaths and packageIds as the union of the previous entry and the report (dedupe case-insensitively for ids) while taking status/current/total/detail from the latest report. Keep PackageWorkflows as is (the webview fix also covers operations started on the other surface). Optionally disable update-all-button while any active op touches the project.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/store/ops.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/ops.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.test.tsx`
- Test: ops.test.tsx: dispatch 'asked' for a 3-package bulk, then a 'running' progress with packageIds of one package; assert busyPackages still has all three until 'completed'. UpdatesTab.test.tsx: after confirming Update all and emitting a per-step progress chunk from the fake host, every confirmed row is aria-busy and update-all-button is disabled.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; precise corrections: (1) Row busy is computed at UpdatesTab.tsx:178 and passed at :187; :188 is the `status` prop (claim said 179, 188). (2) "Existing tests cover a single op only" is too strong: InstalledTab.test.tsx:146-165 does test bulk busy state (all rows aria-busy from the request on) but sends no per-step progress chunk, so no test anywhere exercises a narrower per-step report; and PackageWorkflows.test.ts:745-752 actively pins the per-step `packageIds: [one]` host output, so a host-side fix must update it. (3) The narrowing is not "only the current step" for the whole bulk: during the restore phase PackageWorkflows.ts:351 re-widens `packageIds` to all successfully edited ids of that project, so the busy set goes full → one → one … → edited-per-project; still contradicts §4:63 but a fix test should assert that sequence. (4) Both the originating surface (chunk path, handlers.ts:172-176) and the non-originating one (`ops/progress`, MainPanelHost.ts:85 / SidebarHost.ts:39) narrow identically. (5) Root cause is better stated as a protocol-field ambiguity (op scope vs step subject in `OperationProgress.packageIds`, domain.ts:313-315) combined with replace-not-union merging at ops.tsx:72, rather than generic "behaviour".
  - Claim is right in substance; four sharpenings:
(1) Root cause is "not stored in slice", not "behaviour": the host does send the full set first (PackageWorkflows.ts:186); the ops reducer at ops.tsx:72 then replaces `packageIds`/`projectPaths` with each step's narrower arrays despite its comment saying to keep them. Fix belongs in the slice (union the arrays, or ignore scope on reports that carry `current`/`total`), not in the host, whose per-step shape is pinned by PackageWorkflows.test.ts:746-751.
(2) The restore phase re-widens: PackageWorkflows.ts:351 reports `packageIds: editedIds` for the project, so a user sees all → one → all-edited → gone; this rules out "intentional focus on the current step".
(3) Scope is wider than Update All on the single-project Updates tab: AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:107,182 and the Installed bulk-remove candidates at InstalledTab.tsx:99 (and the sidebar's ProjectUpdates.tsx:49,67,105) read the same isPackageBusy and go through the same editSequence.
(4) Line slips: Row busy is UpdatesTab.tsx:178 (and :187), not 179; candidates is :81, not 82. interaction-model.md:63 is correct.
  - Claim stands at medium; it is incomplete in four ways. (a) Restore phase re-widens: restoreChanged reports `packageIds: editedIds.get(project)` — every successfully edited id of that project (PackageWorkflows.ts:351) — so the observed pattern is full-set → one → one → … → all-edited rows busy during 'restoring' → cleared. With the restore toggle off (`skipRestore` continue at :343-346) there is no re-widen and dropped rows never return until the outcome. (b) Breadth: the same `candidatesOf` exclusion and enabled button exist in All Projects (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:182, :296), and since `bulkUpdateAllProjects` steps narrow projectPaths to one project (PackageWorkflows.ts:195), rows of the other projects drop too. The sidebar reads the same slice (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/ProjectUpdates.tsx:49, :67, :105; ProjectPackages.tsx:44), so during a main-panel Update all its non-current rows lose busy and Enter can start a single `ops/update` on a package already inside the running bulk. Two surfaces, three bulk paths. (c) Fix: one-line union of `packageIds`/`projectPaths` (lower-cased ids) in the `progress` branch at ops.tsx:72 fixes all paths at once; alternatively have editSequence keep the full set and vary only `current`/`detail` at PackageWorkflows.ts:195. Defence in depth: wire the unused `isBusy` (ops.tsx:208) into the Update all `disabled` to restore the 1.x toolbar lock. Add an ops.test.tsx case: running report with [A,B,C], then running with [B], expect A and C still busy; add a UpdatesTab.test.tsx case asserting 'Update all' stays disabled/unchanged across step chunks. Gate: `npm run test:frontend`. (d) Root cause label 'behaviour' should read: reducer merge semantics (spread treats a narrowed array as authoritative) combined with a host report shape that narrows rather than omits; `OperationProgress.packageIds` (origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/domain.ts:313-315) is documented as 'the operation touches', which the per-step report violates.

### MU4 — No VS Code progress notification during Update All and no summary/failure toast afterwards (cross-area: host/ops)

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `other` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: executeBulkUpdatePackages ran inside vscode.window.withProgress (Notification, 'Updating N packages...', per-package '(i/N) id', 'Restoring project...') and ended with showInformationMessage / showWarningMessage (0457c75:src/services/NuGetOperations.ts:192-225); all-projects equivalent at 357-390 and 417-419.
- 2.0: announceOperation only fires an event (origin/labs/2.0:src/extension.ts:141; contract at origin/labs/2.0:src/host/services.ts:37-38); settleOperation announces and invalidates (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/handlers.ts:154-158). git grep for withProgress|showInformationMessage|showWarningMessage|showErrorMessage over src/host, src/extension.ts and src/core/ops finds no operation toast (only cache-cleared toasts at extension.ts:362,369 and sidebar-specific warnings in SidebarHost.ts). Visible feedback is the status bar item 'nUIget: N running' (origin/labs/2.0:src/extension.ts:269-278) and the live-region sentence 'N of N operations completed' (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/outcomes.ts:27-35, announced by MainShell.tsx:101-108). current/total/detail already travel on ops/progress (PackageWorkflows.ts:195; origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/domain.ts:309-320) but nothing in src/webview/main reads .current or .total (grep empty). Contradicts interaction-model §5 'Install / Update ... None (progress toast)' (interaction-model.md:75) and §4 'Partial / failed op: Toast (VS Code notification)' (interaction-model.md:64).
- Fix: Host: in extension.ts subscribe to queue.onProgress and, per opId, open vscode.window.withProgress({ location: Notification, title: `${IN_PROGRESS[kind]} ${total} packages` }) fed by detail/current/total and resolved by events.operationCompleted for that opId; on outcome status partial/failed call showWarningMessage with the describeOutcome text, on a completed bulk showInformationMessage. Webview: read the active bulk op for the current project from useOps().active and render 'Updating 2 of 3...' in the Updates toolbar (replace the button label) while it runs.
- Files: `src/extension.ts`, `src/host/handlers.test.ts`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/AllProjectsUpdates.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.test.tsx`
- Test: handlers.test.ts: a bulkUpdate ticket that ends partial triggers showWarningMessage once and completed triggers showInformationMessage once; progress reports drive withProgress.report. UpdatesTab.test.tsx: a running chunk with current 2, total 3 renders 'Updating 2 of 3'.
- Gate: `npm test`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands (medium). Four precision corrections for the fix phase: (1) interaction-model.md:75 is the single-op 'Install / Update to version' row, :76 is single Uninstall; the bulk row is :77 and says only 'In-panel Dialog … Confirm/Cancel' — so for Update All the doc contradiction is the §4 :64 failure toast ('Partial / failed op → Toast'); the all-success summary toast and the (i/N)/'Restoring…' progress are 1.x parity gaps, not doc contradictions. (2) The live-region sentence is NOT visible feedback: LiveStatus renders `.nuiget-visually-hidden` (primitives.css:8-16), screen-reader only — this makes the gap worse for sighted users than the claim states. (3) 2.0 does have one visible in-list signal the claim omits and the fix must not regress: a per-row Spinner replacing the action button plus a status word ('Updating', etc.) via Row.tsx:106,112 driven by UpdatesTab.tsx:86-94,178-188 (§4 'Pending op'); it still gives no aggregate (i/N), no restore-phase message and no terminal summary. (4) Status-bar text with nothing queued is 'nUIget: operation running' (extension.ts:272-274), the 'N running, M queued' form appears only when pending > 0. Root cause, sharpened from 'other': host — announceOperation is event-only and no host path calls withProgress/show*Message for a ticket's outcome (settleOperation/awaitTicket in handlers.ts); webview — ops/progress already carries current/total/detail but opsReducer keeps them in `active` and no feature reads them. Proposed ≤1-day fix: in host/handlers.ts settleOperation (or a wrapper in extension.ts around queue.submit for bulk kinds) add withProgress(Notification, title from kind+count) fed by onOperationProgress(current/total/detail, 'restoring' status), and a terminal showInformationMessage / showWarningMessage(message) for completed vs partial/failed/rejected BulkOutcome (and failed single OperationOutcome per :64); tests in handlers.test.ts asserting the mock withProgress/show*Message calls for completed and partial bulk outcomes; gate `npm run test:backend`.
  - The claim stands as a parity gap and a documented-intent contradiction; it is not an owner design decision. Four precision fixes: (1) The live region is NOT visible feedback: LiveStatus renders `className="nuiget-visually-hidden"` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/LiveStatus.tsx:29); it is a screen-reader channel only, so sighted users get even less than the claim states. (2) 2.0 does have in-surface progress the claim omits and the fix must not remove: rows get a spinner plus status text "Updating"/"Installing"/"Uninstalling" or "In progress" (src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx:86-94 via IN_PROGRESS in src/webview/shared/installed/rules.ts:10; AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:67; InstalledProjectSection.tsx:74) and the details button reads "Working…" (DetailsPane.tsx:228) — interaction-model §4 "Pending op" (:63) is satisfied. (3) The doc contradiction should be anchored precisely: §5 :75 "progress toast" is the single Install/Update row and :76 the single Uninstall "outcome toast"; the bulk row (:77) specifies only the in-panel confirmation Dialog. For Update All the firm contradictions are §4 :64 "Partial / failed op: Toast (VS Code notification)", which no 2.0 code path implements for any outcome, and §6 :107; the bulk progress notification itself is a 1.x parity gap rather than a literal §5 requirement. (4) Status-bar text is `$(sync~spin) nUIget: operation running` when nothing is queued and `${active} running, ${pending} queued` otherwise (extension.ts:275-277). Root cause is better stated as "data sent by host but consumed by neither a host withProgress/notification wrapper around the ticket nor a webview reader of current/total" than "other". Severity medium stands.
  - Mechanism correct; severity should be split, not left at a single 'medium'. (1) 'No summary/failure toast after Update All' → HIGH, not medium: the claim's premise that the live-region sentence is visible feedback is false (LiveStatus is .nuiget-visually-hidden, screen-reader only), the status bar hides at completion, no banner/row state marks a failure, and the output channel is never revealed (1.x opened it via outputChannel.show(true)). A sighted user whose Update All partly fails gets zero signal — rows silently revert — which a user would call broken. (2) 'No progress notification during Update All' → MEDIUM at most: 2.0 shows per-row spinners + 'Updating' text and a status-bar spinner while running; what is lost is the N-of-M count and visibility when the panel is not in view. The claim's §5 citation (interaction-model.md:75) is the single-op row; the bulk row (:77) promises only a confirmation dialog, so the governing contradiction is §4:64 (Partial/failed → Toast) and §5:76 (outcome toast). (3) Fix is host code, not CSS, and not a scheduled deferral: in src/extension.ts subscribe to events.operationCompleted (services.ts:37) and call showWarningMessage on BulkOutcome.status !== 'completed' / OperationOutcome failed-partial (showInformationMessage on success), optionally wrap bulk ops in withProgress reading current/total from queue.onProgress (domain.ts:309-320); add a handlers/extension test asserting the toast per status; gate `npm test`. Keep the live region and row spinners — they are the 2.0 improvements (a11y announcement on both surfaces; cross-surface busy rows). (4) Context for the fix phase, not a widening of MU4: single-op failures also lost their 1.x showErrorMessage, so the subscriber should cover OperationOutcome as well as BulkOutcome.

### MU5 — No visible revalidation indicator on the Updates tab; the model's progress bar under the search box is missing in the main panel

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Spinner with 'Checking for updates...' while loading (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:449-452) and a 'Checking for more updates...' streaming indicator under the list (717-728); a re-check cleared the list and showed the spinner (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:1670-1673).
- 2.0: MainShell imports no ProgressBar (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:5) and renders none; ProjectUpdates only sets busy (aria-busy) on the listbox (UpdatesTab.tsx:167) and AllProjectsUpdates passes revalidating to the lists (AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:98, 275) with no visible element. ProgressBar is used only by BrowseList (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:83) and the sidebar (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx:164). Interaction-model §4 'Revalidating: Existing rows stay; subtle progress bar under the search box' (interaction-model.md:59). After Refresh the Updates tab shows no change until rows differ.
- Fix: Under .nuiget-main-search render <ProgressBar label="Checking for updates" /> when tab === 'updates' and (updatesSlice.revalidating || (updatesSlice.status === 'loading' && updatesSlice.rows.length > 0) || all.updates.revalidating); add the Installed equivalent in the same spot if the Installed area agrees (installedSlice.revalidating, all.revalidating).
- Files: `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx`
- Test: MainShell.test.tsx: with the updates resource revalidating, getByRole('progressbar') is present; absent once ready; sidebar test at SidebarView already shows the pattern.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The gap stands but its 1.x baseline is overstated for the Refresh scenario. (1) In 1.x single-project mode a manual Refresh with an unchanged installed list showed no Updates spinner either, and did not even re-check updates on the main panel: handleFullRefresh (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2251-2260) posts fullRefresh, NuGetPanel answers with a plain refresh message (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:770), the refresh case only sends getInstalledPackages (App.tsx:735-736), and the installedPackages handler returns prev when the id@version keys match (App.tsx:435-438), so the [installedPackages] effect holding the only setLoadingUpdates(true) (App.tsx:1672, dependency list :1687; grep shows no other call) never re-fires. The sidebar's post-operation path reaches the main panel only through the same refresh/refreshScoped messages (0457c75:src/extension.ts:175-185), so the same content-compare applies. The cited 1670-1673 evidence is the right proof for project switch, prerelease toggle and installed-set changes, not for Refresh. (2) For project switch and prerelease toggle 2.0 is at parity: the key changes, useResource returns status 'loading' with no data (useResource.ts:124-127), and UpdatesTab shows the Skeleton (UpdatesTab.tsx:152-153). (3) In 1.x All Projects mode Refresh did show a spinner (setLoadingAllProjectsUpdates(true), App.tsx:718), but the isLoading && !isStreaming branch (UpdatesTab.tsx:421) replaces the whole list with it, which §4 forbids ("Never blank the list"). Net: the real, confirmed gaps are (a) no visible indicator during same-key revalidation (Refresh, file-watcher or post-op invalidation) in either mode, contradicting interaction-model.md:59, and (b) no visible streaming indicator while first-load chunks arrive. 2.0 does better in never blanking rows on revalidation (useResource.ts:11-12, 43-44) and in putting aria-busy on the list itself; the fix should add a ProgressBar under the search box in MainShell driven by updatesSlice.revalidating / useAllProjects().updates.revalidating (and installedSlice.revalidating) rather than reintroduce the 1.x spinner-replaces-list pattern. Minor citation fix: UpdatesTab.tsx:166, not :167.
  - The claim stands (feature-not-rendered, medium). Four precisions for the fix phase:

1. Do not restore the 1.x behaviour the claim cites: clearing the list and showing a spinner (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:1670-1671) is exactly what interaction-model.md:59 forbids ("Never blank the list"). 2.0 keeping the rows is intended and is the thing to preserve; only the visible bar is missing.

2. The fix belongs in MainShell, not UpdatesTab: one `<ProgressBar>` under the search box (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:180-195), shown when the resource of the current view is revalidating (`installed.revalidating`, `updates.revalidating`, `useAllProjects().updates/installed.revalidating`, transitive), mirroring origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx:164. Note the sidebar itself is only partly compliant: its bar is gated on `installed.revalidating` alone, and its Updates section compensates with a visible SectionHeader spinner (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/UpdatesSection.tsx:178 → origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/SectionHeader.tsx:49). The main panel's Tabs primitive has no such slot (Tabs.tsx:5-23), which is why the main Updates tab is the one surface with zero visible feedback.

3. The 1.x "Checking for more updates..." streaming indicator is a separate sub-gap of lower severity: 2.0 streams rows (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/updates.tsx:59 onChunk) while `status === 'loading'`, and origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx:152 shows the Skeleton only while `rows.length === 0`, so after the first chunk only `aria-busy` remains. interaction-model.md §4 defines no "streaming" state (neither Loading-first nor Revalidating covers "some rows shown, response not complete"); this is a model gap to raise with the owner, not a documented decision.

4. When the bar lands, add the §4 state table for the main panel to ARCHITECTURE.md (today only the sidebar section has one, origin/labs/2.0:ARCHITECTURE.md:502-510) and a MainShell/UpdatesTab test mirroring SidebarView.test.tsx:306 ("a progress bar shows until the new list is there").
  - Severity medium stands; the claim is right but under-scoped and mis-located, which matters for the fix.

1. The gap is the whole main panel, not the Updates tab. The missing element is the §4 bar under the search box, owned by MainShell; the Installed tab lacks it too (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:103, 171 pass only `busy`; nothing under features/installed imports ProgressBar). Fix: one conditional in MainShell.tsx under `.nuiget-main-search`, mirroring SidebarView.tsx:164, keyed on `installedSlice.revalidating || updatesSlice.revalidating || allProjects.revalidating || allProjects.updates.revalidating` (allProjects.tsx:296, 307). Test: a MainShell.test.tsx case that emits `resources/invalidated` naming the updates key and asserts `getByRole('progressbar')` appears and disappears on the answer. Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main`. Do not add an IconButton busy prop or a Tabs affordance; the model names the bar.

2. The invisible window is narrower than "revalidation": post-op revalidation is covered by the row spinner (UpdatesTab.tsx:29, 187); project/prerelease changes swap the key and show a Skeleton (useResource.ts:124-127; UpdatesTab.tsx:152-153). Invisible cases: manual Refresh, watcher/background invalidations, and the first-load phase after the first streamed row arrives (rows > 0 drops the Skeleton at :152-153 while `busy` at :166 has no visual), which replaces 1.x's "Checking for more updates..." and runs on every first open.

3. The "trivial fix" clause refutes high, not medium: the fix is one JSX line plus a test, but rows, counts and actions are intact (so not high), while the model mandates the bar, sibling surfaces have it, 1.x had two explicit cues, and the Refresh wait is a genuine network round with zero visible feedback (so not low).

### MU6 — Updates tab shows a '0' count badge when there are no updates

- 2.0: updateCount is rows.length whenever status is ready and sources were available (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:133-135) and Tabs renders a Badge for any defined count, 0 included (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Tabs.tsx:68).
- Fix: Map a zero to undefined when building tabs (count: updateCount > 0 ? updateCount : undefined), or decide to keep '0' and record that in interaction-model §2.

- Test: MainShell.test.tsx: a ready, empty updates answer leaves the Updates tab named 'Updates' with no badge; a ready answer with 2 rows names it 'Updates 2'.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`

### MU7 — Version cell is one muted string; the newer version and the arrow are not emphasised

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `css` · Estimate: 0.25 d
- 1.18.9: Installed version muted, arrow icon and new version in --vscode-charts-green (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:134-144; markup 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:698-702).
- 2.0: version={`${installed} -> ${latest}`} as a single string (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx:186; AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:114) styled by .nuiget-row-version muted (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:156-160); main.css has no .nuiget-updates rule (grep: only .nuiget-installed*).
- Fix: Pass a ReactNode version (Row.version is ReactNode, Row.tsx:32): <>{installed} <ArrowRightIcon size={12} /> <span className="nuiget-update-to">{latest}</span></>; add --nuiget-color-update: var(--vscode-charts-green, var(--vscode-foreground)) to tokens.css with a forced-colors alias (no hex outside tokens, ADR-008); style .nuiget-update-to in main.css; keep the dialog's text form.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/AllProjectsUpdates.tsx`, `src/webview/main/main.css`, `src/webview/shared/tokens.css`
- Test: UpdatesTab.test.tsx: the row's accessible version text still reads '3.0.0 -> 3.1.0' (icon decorative) and axe passes; theme.ui.test.ts keeps contrast green.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`

### MU9 — Update rows are not sorted alphabetically

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.25 d
- 1.18.9: Rows sorted by id with localeCompare in both modes (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:206, 243).

The order therefore follows whatever the host's installed list order is; that source order was not traced.

- Files: `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/AllProjectsUpdates.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.test.tsx`

### MU11 — Row primitive is a single line; 1.x stacked name / versions / authors, so comfortable rows cannot reproduce the 1.x card (cross-area, bounds MU1/MU2)

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `primitive-lacks` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: .package-item was a card with 12px/16px padding, a 32px icon and a .package-info column holding the name (14px, weight 600), the versions line and the authors line (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:70-133, 559-563); rows estimated at 66px (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:129).
- 2.0: .nuiget-row-option is one flex line: check mark, icon, title (name and version on one baseline) and .nuiget-row-desc pushed right with margin-inline-start: auto, description white-space: nowrap (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:90-100, 143-183); comfortable height 44px (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/tokens.css:26, mirrored in Listbox.tsx:8). Interaction-model §1 asks for 'Comfortable rows (icons, description, badges)' (interaction-model.md:11) but defines no second line. With authors added (MU1/MU2) the text sits right-aligned beside the badges; .nuiget-row-desc has no overflow rule, so long descriptions may overflow (visual behaviour not verified).
- Fix: In comfortable density render the option as two lines: line 1 name + version + badges, line 2 description/status muted with overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; keep compact single-line unchanged. Raise --nuiget-row-height-comfortable if needed and keep Listbox ROW_HEIGHT in step (Listbox.tsx:7-8). Describe the comfortable row anatomy in interaction-model §1. Coordinate with the Installed area (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-131 uses the same Row).
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/composed.test.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/tokens.css`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: Row.test.tsx: comfortable row places description in its own line element and aria-describedby wiring is unchanged; axe passes in composed.test.tsx; ExTester theme.ui.test.ts keeps contrast and target-size green.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend && npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; precision only. (1) 44px is `min-height` (primitives.css:86-88), not a fixed height; rows stay 44px in practice only because every text span is `nowrap` and the option has no `flex-wrap`, so a stacked variant is a Row.tsx/primitives.css change (wrap title+desc in a column), not a token change. (2) The missing overflow rule is specific to the secondary text: `.nuiget-row-name` does have `overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis` (150-154); only `.nuiget-row-version/-description/-status` (156-162) are `nowrap` without overflow, and `.nuiget-row-desc` has `min-width:0` (181), so flex can shrink the box while the inline span overflows it; `.nuiget-listbox` is `overflow:auto` (55-59) so the result would be clipping or a horizontal scrollbar. (3) Add to the gap: the 2.0 comfortable icon is 24px (main.css:200-204 via `--nuiget-icon-size-lg`, tokens.css:31) vs 32px in 1.x (App.css:92-96); the fix should restore 32px if the card is reproduced. (4) The "text sits right-aligned beside the badges" prediction is already observable, not hypothetical: BrowseList.tsx:111 does exactly that today.
  - Two things in MU11 survive and should be re-filed separately rather than dropped. (a) Owner decision, not a bug: whether the comfortable row should stack (name+version / description / authors) like the 1.x card. Nothing documents one line as a visual choice (interaction-model.md:11 names content only; the P3.2 ruling fixes the DOM, STATUS.md:2001-2058 step 3, and its step 8 lists only positioning, actions-visibility and focus-ring CSS). If the owner wants it, the fix is CSS-only under the ruled DOM: make `.nuiget-row--comfortable .nuiget-row-option` wrap title and desc onto two lines (e.g. `flex-wrap: wrap` with `.nuiget-row-desc { flex-basis: 100%; margin-inline-start: 0 }` scoped to comfortable) in src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css; raise or keep `--nuiget-row-height-comfortable` as a min-height (tokens.css:26) and mirror ROW_HEIGHT in Listbox.tsx:8 (webview-tokens.test.ts:69-76 enforces the mirror); the virtualizer already measures (Listbox.tsx:96). Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared` and `npx vitest run --project backend src/test/lint`. No Row prop is needed for authors: features pass them through `description` as BrowseList.tsx:109 does, or MU1/MU2 adds a Verified `Badge` per §1, not a 1.x-style authors line. (b) A real, unruled CSS gap, low severity: `.nuiget-row-description` and `.nuiget-row-status` have `white-space: nowrap` (primitives.css:156-162) with no `overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis`; only `.nuiget-row-name` clips (:150-154), and `.nuiget-row-desc` (:177-183) has `min-width: 0` but no overflow rule, so a long description (BrowseList joins authors, downloads and description with " · ") extends past the option into the hidden actions area and widens the `.nuiget-listbox { overflow: auto }` scroll container (:55-59). Rendering not verified (read-only task); CSS reading only. Fix: `overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis` on `.nuiget-row-description` (and `.nuiget-row-desc { overflow: hidden }`), one Row test asserting the class rules via the stylesheet lint pattern, gate as above. Also correct the claim's wording: "comfortable height 44px" is a min-height and virtualizer estimate, not a fixed height.
  - Medium stands, but on different grounds and with four corrections. (1) The hedge is removable: at the default 50% split the one-line layout truncates "Newtonsoft.Json" to "Newt…" and gives the Browse and Installed listboxes a horizontal scrollbar whenever a description is present — which is every nuget.org browse row (description is required, domain.ts:86) and every enriched Installed row (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124, enricher.ts:65-67). Severity should rest on that measured defect, not on "cannot reproduce the 1.x card". (2) Root cause is the primitive's CSS, not a missing capability ("primitive-lacks" overstates it): Row.tsx already has slots for icon/name/version/description/badges/status; .nuiget-row--comfortable is min-height, not height; Listbox measures each row via getBoundingClientRect (Listbox.tsx:96), so variable-height rows already work; the height token may be retuned by value (STATUS.md:1976) with the ROW_HEIGHT mirror gated by src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts:69-76. (3) It is nonetheless not "trivial CSS": badges and description share one span (.nuiget-row-desc, Row.tsx:94-99), so a two-line comfortable layout (badges beside the title, authors/description below) needs a grid with display:contents on that span or a DOM regroup, a retuned --nuiget-row-height-comfortable + ROW_HEIGHT.comfortable, the compact density left single-line (sidebar), and Row.test.tsx's accessible-description assertion ('Centrally managed Structured logging Updating') plus a new overflow test as regression gates — a half-day item, not a padding change. Short-term one-liner mitigation (overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis on .nuiget-row-description and flex:none or min-width on .nuiget-row-title) would stop the name truncation and the scrollbar but keep the single line. (4) 1.x rows showed no description at all (.package-description is a details-panel rule, App.css:582); the card to match is name / version / authors, and 2.0 Browse already shows strictly more fields per row than 1.x Browse did — the layout, not the data, is what fails. Scoping: medium for Browse and Installed today, and for Updates once MU1/MU2 put authors into its rows; for Updates/AllProjectsUpdates as they stand (no description passed) MU11 alone is cosmetic. Note for MU1/MU2: the enricher already delivers authors/verified/iconUrl into the installed metadata map, which InstalledProjectSection does not pass as icon/badges — that is a feature-rendering gap, not a host or protocol gap.

**Better in 2.0 (do not regress):**
- Unreachable source is a warning Banner with Retry plus 'Updates could not be checked' instead of 1.x's misleading 'All packages are up to date' (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx:156-159, 214-215; AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:299-301) — 1.x turned a check error into an empty list (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:1070-1075; UpdatesTab.tsx:482-488).
- Automatic re-check once a failed source recovers (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/updates.tsx:67-81; allProjects.tsx:222-235).
- Rows stay on refresh/invalidation (useResource keeps data for the same key, updates.tsx:87); 1.x cleared the list and showed the spinner on every re-check (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:1670-1673).
- Update all asks for confirmation in a dialog listing each package 'id: from -> to', grouped per project in All Projects (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdateAllDialog.tsx:15-37); 1.x fired the bulk immediately (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:321-373).
- Central Package Management: a central row updates through a preview dialog of affected projects and is excluded from bulk; an override row updates in place and joins bulk (UpdatesTab.tsx:81-82, 106-109; CentralUpdateDialog.tsx).
- Update all works without pre-selecting (candidates default) and the button reads 'Update selected (N)' vs 'Update all (N)' (UpdatesTab.tsx:83-84, 210-211); busy rows are excluded from candidates (UpdatesTab.tsx:82).
- Per-row Update action button and Enter on a row update one package (UpdatesTab.tsx:175, 194-196); 1.x had no per-row action.
- Stale-response guards: sequence-numbered All Projects stream that applies only the latest request's chunks (allProjects.tsx:187-219) and keyed single-project resource (updates.tsx:49-61).
- All Projects: per-project error banner with Retry, Unsupported badge and read-only rows (AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:79-81, 91, 142), workspace-folder grouping (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/allProjects/folderGroups.tsx), and the section count always visible (AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:140).
- Accessibility: role=listbox with roving tabIndex, aria-multiselectable with Space toggling, keys described via aria-describedby, role=toolbar with one tab stop, rows aria-busy with status text (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx:21-41; Toolbar.tsx:84-87; Row.tsx:80-94); 1.x rows were plain divs with a nested checkbox.
- Theme tokens only, no hex colours, forced-colors and reduced-motion covered in one place (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/tokens.css:11-176); 1.x App.css carried hard-coded fallbacks (e.g. #4ec9b0 at 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:135,142).
- The Verified badge is rendered on single-project rows (UpdatesTab.tsx:192) and the tab count is a Badge that is part of the tab's accessible name (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Tabs.tsx:66-68).

**Notes:**
- Nothing was executed (Node is not on PATH, task is read-only); every finding comes from reading 0457c75 and origin/labs/2.0. MU3 is a static trace from PackageWorkflows.ts to the ops reducer; the row-description overflow in MU11 is not visually verified; the host's installed/update row order behind MU9 was not traced.
