## Main panel: Browse/search results, quick-search suggestions, details panel (Details + README tabs, version selector, Install/Update/Downgrade/Uninstall, Multi Install). 1.x ref 0457c75 (src/webview/app/**, src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts, src/services/**); 2.0 ref origin/labs/2.0 (src/webview/main/features/{search,details}/**, src/webview/shared/{store,search,primitives}/**, src/core/{catalog,feeds}/**, src/host/**).

### MB1 — Comfortable Browse rows render as one plain line: no two-line layout, description right-aligned and unclipped, 24px icon

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `primitive-lacks` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: A browse row was a 32px icon beside three stacked lines: bold package name; meta line with v{version} and a download count with icon; authors line with the Verified badge; 12px/16px padding and a bottom border (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:3060-3083; 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:70-132,559-574).
- 2.0: Row renders the title and the description as flex siblings on ONE line: `.nuiget-row--comfortable` differs from compact only by `min-height: 44px` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:86-88); `.nuiget-row-desc` is pushed to the right with `margin-inline-start: auto` (:177-183) and `.nuiget-row-description` is `white-space: nowrap` with no overflow clipping (:156-162), so a long description crushes the name and overflows the option/listbox (expected from the CSS; not rendered-verified). BrowseList joins authors, downloads and description into one muted string (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:109); the icon is 24px `--nuiget-icon-size-lg` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/main.css:200-204; src/webview/shared/tokens.css:31). Contradicts docs/ux/interaction-model.md:11, which defines comfortable rows as a distinct density with icons, description and badges.
- Fix: In Row, for density='comfortable' only, wrap the option text in a `.nuiget-row-text` column: line 1 = name, version, badges (and an optional new `meta?: ReactNode` for downloads); line 2 = `.nuiget-row-description` with `overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis`. Leave the compact markup and CSS byte-identical. Size the comfortable icon slot at 32px via a new `--nuiget-icon-size-xl: 32px` token (add to tokens.css and to the REQUIRED list of webview-tokens.test.ts). BrowseList passes `description={result.description}` alone and renders `by {authors}` + downloads as meta. Optional row separator with `--nuiget-color-border`. Amend interaction-model §1 to say comfortable = two lines.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/composed.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.test.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/tokens.css`, `src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: Row.test.tsx: 'a comfortable row puts its description under the title and clips it; a compact row is unchanged' (structure + class assertions, axe); BrowseList.test.tsx: extend 'lists the results with the metadata that came first…' to assert the description element is separate from authors/downloads; composed.test.tsx stays green.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/primitives src/webview/main/features/search && npx vitest run --project backend src/test/lint && npm run lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The gap is real and the root cause (primitive-lacks) is right; three refinements. (1) "Contradicts docs/ux/interaction-model.md:11" is overstated: that line lists the CONTENTS of comfortable rows (icons, description, badges) and 2.0 renders all three (BrowseList.tsx:111 icon, :109 description, :112-116 Verified/Installed badges); no 2.0 design doc specifies a two-line/stacked layout (grep of docs/ux, docs/adr returned none; the Row spec at docs/plans/STATUS.md:2019-2025 is DOM-only). So MB1 is a parity/legibility gap vs 1.x, not a violation of the written 2.0 contract — the fix must update the interaction model (and the Row spec) together with the CSS. (2) Overflow consequence, precisely: `.nuiget-listbox` is `overflow: auto` (primitives.css:55-59), so an unclipped nowrap description produces a horizontal scrollbar on the list rather than paint escaping the listbox; "crushes the name" holds because `.nuiget-row-title` and `.nuiget-row-desc` are both shrinkable flex siblings and `.nuiget-row-name` is the element with ellipsis (:143-154). Keep the "not rendered-verified" hedge. (3) Things 2.0 does better that the fix must not regress: browse rows now show the package description (1.x browse rows never did — App.tsx:3060-3083 has only name/version/downloads/authors), Verified is kept as a text Badge with tooltip, and an "Installed" badge is new (BrowseList.tsx:114-115). Related sibling gap for the same fix: 2.0 renders no icon slot at all when `iconUrl` is absent or not a `data:` URI (BrowseList.tsx:111; Row.tsx:97), so rows with and without icons misalign, whereas 1.x always reserved 32px via the `defaultPackageIcon` fallback (App.tsx:3061-3065); reserve the icon column regardless.
  - The claim's facts stand (one-line flex row, right-pushed unclipped nowrap description, joined meta string, 24px icon, 1.x three-line 32px-icon row) and no 2.0 design document or plan ruling records a one-line comfortable layout, so it is NOT an intended, documented decision and cannot be refuted on the design-intent lens. Five corrections: (1) "Contradicts interaction-model.md:11" is overstated — §1 names content (icons, description, badges), all of which the 2.0 comfortable row renders (Row.tsx:97,104-105; BrowseList.tsx:109-117); §1 is silent on arrangement. Reword to "unspecified by §1; no document decides the layout — owner decision needed on stacked vs one-line." (2) The 24px icon is a documented token decision (P3.1 ruling STATUS.md:1976; tokens.css:31), so that sub-point is an owner decision, not a defect; move it to a low/owner-decision note. The 44px row height is in the same ruling as a "starting value ... may retune by value" that was never retuned (open, not decided), and 44px fits two text lines (13px × 1.4 × 2 ≈ 36px), so the token does not force one line. (3) "`.nuiget-row--comfortable` differs from compact only by `min-height`" is imprecise: comfortable also renders the icon (Row.tsx:97) and un-hides description/status that compact clips (primitives.css:186-193). What is missing is a layout rule for the comfortable desc, not the density switch. (4) The description is a 2.0 addition — 1.x browse rows had none (App.tsx:3067-3083) — so the overflow is a defect in a new element; the fix must keep description and badges (§1), not "restore" 1.x's row verbatim. (5) Overflow mechanics, precisely: `.nuiget-listbox { overflow: auto }` (primitives.css:55-59) means an unclipped description produces a horizontal scrollbar on the list rather than bleeding outside it; the name shrinks because both `.nuiget-row-title` and `.nuiget-row-desc` have `min-width: 0`. This remains CSS-inferred — no UI test renders a comfortable row. Root cause "primitive-lacks" is correct: the defect is in primitives.css, not in BrowseList's data. Severity: keep high for the layout + missing-clipping combination (owner decides stacked vs one line; the nowrap-without-ellipsis is a bug under either choice); the icon size is low/owner-decision. Separately worth a gap of its own: ADR-006's bundled default icon is not implemented in 2.0 browse rows.
  - Severity: medium, not high. Root cause: CSS (comfortable-density layout rules missing in primitives.css), not primitive-lacks — Row.tsx already takes icon/description/badges. The interaction-model.md:11 contradiction does not exist; the doc defines densities by icon/description presence, which 2.0 implements. Fix item: in origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css add `.nuiget-row-desc { flex: 1 1 0 }` (the rule that actually stops the name crush) and `.nuiget-row-description { min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis }`; for a stacked comfortable layout add `flex-wrap: wrap` on `.nuiget-row--comfortable .nuiget-row-option` with `.nuiget-row--comfortable .nuiget-row-desc { flex-basis: 100%; margin-inline-start: 0 }`; raise `--nuiget-icon-size-lg` to 32px only if the browse icon should match 1.x (value change, same token). Add a Row.test.tsx case asserting the comfortable class set and a jsdom style assertion, and a theme.ui.test.ts/main-search.ui.test.ts check that `.nuiget-row-name` width ≥ a floor with a 600-char description. The fix lands once on the primitive and also fixes InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124, TransitiveSection.tsx:78 and AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx:129. Keep the description and Installed badge that 1.x never had. If the fix phase wants 1.x's separate authors+Verified line, that needs structured Row props (component change, ≤1 day).

### MB2 — No default package icon: a row without a host-resolved icon shows nothing, so names misalign (ADR-006 promises the bundled default)

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Every browse row showed `defaultPackageIcon` (read from the root dataset) when `iconUrl` was absent or failed to load (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:22,3061-3065).
- 2.0: BrowseList passes an icon only when `iconUrl` starts with `data:`, else `undefined`, and Row then renders no icon span at all (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:111; src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx:97). The host still emits `data-package-icon` on the root element (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/services.ts:106-108) and the CSP `img-src ${cspSource}` admits it (:113), but no 2.0 webview reads that dataset (git grep packageIcon over src/webview: no hit). docs/adr/006-icons-and-csp.md:18 states 'Icons that fail resolve to undefined and the webview shows the bundled default icon'. Icon sourcing itself is at parity: both versions use the flat-container /icon (0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:790-801,2234-2238; origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/icons.ts:199-201).
- Fix: Add a tiny helper `defaultPackageIcon(): string | undefined` reading `document.getElementById('root')?.dataset.packageIcon` once. In BrowseList render `<img className="nuiget-browse-icon" src={iconUrl?.startsWith('data:') ? iconUrl : defaultPackageIcon()} alt="" />` whenever either exists. Set the dataset in the frontend test helper. (Installed/Updates rows belong to another area but should use the same helper.)
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/packageIcon.ts`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.test.tsx`, `src/test/helpers/frontend.tsx`
- Test: BrowseList.test.tsx: 'a result without a host icon shows the bundled default icon' asserting the img src equals the dataset value; existing icon test unchanged.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/search && npm run lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; three precision notes for the fix phase, none refuting:
1. Scope of the 1.x behaviour is broader than "every browse row": 1.x also showed the default icon in Installed rows (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:183-190), transitive rows (:1457-1461, :1568-1572) and Updates rows (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:609-613, :689-693), all fed by `defaultPackageIcon` from App.tsx:3114/3177. The default-icon fix should apply wherever comfortable rows render in the main panel, not only BrowseList.
2. The 2.0 behaviour is pinned by a test: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.test.tsx:79-80 asserts no `<img>` for a non-data: `iconUrl` ("Only a data: URI is an icon here; the policy of the webview loads no other image"). That rationale does not cover the bundled default, which is a `cspSource` URI admitted by services.ts:113; the fix must update this assertion rather than be blocked by it.
3. The 1.x sidebar never read `data-package-icon` either (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetSidebarPanel.ts:1442 emitted it; `git grep iconUrl|packageIcon 0457c75 -- src/webview/sidebar` → no hit), so the sidebar's lack of icons is parity (and matches interaction-model §1), not part of MB2.
  - Two sharpenings, neither weakens the claim. (1) Scope of the owed default: ADR-006:18 speaks of icons that "fail", but in 2.0 `IconResolver.icon` answers `undefined` for a feed that has no icon (`missing`, icons.ts:211), a non-concrete version (:141-143) and a policy-refused package (:144-147) as well as for failures; the webview contract is the same in every case (undefined -> bundled default), so the fix must show the default on every `undefined` iconUrl, not only on failures. (2) The fix conflicts with an existing test: BrowseList.test.tsx:81 asserts `querySelector('img')` is null for the https-URL row; after adding the default `<img>` that assertion must change to expect `src` equal to the root dataset URI (keep the data:-only guard for remote URLs at BrowseList.tsx:111, which is correct and intentional per the test comment at :80). Add a Row/BrowseList test that a result without iconUrl renders the default icon; gate `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/search src/webview/shared/primitives`. Related but outside MB2: installed/updates rows in 2.0 pass no `icon` at all even when the host enriched `iconUrl` (BrowseList.tsx:111 is the only `icon=` in src/webview), so the default-icon fix should land in a shared place (Row fallback or a shared `defaultPackageIcon` module) that those lists can reuse.
  - Mechanism confirmed in full; root cause stands (feature-not-rendered in BrowseList + Row primitive reserves no icon slot; host dataset emitted but unread). Severity: high → MEDIUM. Grounds: icons are present at first paint because the metadata chunk precedes the search answer (Catalog.ts:443-448, search.tsx:103), so the defect is a static 30px ragged left edge confined to mixed lists (uniform lists align regardless of icon frequency), plus a missing placeholder glyph that the interaction model never promises (interaction-model.md:11) — only ADR-006:18 does. A user sees it as sloppy ("clearly worse"), not as broken or missing functionality. The fix is a ≤1-hour item: Row.tsx:97 render the icon span whenever density is comfortable; primitives.css:195 add `inline-size: var(--nuiget-icon-size-lg)`; BrowseList.tsx:111 fall back to `document.getElementById('root')?.dataset.packageIcon` for non-data: URIs; update BrowseList.test.tsx:80-81 to expect the default `<img>` and add a Row test for the reserved slot; gate `npm run test:frontend`. FLAG FOR THE FIX PHASE, as its own item (not MB2): the icon source breaker (icons.ts:24,185-187,218; 404 = miss at flatContainer.ts:179-180; reset only on sources/workspace invalidation, Catalog.ts:555-560) can suppress nuget.org icons for the whole session after five first-time misses — the plausible cause of the owner's "no package icons" in browse on the second machine. A default-icon fix would mask that symptom with placeholders everywhere, so land the breaker item first or alongside MB2.

### MB3 — Search results wait for per-row enrichment (lookup + icon fetch for every result) before the first row renders

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `host-not-sent` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: The host posted lite results immediately and ran enrichment as a second `searchResultsMetadata` phase, only for rows lacking iconUrl/verified; the UI merged it in place (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:593-629; 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:494-512).
- 2.0: `Catalog.search` awaits `searchEnricher.enrich` for ALL results and only then returns (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:443-448). That enricher runs a (cached) `searchById` lookup plus a flat-container icon fetch per row at concurrency 8 (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/feedClients.ts:128,136; src/core/feeds/enricher.ts:46-82), even for API rows that already carry verified/authors/description (src/core/feeds/search.ts:302-317). The slice shows results only on the final response (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/search.tsx:130-143), so the list shows a skeleton until every icon resolved: up to ~3 round-trip rounds × 2 requests for 20 results on a cold cache. The ExTester test logs this cold/warm time (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-search.ui.test.ts:65-69,82-85). The largest latency regression in this area.
- Fix: Step 1 (core only, no contract change): Catalog.search hands the enricher the rows' known verified/authors/description; the enricher skips `lookup` for rows that already have them (icon fetch still runs). Step 2 (CONTRACT CHANGE: src/protocol/messages.ts is frozen, RUN.md:75; sentinel `.claude/.contract-change`): add a `{ kind: 'results'; results: PackageSearchResult[]; sourcesUnavailable?: boolean }` chunk to `search/query` (messages.ts:134), emitted before `enrich`; the search slice sets `answered` from that chunk with `revalidating: true` until the `metadata` chunk and the response arrive. Alternative without a new chunk kind: a `search/metadata` request mirroring `transitive/metadata` (messages.ts:126) that the slice calls after the results.
- Files: `src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts`, `src/core/catalog/Catalog.test.ts`, `src/core/feeds/enricher.ts`, `src/core/feeds/enricher.test.ts`, `src/protocol/messages.ts`, `src/webview/shared/store/search.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/search.test.tsx`, `docs/protocol.md`
- Test: Catalog.test.ts: 'search emits the results chunk before enrichment and the metadata chunk after'; enricher.test.ts: 'skips the lookup for a row that already carries verified and authors'; search.test.tsx: 'shows the results chunk before the metadata and merges the metadata when it comes'.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project backend src/core/catalog src/core/feeds && npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/store && npm run docs:check && npm run lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The claim is right in mechanism and root cause (host-not-sent; the protocol lacks a bare-row chunk for search). Four precisions:

1. 1.x's "immediate" first paint was itself lite, not the full row. In `liteMode` the 1.x API path blanked `verified`, `description` and `authors` for every row (0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:783-785); only id, version, `totalDownloads` and `iconUrl` (a URL string, no fetch) were sent (:790-801). The browser loaded the icon after first paint. So the parity target is "rows appear before enrichment", not "1.x showed rich rows immediately".

2. 1.x Phase 2 was not "only for rows lacking iconUrl/verified". The gate is `results.some(r => r.iconUrl === undefined && r.verified === undefined)` but the whole `results` array is passed to `enrichSearchResultMetadata`, which queries every row (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:620-621; NuGetPackageService.ts:1083-1088). Because lite mode set `verified: undefined` on all rows, the gate reduced to "some row has no icon"; when every result had an icon, Phase 2 never ran and authors/verified stayed blank in 1.x. 1.x's per-row enrichment was also one `q=packageid:` request per package, hard-wired to nuget.org (NuGetPackageService.ts:2184-2250).

3. "The largest latency regression in this area" is not verifiable by code reading (no 1.x cold-search timing exists in either ref). What is on record: 2.0 cold search measured 2323 ms at P5.6 and 1086 ms (warm 541 ms) in the P7.2a CI baseline, both under their targets (perf-baseline.md:35-36); the bare-row chunk (P7.2d) was consciously deferred (perf-baseline.md:49-50, STATUS.md:3479,3520). Keep severity medium and quote the numbers rather than the superlative.

4. The fix cannot copy 1.x's URL-string icons: 2.0's webview CSP is `img-src ${cspSource} data:` only (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/services.ts:113) and icons are host-fetched by design so private feeds work with credentials (icons.ts:1-5). The ≤1-day fix is a `{ kind: 'found' }` bare-row chunk on `search/query` before `enrich`, mirroring the existing `updates/get` precedent (messages.ts:124 `chunk: { kind: 'found'; update }`; Catalog.ts:253-254 `check(..., emit, ...)` then `enrich`), with the slice rendering `found` rows and merging the later `metadata` chunk via the existing `withMetadata` (search.tsx:79-97). Secondary win: skip the `lookup` leg for rows that already carry `verified`/`authors`/`description` from the API (search.ts:302-317) — only the icon fetch is needed for them. Test to extend: Catalog.test.ts:741-757 (`events` should become `['found ...', 'metadata 1', 'resolved']`). This is a protocol contract change (D6) and needs its ⚑ ruling per the plan's own rule.
  - Verdict: a decision for the owner (open P7.2d, the lite `found` chunk on `search/query`, under a D6 contract-change ruling), not a bug. Four points the owner should see: (a) One sub-claim is right and NOT covered by any ruling: enricher.ts:46-58 calls `lookup` (a `packageid:` search, src/core/feeds/search.ts:260,269-270) for every row unconditionally, and `withMetadata` in search.tsx:78-99 only fills gaps, so API rows that already carry verified/authors/description (search.ts:302-317) trigger a redundant lookup each. It costs no extra wall-clock round (it runs in Promise.all with the mandatory icon fetch, enricher.ts:52-56) and is cached through `liteLookup` (src/core/catalog/feedClients.ts:116-128), but it is an undocumented inefficiency fixable without a contract change (skip lookup when the row already has verified+authors+description). (b) The revisit trigger is CI-only, on nuget.org with one source: CI measured 535-1086 ms cold, while the one local measurement was 2323 ms (STATUS.md:77); with several sources the enricher iterates allowed sources per row until one knows the id (enricher.ts:72-79) and the icon resolver iterates sources too (src/core/feeds/icons.ts:189-218), so the owner's machine can sit above target without the safety valve firing. (c) 1.x's fast first row was data-poorer: liteMode stripped verified/authors/description (0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:783-785; NuGetPanel.ts:593-600 passes liteMode=true) and Phase 2 ran only when some row lacked both iconUrl and verified (NuGetPanel.ts:619), yet browse rows render pkg.verified and pkg.authors (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:3078-3081); so a result set where every API row had an icon never got its badge/author line in 1.x. (d) The claim's "largest latency regression" cites no 1.x number; the only 1.x figure is the CHANGELOG claim of ~100-300 ms for the single-nuget.org API path (origin/labs/2.0:CHANGELOG.md:619), unmeasured in CI.
  - Keep MB3 at medium, but correct five points in the write-up:

1. Root cause is not "host-not-sent". The host does send the `metadata` chunk before the response (handlers.ts:297-305; handlers.test.ts:352-365). The hold is a ruled sequencing in core: "From the P4.9 ruling: `search/query`'s `metadata` chunk precedes the response, so results appear after the `take` rows are enriched" (docs/plans/STATUS.md:77), implemented by the `await` in Catalog.ts:443-448, and the slice applies the chunk only at completion (search.tsx:128-136). Category: core sequencing under a frozen contract, plus slice does not render chunks progressively.

2. The 1.x baseline is overstated. The 1.x panel always asked for liteMode (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:594-601); in liteMode API rows carried `verified: undefined`, `description: ''`, `authors: ''` (0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:783-785) and only an icon URL that the webview `<img>` fetched lazily (NuGetPackageService.ts:790-802; 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:3047). 1.x browse rows never rendered a description at all (App.tsx:3040-3140 shows icon, downloads, verified badge, authors only). Phase 2 ran only when some row lacked both icon and verified (NuGetPanel.ts:616), but then over ALL results (NuGetPanel.ts:617) with one `packageid:` search request per row (NuGetPackageService.ts:1085-1088, 2219-2220) — the same N lookups, backgrounded. So the regression is time-to-first-paint only; 2.0's first paint is strictly richer (authors, verified, description, icon inline — BrowseList.tsx:108-118).

3. "3 rounds x 2 requests" overstates the serial cost. Lookup and icon run in `Promise.all` per row (enricher.ts:54-58), so each round costs max(lookup, icon), not their sum; the transport speaks HTTP/2 to `https://api.nuget.org` by default (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/http/Transport.ts:37-38), so the ~40 requests multiplex on one session. Lookups are cached 5 min and found icons indefinitely, both persisted (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/cache/CachePolicy.ts:35-38), so the icon download is paid once per package per machine.

4. The proposed fix cannot be a plain <=1-day item. `search/query` lives in `src/protocol/messages.ts:134`, frozen under decision D6 (docs/plans/2026-10-11-nuiget-2.0-execution-plan.md:50); perf-baseline.md:49-50 pre-specifies P7.2d as a contract-change item with its own ruling and forbids opening it before two consecutive over-target cold runs — which have not occurred. The code change is small (emit results before `enrich`, apply chunks incrementally in the slice, keep the existing `handlers.test.ts` chunk-order test); the gate is procedural.

5. One omitted amplifier that strengthens the claim in worst cases: the icon fetch passes no `timeoutMs` (flatContainer.ts:175), inheriting the 10 s default (Transport.ts:54,154-155), so one stalled icon from a healthy-but-slow feed holds the whole response; and a `failed` icon result is not cached (icons.ts `resolveFromSources`, the `continue` on `failed`), so a feed that errors on `/icon` re-pays that cost on every search. Neither affects the nuget.org happy path.

### MB4 — Multi Install is offered only in All Projects mode; a single-project selection in a multi-project workspace lost it

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: The browse details showed a 'Multi Install' dropdown whenever `projects.length > 1` and the package was not installed in the selected project, listing every project with its installed version (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:301-379; 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2385-2389,2223-2236; 0457c75:CHANGELOG.md:543,549; 0457c75:README.md:18).
- 2.0: `pickProjects = allMode && selection.origin === 'browse' && !stale`; outside All Projects the primary button installs into the selected project only (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.tsx:99,113,126-130,259-268). MultiInstallDialog reads the allProjects slice, which is active only under ALL_PROJECTS (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/allProjects.tsx:290). docs/ux/interaction-model.md:42-50 scopes the picker to All Projects, so the doc codified the regression.
- Fix: Add a secondary 'Install in projects…' button to the version row when `projects.length > 1 && selection.origin === 'browse' && entry === undefined && !allMode`. Give the allProjects slice an `enableInstalled()` (same pattern as `enableTransitive`, allProjects.tsx:285) so the dialog can stream `allProjects/installed` outside All Projects while it is open. Reuse MultiInstallDialog unchanged (N76 CPM exclusion kept). Amend interaction-model §3/§7.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/details/MultiInstallDialog.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/allProjects.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/allProjects.test.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: DetailsPane.test.tsx: 'a browse selection in a single project with several projects offers Install in projects… and installs into the picked ones'; allProjects.test.tsx: 'enableInstalled streams the installed lists outside All Projects'.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/details src/webview/shared/store && npm run docs:check && npm run lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The claim stands; precise corrections and sharpenings: (1) Citation: `allProjects.tsx:290` only shows `status` idling; the operative gates are `:149` (`active`), `:164-170` (`reset` when inactive, emptying `chunks`) and `:266-267` (fetch effect skipped when inactive). Consequence: ungating `pickProjects` alone would open a dialog over an empty chunks map — the dialog needs its own `allProjects/installed` request on open (as 1.x `requestStreamedMultiInstall`, 0457c75:App.tsx:210-214, did), so root cause is feature-not-rendered PLUS dialog-data-source-tied-to-inactive-slice. (2) The host needs no change: `handlers.ts:292` answers `allProjects/installed` regardless of selection; the fix is webview + docs + tests only. (3) Scope is main panel only: the sidebar's project QuickPick was All-Projects-browse-gated in 1.x too (0457c75:NuGetSidebarPanel.ts:1098,1166 ↔ origin/labs/2.0:SidebarHost.ts:118,161) — no sidebar regression; do not touch SidebarHost. (4) A 2.0 test pins the regressed behaviour: `DetailsPane.test.tsx:164-177` asserts a browse result goes straight to `ops/install` into the selected project; the fix must retitle/reassert that test (behaviour change with updated coverage, not a weakening) and add a `projects.length > 1` single-project picker test beside `:311`. (5) Doc codification is wider than interaction-model §3 (`:44-50`): `README.md:27` and `ARCHITECTURE.md:683` also scope it to All Projects and need the same edit; gate `npm run docs:check`. (6) Carry over 1.x's hide-when-installed rule (0457c75:PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:302 `!isInstalled`, rationale CHANGELOG.md:549): a single-project picker should be offered only when `entry === undefined`, otherwise the fix recreates the Uninstall/Multi-Install scope confusion that entry removed. Severity medium is right: the capability still exists but requires switching to All Projects (a full allProjects re-stream) and back.
  - Three corrections to MB4 if it is kept in the report:

(a) The causal story is inverted. The §3 scoping was written 2026-10-11 in the owner-landed design commit b8a97a8 (docs/ux/interaction-model.md:41-44), four days before the first details-pane and picker code (f8bfe3f, 359a2a4 on 2026-10-15); the doc was not adjusted to match the code. Reclassify from "regression" to "owner decision"; severity is then a product question, not a defect rating.

(b) Root cause is design scope, not "feature-not-rendered". §7 specifies the single-project details pane with one primary button and no picker (interaction-model.md:119-125); plan P5.9 places the picker in the `all-projects` feature (plan:282) and P5.7 `details` has none (plan:280); the `allProjects` slice is inactive outside ALL_PROJECTS by design (allProjects.tsx:288-290) and `pickProjects` gates on `allMode` to match (DetailsPane.tsx:99).

(c) What the claim gets right and the owner should act on: the loss of the 1.x single-project-mode affordance (0457c75:PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:302) is recorded nowhere as a removal — not in CHANGELOG.md:12-17 Breaking items, D1–D13, any ADR, or the ledger. Owner picks one: (1) keep the design and add a CHANGELOG `### Changed` headline "**Multi-project install is offered under All Projects**"; or (2) a ≤1-day parity item: DetailsPane.tsx — `pickProjects = (projects?.length ?? 0) > 1 && selection.origin === 'browse' && entry === undefined && !stale` (replacing `allMode`), and MultiInstallDialog.tsx requests `allProjects/installed` itself on open (or the slice gains an on-demand activation) instead of relying on ALL_PROJECTS activation; keep the N76 CPM-preview exclusion; test in DetailsPane.test.tsx "a browse selection in a single project of a multi-project workspace offers the picker" and MultiInstallDialog.test.tsx "the picker lists every project when opened outside All Projects"; update interaction-model.md §3 and §7 in the same change (the doc requires it, :4-5); gate `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/details` and `npm run docs:check`.
  - Keep MB4 at medium but narrow it: 2.0 did not lose Multi Install; it moved the only entry point to All Projects and removed every in-UI pointer to it from the default single-project view, along with the inline per-project installed-version glance. Add to the fix item: (1) the sidebar must not change — 1.x already scoped its picker to All Projects (SidebarApp.tsx:1246-1260); (2) the fix is not CSS — MultiInstallDialog depends on useAllProjects(), idle outside ALL_PROJECTS (allProjects.tsx:149,290), so either add a secondary 'Install in several projects…' action in single-project browse details that opens the dialog backed by an on-demand `allProjects/installed` request (mirroring 1.x's `checkAllProjectsInstalled` with `context: 'multiInstall'`, App.tsx:210-214, and still honoring the `cpm/preview` and 'not listed yet' blocks), or, as a half-day mitigation, a hint on the primary button/note that switches the selector to All Projects; (3) the all-projects-mode behaviour (primary button 'Install…' opens the picker, DetailsPane.test.tsx:311-339) is better than 1.x's disabled Install button plus separate dropdown (PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:282-285) and must not regress; (4) update docs/ux/interaction-model.md §3 and README.md:25-28 if the entry point is widened.

### MB5 — Ctrl+Enter on a browse row no longer installs; details buttons carry no key hints

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: Ctrl+Enter on the focused browse row installed the selected version (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:3030,2292-2296); button titles said 'Install (Ctrl+Enter)' / 'Uninstall (Del)' (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:232,288); 0457c75:README.md:15 'Ctrl+Enter to quick install'.
- 2.0: BrowseList wires only `onSelect`/`onActivate` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:88-98) although Listbox offers `onSecondaryActivate` for Ctrl/Cmd+Enter (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx:36-37,149-153); the list hint is 'Enter shows the package's details.' (BrowseList.tsx:15); the 2.0 README lists Ctrl+Enter for the sidebar only (origin/labs/2.0:README.md:120); DetailsPane button titles carry only the disabled reason (DetailsPane.tsx:225).
- Fix: Extract the install decision of DetailsPane.changeVersion (ops/install, cpm/preview, CentralUpdateDialog) into a `useInstallAction` hook. BrowseList passes `onSecondaryActivate={item => install(item.result.id, item.result.version, item.result.sourceUrl)}` into the committed single project (in All Projects: open the multi-install picker). Update HINT to name Ctrl+Enter; add '(Ctrl+Enter)' and '(Delete)' to the details button titles; update the README keyboard table and interaction-model §5.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/details/useInstallAction.ts`, `src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.tsx`, `README.md`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: BrowseList.test.tsx: 'Ctrl+Enter on a row installs the version found into the selected project (ops/install with its source)'; DetailsPane.test.tsx stays green after the extraction.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features && npm run docs:check && npm run lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; four precisions for the fix item. (1) The claim understates 1.x: there were two Ctrl+Enter install paths, both gone in 2.0 — the browse-row handler (App.tsx:2292→3030) and a search-box handler that quick-installed the highlighted quick-search suggestion's latest version (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2663-2670 `e.ctrlKey && e.key === 'Enter'` → `pendingQuickInstallRef` → `installPackage` at App.tsx:793-808; README.md:78). 2.0's `useSearchTyping.ts` has no ctrl/meta handling (grep empty), so the fix should cover the search box too, not only BrowseList. (2) Only the hint is lost for Uninstall, not the behaviour: 2.0's Installed list wires `onDelete={item => onUninstall(item.entry)}` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx:109) and its hidden listbox hint says "Delete uninstalls it" (:39); the regression is the button tooltip only (DetailsPane.tsx:200). The browse Ctrl+Enter loss is a real behaviour loss. (3) Design-doc angle: origin/labs/2.0:docs/ux/interaction-model.md:87-89 is a generic listbox contract ("Ctrl+Enter (Cmd+Enter on macOS) performs the secondary action") and line 26 defines the browse-row Enter key only for the sidebar; the doc never names a main-panel browse secondary action. So BrowseList leaves a doc-promised key unbound rather than contradicting a specified main-panel behaviour. (4) 2.0 does better and the fix must not regress it: 1.x checked `e.ctrlKey` only (App.tsx:2292, 2663), so Cmd+Enter never worked on macOS; 2.0 Listbox maps `ctrlKey || metaKey` (Listbox.tsx:137, tested at Listbox.test.tsx:109-119). Wiring `onSecondaryActivate` in BrowseList gets Cmd for free — do not reintroduce a ctrl-only check. Also, 1.x installed `selectedVersion || pkg.version` (the version chosen in the details pane when any), so a faithful 2.0 wiring should install the details selection's version, not blindly `result.version`.
  - Reclassify MB5 from "parity gap, root cause feature-not-rendered" to "owner decision + doc wording": the main panel's key map (Enter = details, install from the details pane, no Ctrl+Enter) is the designed and documented 2.0 behaviour (b8a97a8:docs/ux/interaction-model.md:79-80; §5 Actions table; README.md:119-121), not a contradiction of the model. Three residual items for the fix phase, none a bug: (1) tighten §5 keyboard wording, which lost its "(sidebar ...)" scoping in 81ec41d: either "the main panel's lists have no secondary action" or define one; (2) if the design stands, add a CHANGELOG 2.0.0 Breaking-changes line that the 1.x main-panel Ctrl+Enter quick install (0457c75:README.md:15,78; App.tsx:3030) and the quick-search-dropdown Ctrl+Enter (App.tsx:2663) are gone; (3) if the owner reverses it, the ≤1-day wire is BrowseList.tsx:97 `onSecondaryActivate` → the details slice's install at the shown version, HINT at BrowseList.tsx:15, README.md:120, §5, plus a BrowseList.test.tsx case; gate `npm run test:frontend`. Split the button-hint sub-claim: `Install (Ctrl+Enter)` would be a false title in 2.0 (nothing to restore); `Uninstall (Del)` is low/cosmetic because Delete works and §5 puts key descriptions on the listbox hint, not on buttons. Note also that 1.x's own README (0457c75:README.md:97 "Enter | Install (Browse)") did not match its list handler (App.tsx:2348 returns on plain Enter), so 1.x is not a clean baseline for this key.
  - Keep MB5 at medium, but amend it: (1) Scope is wider — both 1.x main-panel Ctrl+Enter install paths are gone: the browse row (App.tsx:2292-2296,3030) and the quick-search dropdown quick install (App.tsx:2663,793-808; README.md:78). The claim's README:15 citation is the features bullet; :78 is the dropdown table. (2) Drop the 'contradicts interaction-model.md' framing — the model defines no secondary action for main-panel rows and lists Install only on the details button, sidebar row action and context menu (interaction-model.md:72,84-89); this is a 1.x parity gap only. (3) Split severity: shortcut loss = medium; missing tooltip hints = low on their own (Delete still works in 2.0 main Installed, InstalledProjectSection.tsx:109), and 'Install (Ctrl+Enter)' must not be added to the DetailsPane button until the key is wired or the tooltip is false. (4) Root cause is 'feature not wired', not 'not rendered', and it is not a one-line BrowseList change: the install decision (chosen version, cpm/preview, All Projects picker) lives in DetailsPane.tsx:135,220-229,255-266, so BrowseList's onSecondaryActivate needs a details-store action (or ref) that runs the pane's primary action; then update the BrowseList hint, the Install tooltip, README.md:120 and add a CHANGELOG line. Tests: BrowseList.test.tsx (Ctrl+Enter on a row runs the pane's primary action with the chosen version; no-op when primaryWhy is set), DetailsPane.test.tsx (tooltip once wired). Gate: npm run test:frontend. (5) Do-not-regress: 2.0 Updates rows update on plain Enter (UpdatesTab.tsx:174, AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:103), better than 1.x's Ctrl+Enter (UpdatesTab.tsx:647).

### MB6 — Quick-search dropdown lost Ctrl+Enter quick install and shows no loading row while suggestions are fetched

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: Ctrl+Enter on a highlighted suggestion installed its newest version (versions take 1 → installPackage) (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2663-2697,793-811); a 'Loading...' row appeared while suggestions were on their way (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2863-2867); 0457c75:README.md:71-78.
- 2.0: SearchBox options can only be picked, which runs an exact search (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.tsx:73-81,94-102; src/webview/main/features/search/useSearchTyping.ts:94); the slice's `suggesting` flag (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/search.tsx:38,201) is consumed nowhere (useSearchTyping.ts:100 returns suggestions only).
- Fix: SearchBox: new `onQuickInstall?(suggestion)` fired on Ctrl/Cmd+Enter with an active suggestion, and a `pending` prop that renders a non-selectable 'Searching…' option. useSearchTyping: expose `quickInstall(suggestion)` → `package/versions` ({ take: 1 }) → the shared install hook of MB5 into the committed single project; MainShell wires both.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/search/useSearchTyping.ts`, `src/webview/main/features/search/useSearchTyping.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `README.md`
- Test: SearchBox.test.tsx: 'Ctrl+Enter on the active suggestion calls onQuickInstall; a pending list shows Searching…'; useSearchTyping.test.tsx: 'quickInstall asks the newest version and installs it'.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/search src/webview/main/features/search && npm run lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; precise corrections for the fix phase: (1) Root cause is split across two layers, not only "feature-not-rendered": the slice stores `suggesting` (search.tsx:38,201); useSearchTyping.ts:41/100 drops it; and the shared primitive SearchBox has no loading prop and its `open` gate (SearchBox.tsx:63) hides the list when there are zero options — a fix must touch both useSearchTyping.ts/MainShell.tsx and SearchBox.tsx (shared with the sidebar, SidebarView.tsx:147). (2) The 1.x loading row was narrower than implied: it rendered only when `quickSearchLoading && quickSearchSuggestions.length === 0` (App.tsx:2863), i.e. during the first fetch for a dropdown session, not while re-fetching over already-shown suggestions. (3) This is a 1.x parity regression, not a contradiction of 2.0's own interaction model (interaction-model.md:26 specifies only suggestions while typing); it is also an undocumented drop — absent from CHANGELOG 2.0.0 Breaking changes (lines 12-16) although 1.x README.md:76 advertised it in bold. (4) Fix-shaping facts: `ops/install` takes optional `version` (origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/messages.ts:155; host passes it through at src/host/handlers.ts:59) and `addPackageArgs` omits `--version` when undefined (src/core/cli/commands.ts:75-77), so dotnet picks the latest stable — that would silently ignore the session's prerelease toggle, which 1.x honoured via `getPackageVersions … includePrerelease, take: 1`; the fix should resolve the newest version under `session.includePrerelease` first (or explicitly accept the behaviour change). It must also guard the All Projects sentinel (host rejects it: src/host/handlers.test.ts:321; 1.x gated on a truthy `selectedProjectRef.current`, App.tsx:2670) and respect the CPM `cpm/preview` rule (interaction-model.md:78) that 1.x's direct install never had. (5) Severity split: quick install lost = medium (documented shortcut gone); missing loading row = low (cosmetic feedback). Note 1.x's ArrowRight version picker (App.tsx:2658-2662, README:72) is a separate gap not covered by MB6.
  - Claim MB6 is partly right; split it. (1) Ctrl+Enter quick install: reclassify from bug to owner decision. 2.0 documents the new combobox keys (README.md:103-113, ARCHITECTURE.md:604-621) and reassigns Ctrl+Enter to the sidebar (README.md:120); the interaction model lists no install surface in the dropdown (§5) and its Ctrl+Enter rule is for listboxes, not the combobox popup. "Lost" overstates it: Ctrl+Enter is not a no-op but a pick (SearchBox.tsx:94-102) → exact search → Install in the details pane. What is missing is the record of the decision: add a CHANGELOG "Breaking changes" line (and optionally a README migration note) that the one-keystroke quick install from the suggestions list is gone; if the owner wants it back, that is a new feature request (SearchBox modifier handling + `package/versions` take-1 + `ops/install`), not a parity bug. (2) Loading row: stands as a feature-not-rendered gap, severity low (not medium): the slice flag `suggesting` was ordered by the P5.6 ruling and is tested but no consumer exists, so the popup simply stays closed until the answer arrives (150 ms debounce + round trip) and slow sources give no feedback. Fix (≤1 day): add a `pending?: boolean` prop to SearchBox that renders one non-selectable "Loading…" option (aria-disabled, excluded from `active` navigation) when `pending && suggestions.length === 0`; expose `suggesting` from useSearchTyping (return it alongside suggestions) and pass it from MainShell.tsx:191; style in search.css with tokens only. Tests: SearchBox.test.tsx "shows a loading option while suggestions are pending and no option is active" + axe; useSearchTyping.test.tsx asserts `suggesting` true between suggest and the host's answer. Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/search src/webview/main/features/search`.
  - Medium stands; sharpen scope and root cause rather than downgrade. (1) The claim understates the loss: the same 1.x dropdown also had ArrowRight -> version picker -> Enter installs the chosen version (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2658-2662, :2092, :2810-2850), gone in 2.0, and 1.x browse-result rows had Ctrl+Enter install (:2264-2294, :3030) that 2.0 BrowseList does not wire (`onSecondaryActivate` unused) — so the "pick, then Ctrl+Enter on the row" fallback does not exist either; the row half may belong to the browse-list claim, so reference it rather than fold it into MB6. (2) The `suggesting` gap is not merely an absent Loading row: because `suggest()` empties the slice before the request and SearchBox unmounts the list when empty, the dropdown closes during every debounced fetch and reopens on the answer, whereas 1.x kept stale suggestions visible (stale-while-revalidate) and showed Loading only when the list was empty. (3) Fix shape (<=1 day, not CSS): keep `groups` in the slice while `pending` (or expose `suggesting` and let SearchBox keep the list open with an `aria-busy` listbox and a disabled Loading option), add an `onQuickInstall(suggestion)` prop plus a Ctrl/Cmd+Enter branch to SearchBox, implement it in useSearchTyping via a `versions` request (`take: 1`, committed prerelease flag) followed by `ops/install` into the committed single project (no-op under All Projects, matching 1.x), wire BrowseList `onSecondaryActivate` to install `result.version`; tests in SearchBox.test.tsx (Ctrl+Enter picks the quick-install path; list stays open with Loading while `suggesting`) and useSearchTyping.test.tsx (quick install sends versions then ops/install; suggestions persist across a second fetch); gate `npm run test:frontend`; add the removal/restoration to CHANGELOG `## [Unreleased]` and the README "Search box" key table.

### MB8 — Details 'Size' is never shown: nothing produces packageSize although README, CHANGELOG and interaction model §7 list it

The docs claim it anyway: origin/labs/2.0:README.md:13, CHANGELOG.md:28, docs/ux/interaction-model.md:119-120.
- Fix: In `fromSource`, inside the `filterAllowedSources` loop (rule in src/core/feeds/STATUS.md:235), when `source.endpoints.packageBaseAddress` is set, run `flat.packageSize(source, id, version, signal)` in parallel with the registration call and set `metadata.packageSize` when > 0. `PackageMetadata.packageSize` already exists (src/protocol/domain.ts:128): no contract change.
- Files: `src/core/feeds/metadataResolver.ts`, `src/core/feeds/metadataResolver.test.ts`
- Test: metadataResolver.test.ts: 'fills packageSize from the flat container of the source that answered' and 'leaves packageSize absent when the HEAD fails'.

### MB9 — Details drop the facts the row already knew: Downloads for nuget.org packages, the Verified badge, and description/authors when no source has metadata

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `store-not-kept` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: Downloads came from the search row (`searchResult?.totalDownloads || packageMetadata?.totalDownloads`), the Verified badge sat beside Author(s), and description/authors fell back to the row (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:423,432-436,450-454; 0457c75:CHANGELOG.md:748 'Verified Badge in Package Details Panel').
- 2.0: DetailsInfo reads `metadata` alone (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsInfo.tsx:25-42,73-76,86). Registration metadata carries no `totalDownloads` (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/registration.ts:85-104; only the search fallback sets it, src/core/feeds/metadataResolver.ts:35-39), so nuget.org details show no Downloads fact. `PackageMetadata` has no `verified` (origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/domain.ts:116-134) and `DetailsSelection` carries no row facts (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx:9-19), so the badge cannot render; `metadata === null` shows an EmptyState although the row had a description. Contradicts docs/ux/interaction-model.md:119 ('Details (description, authors, downloads …)') for the common nuget.org case.
- Fix: Add optional `known?: { verified?: boolean; totalDownloads?: number; authors?: string; description?: string; iconUrl?: string }` to `DetailsSelection` (not a frozen file) and fill it at every `select()` from the row (PackageSearchResult; PackageUpdate/InstalledPackage already carry verified/authors/iconUrl — one-line changes in the Installed/Updates call sites of the other area). DetailsInfo: Authors fact with `<VerifiedIcon>` plus visually hidden 'Verified' when `known.verified`; Downloads = `metadata.totalDownloads ?? known.totalDownloads`; description/authors fallback; when metadata is null render the known facts above the 'No package source has details' note instead of a bare EmptyState.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/details.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsInfo.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.test.tsx`
- Test: DetailsPane.test.tsx: 'the Details tab shows the row's downloads and Verified badge when the registration has none' and 'without metadata the Details tab still shows the row's description'; details.test.tsx: 'select keeps the row facts and clears them with the selection'.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/details src/webview/shared/store && npm run lint`

### MB10 — README typography lost: headings, inline code, tables, blockquotes, lists and horizontal rules are unstyled

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `css` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: 124 `.readme-rendered` rules: accent-bordered h1-h3 with sizes, h4-h6, paragraph/list spacing, inline `code` chip, bordered zebra tables with styled headers, blockquote, hr, image radius (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:1781-2202).
- 2.0: 28 `.nuiget-readme*` rules covering only `img`, `pre`, the copy button and hljs token colours (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/main.css:303-382); headings, inline code, tables, blockquotes, lists and hr fall back to browser defaults inside the editor font.
- Fix: Port the 1.x README rules under `.nuiget-readme` using tokens only: em-based h1-h6 (`--nuiget-font-size-*`, borders in `--nuiget-color-link`/`--nuiget-color-border`), inline `code` chip on `--nuiget-color-code-bg`, tables with `--nuiget-color-border` and a new `--nuiget-color-table-header-bg: var(--vscode-keybindingTable-headerBackground, var(--vscode-editor-inactiveSelectionBackground))`, blockquote with new `--nuiget-color-blockquote-bg: var(--vscode-textBlockQuote-background)`, hr, list and paragraph spacing. Add any new token to tokens.css (and to the REQUIRED list if the primitives rely on it). No hex, no px font-size, no !important, no Chromium-115+ feature.
- Files: `src/webview/main/main.css`, `src/webview/shared/tokens.css`, `src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts`, `src/webview/main/features/details/markdown.test.ts`
- Test: Lint suite green: src/test/lint/webview-colours.test.ts, webview-stylesheets.test.ts, webview-css-baseline.test.ts, webview-tokens.test.ts; markdown.test.ts 'supports GFM tables' and friends unchanged; theme.ui.test.ts unchanged.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project backend src/test/lint && npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/details && npm run lint`

### MB13 — Hover prefetch of versions and metadata removed; every row selection waits for two round trips

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: A 150 ms hover dwell prefetched the versions list (take 20) and, when the version was known, the metadata into frontend LRU caches, with pending-dedupe and a backend prefetch slot (0457c75:src/webview/app/hooks/useHoverPrefetch.ts:35-102; 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:3057-3058; 0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:966-1000; 0457c75:CHANGELOG.md:78).
- 2.0: No pointer handling on browse rows (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:104-118; Row.tsx:57-116 exposes no pointer props); the details slice asks `package/versions` and `package/metadata` only after `select()` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx:176-191). Note: `package/prewarm` (messages.ts:143) warms credentials and service indexes only (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:529-539) and no webview calls it; it is unrelated dead surface, not a prefetch.
- Fix: Hook: on pointer enter start a 150 ms timer, then `bus.request('package/versions', { packageId, sourceUrl, includePrerelease })` and `bus.request('package/metadata', { packageId, version, sourceUrl })` under one AbortController; abort on leave/unmount; no webview cache needed because the coordinator dedupes per (key, generation) (docs/adr/002-query-coordinator.md:18), so the click's request joins the in-flight one. Row gets `onPointerEnter`/`onPointerLeave` passthrough on the presentational wrapper.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/search/useHoverPrefetch.ts`, `src/webview/main/features/search/useHoverPrefetch.test.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx`
- Test: useHoverPrefetch.test.tsx: 'asks versions and metadata 150 ms after the pointer enters and aborts on leave'; Row.test.tsx still 'the row wrapper carries no aria attribute and no tabindex'.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/search src/webview/shared/primitives && npm run lint`

### MB14 — README copy button is a text-only language label with no copy icon and no 'copied' feedback

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.25 d
- 1.18.9: 1.x intended a header button with copy/check icons, a language label and a 2 s `copied` state (0457c75:src/webview/app/markdownSetup.ts:104-115; 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:1750-1762), but its `<button>` is forbidden by its own sanitizer (markdownSetup.ts:171), so it never survived rendering — origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/details/markdown.ts:3-5 records exactly this. Not runtime-verified; treat this item as an improvement, not a regression.
- 2.0: The button carries only the language name and the click writes the clipboard without any visual confirmation (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/details/markdown.ts:105-122; ReadmeView.tsx:34-39).
- Fix: Add a pure `CopyIcon` to icons.tsx; `addCopyButtons` renders icon + label into the button via DOM; ReadmeView sets `data-copied` on the clicked button for 2 s (swap to CheckIcon via CSS) and falls back to `ui/copyToClipboard` when `navigator.clipboard` is unavailable.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/icons.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/details/markdown.ts`, `src/webview/main/features/details/ReadmeView.tsx`, `src/webview/main/main.css`, `src/webview/main/features/details/markdown.test.ts`, `src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.test.tsx`
- Test: DetailsPane.test.tsx: extend 'the README tab … copies code' to assert the copied state appears and clears; markdown.test.ts 'adds copy icon and language label to code blocks'; src/test/lint/webview-icons-pure.test.ts stays green.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/details && npx vitest run --project backend src/test/lint && npm run lint`

### MB15 — The details header (version selector, Install/Uninstall) scrolls away with long Details or README content

### MB16 — After the prerelease toggle the version selector stays on the previous version instead of the new newest; the list is unbounded

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.25 d
- 1.18.9: When the chosen version was the newest, the reloaded list's newest was selected (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:834-840); lists were asked with take 20 (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:1620-1626).
- 2.0: `version` changes only through `chooseVersion`; a reloaded list keeps it (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx:176-183,200-203); `package/versions` is asked without `take` (details.tsx:180) and the Catalog lists every version, unlisted included (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:457-461), so a dropdown can hold hundreds of entries.
- Fix: Track in DetailsProvider whether `version` equalled the previous list's first entry for the same selection; when a new list arrives (prerelease or invalidation), move to its first entry in that case. Keep the full list (the 1.x README promises unlisted versions are selectable) but consider a `take` of 50 with a 'Show all' option as a follow-up.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/details.test.tsx`
