### MB19 — Browse first-load shows skeleton rows without the status announcement that interaction model §4 requires

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.25 d
- 1.18.9: A visible 'Searching...' spinner with `aria-busy` and an aria-label (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:3005-3009).
- 2.0: Only the aria-hidden `Skeleton` is rendered while loading and `announce` runs on `ready` alone (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:67,75-77; DetailsInfo.tsx:67-69 and ReadmeView.tsx:41-43 likewise); docs/ux/interaction-model.md:58 defines first load as 'Skeleton rows (3) + role=status text "Loading …"'.
- Fix: Announce `Searching for "${query}"…` when the search status becomes `loading` (one effect keyed on status/query) and `Loading details…`/`Loading README…` when the details loadables enter `loading` for a new key; the LiveStatus region already exists in MainShell.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.tsx`
- Test: BrowseList.test.tsx: 'the first load announces Searching… and the answer announces the count'.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/search src/webview/main/features/details && npm run lint`

**Better in 2.0 (do not regress):**
- Browse rows show the package description and an 'Installed' badge with the installed version (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:109,114-115); 1.x browse rows showed neither (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:3067-3083). Keep both when fixing MB1.
- An unreachable source is a warning banner with Retry over the results, never 'no results' (BrowseList.tsx:84-86,98; src/webview/shared/store/search.tsx:27-28); 1.x showed 'No packages found'.
- Search failure is an inline error with Retry, and a repeated search (source/prerelease change, Retry) keeps the rows under a progress bar instead of blanking (BrowseList.tsx:78-87,94; search.tsx:30-31,221).
- The search box is a real combobox: prefixes and quick-search suggestions in one `role=listbox` with `aria-activedescendant`, source labels only when several sources answer, Escape closes/clears/leaves in order, axe-tested (src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.tsx:126-168; SearchBox.test.tsx:119).
- Browse results are a virtualized `role=listbox` with roving tabindex, Home/End, aria-posinset/setsize and an aria-describedby key hint (src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx; Row.tsx:80-94); 1.x used a focusable div with hand-rolled key handling (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2268-2367).
- Navigation from the sidebar ('View details') carries the version into the exact-match search and the details select that version (search.tsx:9-14; BrowseList.tsx:60-63; src/webview/main/features/search/useSearchTyping.ts:77-85).
- Stale-selection rule: a project switch keeps the details read-only with a banner until the new installed list confirms the package (src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx:136-157; DetailsPane.tsx:190); 1.x cleared the details (ledger U8).
- Version dropdown marks '(installed)' and keeps the chosen version visible with '(loading versions…)' instead of a disabled 'Loading...' select (DetailsPane.tsx:214-218 vs 0457c75 PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:241-244); empty list and local-source cases say why (DetailsPane.tsx:235-241); version errors are a Banner with Retry (:242).
- Install/Update/Downgrade decided by the host's order, 'Working…' while busy, and the disabled reason shown as a note (floating/range, unresolved, no single project, already installed) (DetailsPane.tsx:36-43,112-123,224-231).
- An install into a CPM project asks `cpm/preview` first and opens the central-update dialog; central rows explain the central version; uninstall reasons cover implicit, props-declared, unresolved and unsupported (DetailsPane.tsx:137-170,232-234; src/webview/shared/installed/rules.ts:25-39) vs 1.x implicit-only (0457c75 PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:231-232).
- Multi-install is a modal Dialog with focus trap and per-project notes (installed version, unsupported, centrally managed, not listed yet) (src/webview/main/features/details/MultiInstallDialog.tsx:94-135) instead of a backdrop dropdown (0457c75 PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:314-376).
- Details and README loading/error states: Skeleton, Banner with Retry, and an offline banner that says whether a source is down or no source knows the version (DetailsInfo.tsx:67-85; ReadmeView.tsx:41-48).
- README images are fetched host-side with the source credential, size cap, SSRF/public-host checks and `data:` delivery; `srcset` is stripped; http of known hosts upgraded; four at a time, aborted with the selection (src/webview/main/features/details/markdown.ts:84-93,131-170; src/core/feeds/readmeImage.ts:41-78).
- The README copy button is added after sanitizing, so it exists in the DOM (markdown.ts:101-122); 1.x injected a `<button>` that its own DOMPurify FORBID_TAGS removed (0457c75:src/webview/app/markdownSetup.ts:113,171) — not runtime-verified.
- Markdown renderer and highlight.js are built lazily and kept out of the sidebar bundle (markdown.ts:45-71).
- Every details request is correlated and cancellable: a late answer for another package or version is never shown, and invalidations re-ask only the matching keys (details.tsx:72-101,161-174); 1.x compared ids case-sensitively (ledger Y12).
- Facts are a semantic `<dl>`, vulnerabilities give severity in words plus icon, links are limited to http(s) (DetailsInfo.tsx:20-52,88-101).
- Row download counts are abbreviated (1.2M downloads) (BrowseList.tsx:17-25).

**Notes:**
- Read-only audit of two git refs; nothing was rendered or run (Node not on PATH). Flagged as not runtime-verified: the overflow behaviour of the nowrap description (MB1), the 1.x copy-button sanitizer claim (MB14), double-encoded descriptions (MB17), and the actual latency cost of MB3.
- Owner's 'no package icons' observation: for Browse, icon sourcing is at parity (both read the flat-container /icon; 1.x 0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:790-801,2234-2238; 2.0 src/core/feeds/icons.ts:199-201), so missing icons there are packages without a flat-container icon plus the missing default (MB2); icons on Installed/Updates rows are another area's gap.
- MB1 changes the shared Row primitive, which also renders Installed, Updates, transitive and sidebar rows: the compact density must stay byte-identical; regression guards are Row.test.tsx, composed.test.tsx, Listbox.test.tsx, Listbox.bench.ts and the sidebar tests.
- MB3 step 2 and the `search/metadata` alternative are CONTRACT CHANGES: src/protocol/messages.ts is frozen (docs/plans/RUN.md:75); the run protocol requires the `.claude/.contract-change` sentinel, and docs/protocol.md must be regenerated (`npm run docs:protocol`, checked by `npm run docs:check`). Step 1 (skip the lookup for rows that already carry verified/authors) needs no contract change.
- Doc contradictions found: interaction-model §1 (comfortable density) vs MB1; §3 scopes Multi Install to All Projects vs 1.x behaviour (MB4); §4 first-load announcement vs MB19; §7 lists downloads and size vs MB8/MB9; README.md:13 and CHANGELOG.md:28 claim size (MB8); ADR-006:18 promises a default icon (MB2).
- `package/prewarm` (messages.ts:143; Catalog.ts:529-539) is called by no 2.0 webview — dead protocol surface warming credentials/service indexes; it is not a replacement for hover prefetch (MB13).
- Lint constraints for any CSS fix (MB1, MB10, MB15): src/test/lint/webview-colours.test.ts (no colour literals outside tokens.css), webview-stylesheets.test.ts (no px font-size, no !important, no raw durations, `nuiget-` prefix), webview-css-baseline.test.ts (Chromium 114 denylist), webview-tokens.test.ts (REQUIRED token list).
- Deliberate 2.0 changes not listed as gaps: vulnerability severity shown in words + icon instead of per-severity colours (0457c75 App.css:178-224; ledger U6 closed); 'Package Details'/'Readme' tab labels now 'Details'/'README'; download counts abbreviated.
- Dropped after checking: a suspected 'external iconUrl fallback' gap — 1.x also rewrote feed iconUrls to the flat-container /icon, so 2.0's host-side fetch is equivalent.
- Shared fix dependency: MB5, MB6 and MB7 all install from outside DetailsPane and should reuse one `useInstallAction` hook extracted from DetailsPane.changeVersion so the CPM preview (N77) is never bypassed.
- The owner also reported a CI failure; CI logs were not examined in this area's task.

## Main panel shell: header/toolbar, tabs, layout (split/sash), unified search box, source settings dialog, tokens/theming (src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx, main.css, features/sources/**, shared/search/**, shared/primitives/{Tabs,Toolbar,Sash,Dialog,Popover}, shared/tokens.css, shared/primitives/primitives.css) compared with 1.x App.tsx header/search/tabs, SourceSettingsOverlay.tsx, DraggableSash.tsx, App.css

### MH1 — "Restore after operations" checkbox is gone from the main panel header

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Header rendered two checkboxes side by side: Restore after operations (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2411-2418, bound to restoreEnabled, persisted via saveSettings :1603-1612, synced from the sidebar by restoreChanged :1327-1333) and Include prerelease (:2419-2426).
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:160-179 renders project select, SourcesControl (select + gear, origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/sources/SourcesControl.tsx:34-46), ONE checkbox (Include prerelease, MainShell.tsx:174-177) and Refresh. The value exists and is shared: SessionSnapshot.restoreEnabled (origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/domain.ts:209), SessionIntent allows it (:214), the sidebar host toggles it (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/SidebarHost.ts:105-107) and package.json:215-224 exposes it only as sidebar view/title commands. Docs conflict: origin/labs/2.0:README.md:57-58 and CHANGELOG.md:41 describe the toggle as sidebar-only, while docs/ux/interaction-model.md:14-17 says both surfaces show "project, source, prerelease, restore toggle". Owner-confirmed regression ("a gear icon and a single checkbox").
- Fix: In MainShell header add a second .nuiget-main-toggle label before the prerelease one: <input type="checkbox" checked={session?.restoreEnabled ?? true} disabled={!hasSession} onChange={e => set({ restoreEnabled: e.target.checked })} /> Restore after operations. No new CSS (reuses .nuiget-main-toggle, main.css:47-52). Update README.md:57 ("shield button in the sidebar's title bar or the checkbox in the main panel header"), add a CHANGELOG [Unreleased] ### Fixed headline, and make interaction-model §1:16-17 say explicitly that the main header shows the prerelease and restore checkboxes. Do NOT land this silently against README:57: the owner should confirm the model's reading (both surfaces) wins.
- Files: `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx`, `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: MainShell.test.tsx: extend 'choosing a project or prerelease asks the host and shows the choice at once' (line 84) with the restore checkbox: unchecking sends session/set {restoreEnabled:false} once, the box shows unchecked before the answer, and a session/changed snapshot with restoreEnabled:true re-checks it. Optional harness test in src/test/harness: toggle in the main panel flips the nuiget.restoreEnabled context key the SidebarHost sets (SidebarHost.ts:36).
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx && npm run check-types && npm run lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; two precisions and three strengthening facts. (1) `CHANGELOG.md:41` does not say the toggle is sidebar-only — it describes the 2.0 header as "project, source and prerelease setting … (project and prerelease can be changed there)" and merely omits restore; only `README.md:57-58` explicitly locates the toggle in the sidebar title bar. (2) "side by side" is layout wording not verified against `App.css` (`.preview-checkbox` at `0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:1097`, `.header-selectors`); both labels are adjacent siblings at `App.tsx:2411-2426`, which is what matters. Add: (a) `origin/labs/2.0:package.json:166-173` hides `nuiget.sidebar.toggleRestore`/`toggleRestoreOff` from the Command Palette (`"when": "false"`), so without the sidebar there is NO path to change the setting — supports severity high. (b) `origin/labs/2.0:src/host/handlers.ts:191-193` applies `skipRestore: !restoreEnabled` to main-panel operations too, so the main panel runs under an invisible, uncontrollable setting. (c) `origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/STATUS.md:61` shows the P5.3 shell spec itself listed only a "prerelease checkbox"; no ADR/ruling dropped the main-panel restore toggle, and `ARCHITECTURE.md:533` ("shared context (interaction model §1)") conflicts with the code like `interaction-model.md:14` does. Fix is a ≤1-hour item: add a second `<label className="nuiget-main-toggle">` after `MainShell.tsx:177` with `checked={session?.restoreEnabled ?? true}` and `onChange={e => set({ restoreEnabled: e.target.checked })}` (SessionIntent and the session slice already accept it); add a MainShell.test.tsx case mirroring `:84-92` asserting `session/set` receives `{ restoreEnabled: false }` and the checkbox flips; update `README.md:57-58`, `CHANGELOG.md` Unreleased, `ARCHITECTURE.md:533` wording; gate `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main` then `npm test`.
  - The claim stands: the missing main-panel restore toggle is an undocumented deviation from the normative interaction model (§1:14), not an intended design decision; it is a bug/parity gap, not an owner decision. Three precisions: (1) The strongest counter-reading is interaction-model.md:16-17, which names only "main panel header selectors" for project/source and puts "source/prerelease/restore state" under the sidebar's title-bar icons; it cannot be read as excluding main-panel toggles because it would equally exclude the prerelease checkbox MainShell.tsx:174-177 does render, so sentence 1 (:14) governs. (2) README.md:57-59 and CHANGELOG.md:41 are post-hoc descriptions from P7.5c/P5.3, and README.md:54-55 in the same file contradicts them by saying the main panel shows the restore choice; the fix should also correct those lines. (3) "Owner-confirmed regression" rests on the harness-computed context ("a gear icon and a single checkbox"), not on the user's relayed words ("the design is completely broken"); keep the finding, soften the provenance. Proposed fix remains a ≤1-day render-only item: add a second `.nuiget-main-toggle` checkbox in MainShell.tsx bound to `session?.restoreEnabled` with `set({ restoreEnabled })`, a MainShell.test.tsx case mirroring :74-92 for the restore checkbox, a harness scenario `toggle('main','restoreEnabled')` (harness.ts:241 already supports it), update the MainShell JSDoc, CHANGELOG.md:41 and README.md:57-59; gate `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main` and `npx vitest run --project integration src/test/harness`.
  - Severity should be MEDIUM, not high. The gap exists exactly as described (feature-not-rendered; contradicts interaction-model.md:14), but a typical user would not notice: the toggle defaults ON, 2.0 resets it to ON, and the only users who care (those wanting to skip the ~1-3 s trailing restore) still have a working, documented control in the sidebar title bar that governs main-panel operations too. Nothing is broken; one of two entry points is gone. It is not LOW because the main panel now runs every operation under a switch it neither displays nor can change, and a skipped restore is announced as a plain success (outcomes.ts:41-42) — a behavioural blind spot, not styling. Two minor corrections to the claim text: CHANGELOG.md:41 is the main-shell entry listing the header as project/source/prerelease (it does not "describe the toggle as sidebar-only"; README.md:57-58 does); and "regression" should note the capability predates 1.18.5 as `nuiget.noRestore`, only the header UI dates from 1.18.5. The "trivial CSS" criterion is not met literally (it is JSX + binding + test), but the fix is ~10 lines: add a second `<label className="nuiget-main-toggle">` after MainShell.tsx:177 bound to session.restoreEnabled with `set({ restoreEnabled })`, disabled when !hasSession; add a MainShell.test.tsx case mirroring :84-92 asserting `session/set` receives `{ restoreEnabled: false }`; optionally a harness scenario (harness.ts Toggle already includes restoreEnabled); update CHANGELOG.md:41 and README.md:57-58 to name both surfaces. Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx`.

### MH2 — List/details split: default moved 35% to 50%, range narrowed 20-80 to 25-75, and the position is no longer remembered

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `store-not-kept` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: splitPosition default 35 (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:93), double-click reset to 35 (:2197), drag clamped 20-80 (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/DraggableSash.tsx:73-74), saved to globalState nuget.splitPosition on drag end (App.tsx:2198-2200; 0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:1263-1277) and requested at start (App.tsx:1432, :1383-1388).
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:57 SPLIT = {min 25, max 75, initial 50}; :75 useState only; :205 Sash has no onChangeEnd. The host already serves layout/get and layout/set over globalState nuiget.layout.splitPosition (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/handlers.ts:241-254; protocol origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/messages.ts:115-116, domain.ts:333-338) but no webview file calls them (git grep 'layout/' origin/labs/2.0 -- src/webview: no match). The 2.0 sidebar does remember its split via getState/setState (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx:15-46, :190). CHANGELOG.md:16 only says the 1.x position is reset once, not that the panel never remembers it. A 50% list also makes the details pane a third narrower than 1.x by default (owner: "details panel is sparser").
- Fix: MainShell: on mount bus.request('layout/get', {}) and setSplit(clamp(result.splitPosition)) when present; pass onChangeEnd={value => bus.request('layout/set', { splitPosition: value }).catch(() => undefined)} to the Sash. Owner decision: restore the 1.x default (35) and range (20-80) or keep 50/25-75; if changed, update SPLIT and the assertion '50%' in src/test/ui/v2/main-shell.ui.test.ts:44-45. Sash.tsx needs no change (onChangeEnd exists, Sash.tsx:22).
- Files: `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx`, `src/test/ui/v2/main-shell.ui.test.ts`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Sash.tsx`
- Test: MainShell.test.tsx with the bus fake (shared/bus/testSupport.ts): layout/get answering {splitPosition:40} yields --nuiget-split: 40% on .nuiget-main-split; a keyboard ArrowRight on the sash sends layout/set with the new value exactly once; an absent/invalid value keeps the default.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx src/webview/shared/primitives/Sash.test.tsx && npm run check-types; npm run test:ui (env-dependent, main-shell.ui.test.ts)`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Two precision fixes. (1) Arithmetic: 1.x details pane defaults to 100-35 = 65% of the panel width, 2.0 to 100-50 = 50%; that is 15 points, (65-50)/65 ≈ 23% narrower (about a quarter), not "a third". (2) Scope of "no longer remembered": both refs create the panel with `retainContextWhenHidden: true` (`0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:115`, `origin/labs/2.0:src/host/MainPanelHost.ts:31`), so the split survives hiding and re-showing the tab in both versions; what 2.0 lost is survival across panel close/reopen, window reload and workspaces, which 1.x got from globalState. Root cause is sharper than "store-not-kept": host handlers, protocol types and the Sash primitive (`onChangeEnd`) are all in place; the gap is solely that `MainShell.tsx` neither requests `layout/get` on mount nor passes `onChangeEnd` → `layout/set`. The same is true of `sidebarSectionSplit` in `layout/*`: the sidebar persists through webview state instead, so the host's `layout/*` handlers are currently unused by both surfaces.
  - The claim stands but should be split by sub-claim. (1) Not remembered: a genuine regression that contradicts documented 2.0 intent (ADR-001:28-29, protocol `LayoutPreferences.splitPosition`, the P7.1 ruling), not an owner decision; root cause is accurately "store-not-kept" (webview never calls `layout/get`/`layout/set`; no `getState/setState` either, and no panel serializer exists so only `layout/*` gives 1.x parity). Fix: MainShell requests `layout/get` on mount and sends `layout/set { splitPosition }` from `Sash.onChangeEnd` (write-on-end, per the gotcha at .github/copilot-instructions.md:221), plus a MainShell test for restore/write; `MainShell.test.tsx:183` and `src/test/ui/v2/main-shell.ui.test.ts:44-45` pin `50%` and must move with any default change. (2) Default 35→50 and range 20-80→25-75: not documented in interaction-model.md or ADR-008; recorded only descriptively in ARCHITECTURE.md:547 after an unconsulted P5.3. This is an undocumented implementer choice the owner may ratify or revert; it is not a documented design decision, so it is not refuted, but its severity is arguably low (cosmetic) once persistence is restored. (3) Arithmetic: a 50 % list leaves the details pane at 50 % of the width versus 65 % in 1.x, about 23 % narrower (15 points), not "a third narrower".
  - Medium is right, but only for the persistence loss; three parts of the claim need tightening. (1) Arithmetic: "a third narrower" is wrong. The details pane goes from 65 % (1.x, App.tsx:3097 `width: 100 - 35`) to 50 % (main.css:98 default), which is ~23 % narrower (1.x was 30 % wider). The owner's "details panel is sparser" is about content, not pane width, and should not be borrowed here. (2) Scope: both versions set `retainContextWhenHidden: true` (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:115; origin/labs/2.0:src/host/MainPanelHost.ts:31) and neither registers a WebviewPanelSerializer (grep empty in both refs), so the 2.0 split survives hiding/showing the editor tab within a window and resets only on panel close/reopen, window reload and new windows; 1.x survived all of those via globalState. (3) Internal severity split: the default change 35→50 and the range change 20-80→25-75 are low on their own (documented at ARCHITECTURE.md:547, re-draggable, double-click/Enter reset to 50 at Sash.tsx:52-53,96); the fixer should not read MH2 as requiring a revert to 35. What 2.0 does better and the fix must keep: the Sash has keyboard operation (ArrowLeft/Right, Shift x10, Home/End, Enter reset) and full ARIA (role=separator, aria-valuenow/min/max, aria-controls) tested at MainShell.test.tsx:179-190, versus 1.x's pointer-only sash. Fix (half day): in MainShell.tsx call `layout/get` once after the session arrives and seed `split` from `splitPosition` clamped to SPLIT, pass `onChangeEnd={value => bus.request('layout/set', { splitPosition: value })}`; add a MainShell.test.tsx case that a stored 40 renders `--nuiget-split: 40%` and that End/drag end sends `layout/set { splitPosition: 75 }`; gate `npm run test:frontend`.

### MH3 — Installed tab lost its package-count badge and its vulnerability badge

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `primitive-lacks` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2952-2962: Installed tab shows a count badge (installedPackages.length) and, when vulnerable packages exist, a tab-badge-vuln with WarningIcon, count, severity colour class and tooltip; Updates tab shows its count (:2995). Styles 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:696-738 (severity colours from --vscode-charts-*).
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:135 adds count to the Updates tab only; origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Tabs.tsx:5-11 TabItem carries a single count?: number and renders it as a neutral Badge (:68). Interaction model §2:25 says 'Tabs Installed / Updates (with badge)'. Data is in the store: installed slice result: InstalledResult with packages[] (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/installed.tsx:16; InstalledTab.tsx:186 reads section.result?.packages), InstalledPackage.vulnerabilities (domain.ts:66) and metadata PackageMetadataLite.vulnerabilities (installed.tsx:18, domain.ts:129); Badge has tone 'warning'|'error' (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Badge.tsx:4-12).
- Fix: Tabs.tsx: extend TabItem with badges?: ReadonlyArray<{ label: string; title?: string; tone?: BadgeTone }> (keep count as sugar for one neutral badge) and render each with <Badge>; the name stays 'Installed 12 2 vulnerable' via Badge title. MainShell: installedCount = single project ? installedSlice.result?.packages.length : sum over useAllProjects().installed chunks; vulnerableCount = packages whose vulnerabilities?.length > 0 (fall back to metadata map); tone 'error' for Critical/High, 'warning' otherwise. Mention the Installed badges in interaction-model §2:25.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/Tabs.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Tabs.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: Tabs.test.tsx: a tab with two badges renders both and its accessible name includes both titles; axe clean. MainShell.test.tsx: an installed result with 3 packages, one with a High vulnerability, shows '3' and '1' badges on the Installed tab; an unresolved/loading result shows none.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/primitives/Tabs.test.tsx src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx && npx vitest run src/test/lint/webview-colours.test.ts && npm run check-types`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands (gap is real, medium severity), with three precision corrections. (1) Root cause is split, not solely 'primitive-lacks': the installed COUNT badge is 'not rendered by feature' — `TabItem.count` already exists and Tabs.tsx:68 renders it; MainShell.tsx:135 simply never computes/passes an installed count (available as `installedSlice.result?.packages.length`, installed.tsx:16). Only the severity-toned VULNERABILITY badge needs a primitive extension (TabItem has no tone/icon/second-badge slot; Badge tones are border-only, no per-severity colour). (2) The data-path citation `InstalledPackage.vulnerabilities (domain.ts:66)` is a type field the 2.0 host never populates on `installed/get`: `git grep vulnerabilities origin/labs/2.0 -- src/core/projects src/host` (excluding tests) returns nothing, and handlers.ts:265-269 passes `catalog.installed(...)` through unchanged. Installed-row vulnerability data arrives via `transitive/metadata` → `PackageMetadataLite.vulnerabilities` (domain.ts:189+, Catalog.ts:282, :514-515) into `installedSlice.metadata` (installed.tsx:18, asked for all shown rows at installed.tsx:62-80), consumed per-row at InstalledProjectSection.tsx:42 `isVulnerable`. A fix must derive the tab count from `installedSlice.metadata` + `result.packages`, not from `InstalledPackage.vulnerabilities`. (3) Partial mitigation exists and must not regress: 2.0 shows per-row 'Vulnerable' error-tone badges (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:131, TransitiveSection.tsx:79-80) and the `@vulnerable` filter (MainShell.tsx:55,136) — row-level, not the tab-level aggregate, so the gap stands. Scope notes: interaction-model.md:25 '(with badge)' does not say which tab, so cite as weak support rather than a contradiction; in 1.x the installed count badge was single-project only (NuGetPanel.ts:305 breaks on ALL_PROJECTS_SENTINEL, so `installedPackages` is empty in all-projects mode) — parity scope is single-project, an all-projects count would be new behaviour.
  - Claim stands (medium, not an intended design decision), with three corrections: (a) Root cause is only half "primitive-lacks". The COUNT badge is a shell omission: `TabItem.count` already exists (Tabs.tsx:8) and MainShell already holds `installedSlice` (MainShell.tsx:73; `installedSlice.result` used at :95), but :135 only adds `count` to `updates`. Only the VULNERABILITY badge is a primitive gap (Tabs.tsx:68 renders a single neutral count; no tone, icon or second-badge slot). (b) The data source differs from 1.x: the 2.0 host never populates `InstalledPackage.vulnerabilities` (domain.ts:66) — vulnerabilities are enriched onto `PackageMetadataLite` (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/enricher.ts:57,72; src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:514-515; feedClients.ts:45) and the Installed feature reads them from the metadata map (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:41-42, installed.tsx:18). A fix must count from `installedSlice.metadata` (and the allProjects slice's metadata in All Projects), and honour the documented "unknown is not empty" rule (enricher.ts:4-5; InstalledProjectSection.tsx:49-51): no vuln badge until metadata has arrived. (c) Two documented 2.0 constraints bound the fix: the Installed count should follow the same "only when the answer is known" rule as the Updates count (ARCHITECTURE.md:579-580; MainShell.tsx:130; InstalledProjectSection.tsx:170 uses `result !== undefined`), and badge tones colour the border only for contrast (origin/labs/2.0:ARCHITECTURE.md:241-242), so 1.x's `color-mix` severity fills (App.css:720-738) must not be ported as-is — extend `TabItem` with an optional second badge `{ label, tone, title }` and render severity via tone + the visually-hidden title. Proposed ≤1-day item: MainShell.tsx (installed count + vuln badge from metadata), Tabs.tsx (secondary badge slot), Tabs.test.tsx + MainShell.test.tsx (count present once `result` defined; absent while loading; vuln badge absent with empty metadata, present with one vulnerable id), interaction-model §2 wording "(with count badge; Installed also a vulnerability badge)", gate `npm test`.
  - MH3 is partly right but bundles two gaps of different severity and misstates the root cause for one of them. Split it:

MH3a (LOW, not medium): Installed tab count badge. Root cause is feature-not-rendered, not primitive-lacks — `TabItem.count` exists (Tabs.tsx:9) and MainShell already has `installedSlice` in scope (MainShell.tsx:72, used only at :91). The count still shows in the sidebar Installed header and in All Projects project headers; only the single-project main panel lost it. Fix: one expression in MainShell.tsx:135 reusing the rule at InstalledProjectSection.tsx:170 (`undefined` while result undefined or unsupported-and-empty, otherwise `result.packages.length`), one assertion in MainShell.test.tsx (`getByRole('tab', { name: 'Installed 2' })` after the installed/get response). Gate: `npm run test:frontend`. This is trivial TSX (not CSS), so it does not merit medium.

MH3b (MEDIUM stands): aggregate vulnerability badge on the Installed tab. Root cause is primitive-lacks (single neutral `count`; no second-badge/tone/icon slot; Badge tones colour only the border per primitives.css:275-290, so 1.x's four-step severity colour cannot be reproduced without a primitive change) plus feature-not-rendered. It is "clearly worse" for a user who has vulnerable packages and sits on the Updates tab or has a long list; invisible to everyone else, so not high. Correct the claim's data path: compute from `installed.metadata`/`all.metadata` (`PackageMetadataLite.vulnerabilities`), not `InstalledPackage.vulnerabilities`, which the 2.0 host never populates; the badge is therefore eventually consistent (arrives after the list) and must show no number while metadata is unknown or absent (sourcesUnavailable), mirroring the Updates badge's `undefined`-while-unknown rule. Fix (≤ half day): extend `TabItem` with an optional `extra?: React.ReactNode` (or `badges`) rendered after the count in Tabs.tsx:68; in MainShell derive `vulnerableCount` from the metadata map (single project: `installedSlice.metadata`; All Projects: union over `all.metadata`) and pass `<Badge label={String(n)} tone="error" title="N packages with known vulnerabilities" />`; test in MainShell.test.tsx driving the `transitive/metadata` response (pattern InstalledTab.test.tsx:236) and asserting the tab's accessible name. Gate: `npm run test:frontend`, `npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui` for the tab-bar testids.

Drop the interaction-model contradiction: §2:25 "(with badge)" does not say which tab.

2.0 does better, protect in the fix: `@vulnerable` with no metadata yet says "Vulnerability data is not available for this project yet." (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:49-51) where 1.x printed "No vulnerable packages found" regardless of enrichment (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:1258-1260); and 2.0's metadata is live per source (Catalog.ts:514-515) rather than a 1h-TTL enrichment. Any aggregate badge must keep "no number while unknown".

### MH4 — Unreachable sources are no longer marked in the header source selector

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2454-2461 prefixes failed sources with ⚠ in the source <select>; :2474-2486 renders a source-warning-indicator button ('N source(s) unreachable. Click to refresh') that posts refreshSources; icon colour App.css:3405-3407.
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/sources/SourcesControl.tsx:22 destructures only sources, status, enable; the slice exposes failed: readonly FailedSource[] fed by the sources answer, its health chunk and sources/health events (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/sources.tsx:10-11, :67, :88, :135) but nothing in the header reads it. README.md:47-49 relocates the signal to banners that exist only inside the Updates and browse lists (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx:214, features/search/BrowseList.tsx:85), so on the Installed tab a dead feed is invisible.
- Fix: SourcesControl: const { failed, refresh } = useSources(); mark each failed option (text suffix ' (unreachable)' and title with the error) and, when failed.length > 0, render <IconButton label={`${failed.length} sources unreachable. Retry`} data-testid="source-health-indicator" onClick={refresh}><WarningIcon /></IconButton> next to the gear, coloured var(--nuiget-color-warning) in main.css. Add the test id to interaction-model §8 (it must be rendered or src/test/lint/interaction-model-testids.test.ts fails).
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/sources/SourcesControl.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/sources/SourcesControl.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/main.css`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: SourcesControl.test.tsx: a sources answer with failed:[{url,error}] shows the suffix on that option and the indicator; clicking it calls sources/list again; a sources/health event with failed:[] removes it; axe clean.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/sources && npx vitest run src/test/lint/interaction-model-testids.test.ts && npm run check-types`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands (medium, feature-not-rendered) with these precision fixes. (1) 'nothing reads failed' overstates: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsInfo.tsx:63 destructures failed and uses it at :75 (empty-state text 'No details: a package source could not be reached.') and :81-83 (offline banner wording). Nothing in the header or any Installed list reads it, so the conclusion holds. (2) 'banners exist only inside the Updates and browse lists' undercounts: also AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:300 (updates-unavailable-banner), sidebar UpdatesSection.tsx:119,146 and sidebar BrowseResults.tsx:135; all are per-answer sourcesUnavailable, none driven by live sources/health. (3) This is a 1.x-parity gap, not a contradiction of docs/ux/interaction-model.md: line 67 deliberately specifies the banner model. One nuance: that line places the banner 'under the search box', while MainShell.tsx:196 renders only projectsError there and the health banners sit inside tab content, so a shell-level banner fed by useSources().failed would satisfy both the doc's placement and 1.x parity. (4) The data layer is better in 2.0, not worse: extension.ts:239-242 pushes sources/health live from the monitor on every pass, whereas 1.x refreshed the indicator only on getSources/refreshSources/source mutations (NuGetPanel.ts:722-727, 746-752). The fix is render-only: SourcesControl (or MainShell) reads useSources().failed and calls the already-exported refresh (used at SourceSettingsDialog.tsx:189; handlers.ts:333-338 invalidates sources and re-lists with catalog.sourceHealth()); do not rebuild plumbing. Add a SourcesControl.test.tsx case responding failed: [{url,error}] and asserting a marker plus a sources/refresh request on click. (5) Minor: 1.x's indicator is a <span role="button">, not a <button>. Severity medium is right: Updates/browse do warn when the answer says so; only the header and the Installed tab are blind.
  - The claim stands: the loss of the header health marker is a regression, not a documented design decision. Neither docs/ux/interaction-model.md nor docs/adr/008 on origin/labs/2.0 records dropping the per-source '⚠' prefix or the 'N source(s) unreachable. Click to refresh' control; §4 only defines a sourcesUnavailable banner for updates/search, and 2.0 even violates that row's placement ('under the search box') by rendering it inside UpdatesTab/AllProjectsUpdates/BrowseList, so the Installed tab shows no signal. CHANGELOG.md:37 ('the source list marks the failed ones') records an intent no 2.0 UI delivers. Precision fixes to the claim's wording: (1) `failed` is not dead data — DetailsInfo.tsx:63 reads it for the offline banner/empty-state copy; the claim is right only that nothing in the header reads it. (2) Banners do not exist 'only' in Updates and browse: AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:84 and the sidebar (sidebar-updates-unavailable, sidebar-browse-unavailable per §8) also show them; the 'Installed tab is blind' point is unaffected. (3) MainShell.tsx:133-134 suppresses the Updates tab count when sourcesUnavailable (no number rather than a wrong one) — a silent partial mitigation the fix must not regress. (4) §4's scoping of the banner to updates/search means the absence of a banner in the Installed *list* is consistent with the doc; the gap is the header (shell-level, tab-independent) indicator. Fix remains ≤1 day: read `failed` in SourcesControl.tsx (option prefix/marker + an IconButton 'N source(s) unreachable' that calls useSources().refresh() or requests sources/refresh), add the hook to §8, extend SourcesControl.test.tsx with a `failed: [SHARED]` response; keep severity medium.
  - Keep MH4 at medium, but amend the claim in four ways. (1) Reframe: 2.0 does NOT contradict docs/ux/interaction-model.md — §4:67 specifies 'Warning banner under the search box with Retry' keyed on sourcesUnavailable and §9:155 lists the four *-unavailable test ids; the code follows its own design. MH4 is a 1.x-parity gap only, and the fix must also amend interaction-model.md §1/§4 (and README.md:47-49) to add the header marker. (2) State the real loss precisely: 2.0 drops WHICH source failed (FailedSource has url+error but no banner names it), not merely a persistent icon. (3) Soften 'invisible on Installed tab' to 'no labelled signal' — MainShell.tsx:134 blanks the Updates badge count. (4) Record that 1.x's indicator was a one-shot snapshot that never cleared on recovery (no monitor→webview push), so the fix should be built on the live 2.0 slice, not a port of 1.x. Side note for the fix phase: CHANGELOG.md:37 ('the source list marks the failed ones') is true only at protocol level (sources/list carries `failed`, STATUS.md:73); either reword it or make SourceSettingsDialog mark failed rows with the same ~5-line change.

### MH5 — Comfortable-row density/typography tokens are flatter than 1.x (the CSS half of 'rows are plain text')

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `css` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:70-79 .package-item: padding 12px 16px, gap 12px, 1px bottom separator (:75); selected row 3px left accent (:85-90); icon 32px (:92-104); name 14px/600 (:112-116); version 12px (:118-121); meta line 11px (:123-128). Shell type: header h2 14px/600 (:38), tab 13px (:650) active 500 (:666), search input 13px (:800), selectors 13px (:747).
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/tokens.css:25-32 row-height-comfortable 44px, icon-lg 24px; :49-52 font-sm 0.85em / md 1em / lg 1.15em (about 11/13/15px at VS Code's 13px). origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:150-154 .nuiget-row-name has no font-weight; :90-100 option padding 0 8px, gap 6px, no separator, no selection accent; :143-148 title row; main.css:200-204 browse icon = icon-lg (24px). Even after the lists area wires icon/authors/description into Row, the row stays visibly lighter than 1.x. Constraint: src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts:69 ties the row-height tokens to Listbox's estimates, and webview-colours.test.ts forbids colour literals outside tokens.css. Depends on the lists area for data; this item is the styling.
- Fix: tokens.css: add --nuiget-font-weight-title: 600, --nuiget-icon-size-xl: 32px, and raise --nuiget-row-height-comfortable to 56px (update the Listbox estimate the tokens lint checks). primitives.css: .nuiget-row--comfortable .nuiget-row-name { font-weight: var(--nuiget-font-weight-title); } .nuiget-row--comfortable .nuiget-row-option { padding: var(--nuiget-space-5) var(--nuiget-space-6); gap: var(--nuiget-space-5); border-block-end: 1px solid var(--nuiget-color-border); } .nuiget-row-option[aria-selected="true"], .nuiget-listbox[aria-multiselectable="true"] .nuiget-row-option[aria-current="true"] { box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--nuiget-color-focus); } main.css .nuiget-browse-icon → icon-xl. Keep compact rows unchanged (sidebar). All values via tokens, no literals.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/tokens.css`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, `src/webview/main/main.css`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx`, `src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts`
- Test: Row.test.tsx: comfortable row has the title weight and separator classes; Listbox.bench and Listbox.test still pass with the new estimate; webview-tokens lint declares the new tokens; theme.ui.test.ts target-size unchanged or better.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/primitives && npx vitest run src/test/lint && npm run bench && npm run lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; four precisions for the fix item. (1) 'No selection accent' overstates: 2.0 keeps the selected-row background and foreground (primitives.css:106-111; multi-select variant :117-126). Only the 1.x 3px border-left + 13px padding-left (App.css:88-89) is missing; say 'no left-edge accent'. (2) Root cause is 'css + primitive', not css alone: the single-line layout comes from Row.tsx:98-108 putting .nuiget-row-title and .nuiget-row-desc side by side in an align-items:center flex row, with margin-inline-start:auto (primitives.css:182) and white-space:nowrap (:161). A 1.x-style stacked name / meta / authors row needs a Row.tsx markup change or comfortable-only wrapping rules, not token edits. Any change to --nuiget-row-height-comfortable must change ROW_HEIGHT in Listbox.tsx:8 in the same commit (webview-tokens.test.ts:69-77); Skeleton heights follow the token automatically (primitives.css:432-438); muted secondary text on a selected/current row must keep the color:inherit override pattern (primitives.css:164-175, theme.ui.test.ts contrast). (3) Shell typography is at parity in size, not flatter: tabs, select and input use font:inherit (primitives.css:672, main.css:40, :73), which is the same 13px as 1.x. The two real shell regressions are narrower: no panel heading at all (MainShell.tsx:160-179 vs 1.x .header h2 14px/600 at App.css:36-41) and no font-weight on the selected tab (primitives.css:682-685 vs App.css:666). (4) Two 1.x details should not be ported back: .package-authors used opacity 0.7 (App.css:562), which the repo's own guidance replaces with colour; and the 1.x verified mark was a coloured icon (App.css:565-574) whereas 2.0 renders a bordered text Badge with tone='info' (BrowseList.tsx:114, primitives.css:280-282) by design of the tone system. Measured size deltas for the item: icon 32px to 24px; name 14px/600 to 13px/400; row from ~3 stacked lines with 12px vertical padding and a 1px separator to one 44px centred line with no separator.
  - MH5 stands, with four corrections. (a) Carve out the typography-unit half: px→em is an intended, documented decision (ADR-008:34, U5, lint webview-stylesheets.test.ts:86), so the gap is not "sizes shrank from 14/12/11 px" but "the em scale and weights were never tuned to 1.x's hierarchy" (name had 600 weight and ~1.08em; 2.0 name is 1em/normal, .nuiget-row-name has no font-weight). The fix must stay in em tokens; font-weight is unconstrained and a --nuiget-font-weight-* token may be added. (b) The dominant root cause is layout, not token values: 2.0's comfortable row is a single 13 px line inside a 44 px min-height with the description right-aligned and clipped by nowrap (primitives.css:90-100, :143-148, :156-162, :177-183), whereas 1.x stacked name over a meta line (App.css:106-128, InstalledTab.tsx:193-232). The fix item should give the comfortable density a second line (description/authors under the title, badges beside it, icon 32 px via a new or retuned token) rather than only heavier text; the 44 px value is explicitly a "starting value ... may retune" (STATUS.md:1976), so retuning the comfortable token plus Listbox ROW_HEIGHT (webview-tokens.test.ts:69-77) is within the recorded intent. The 24 px compact row (sidebar) is ruled (STATUS.md:2136) and must not change. (c) Constraints the claim omitted: webview-stylesheets.test.ts:86 (no px font-size), :97 (every class .nuiget-), :101 (every cursor:pointer rule sets min-height: var(--nuiget-target-min)); webview-css-baseline.test.ts (Chromium 114 denylist); src/test/ui/v2/theme.ui.test.ts (axe color-contrast and target-size measured in VS Code — secondary text on a selected row must keep the row foreground, primitives.css:164-175). (d) Severity: as a CSS-only item after the lists area wires icon/authors/description, this is "clearly worse" (medium), not "broken" (high); the owner's "rows are plain text" is primarily the data gap. Also, the shell-type comparison is overstated: selectors and search input are font: inherit (13 px) in both versions; tabs differ only by the lost active weight; and the 1.x <h2>Manage NuGet packages</h2> (App.tsx:2409) is absent from the 2.0 header altogether (MainShell.tsx:160-180) — a removal that belongs to a header claim, not MH5.
  - Severity "high" stands only because of a mechanism the claim does not name; the mechanism it does name is medium on its own. Token values alone are a small delta: both surfaces run at VS Code's body font (1.x App.css:6 `font-size: var(--vscode-font-size)`), so name 14→13px and version 12→11px are 1px changes; the real typographic loss is `font-weight: 600`, plus the 1px separator and the 3px selected accent — trivial, scoped CSS (medium at most). What makes a user call the rows "plain text" even after icons/authors/description are wired is the Row primitive's single-line layout: `.nuiget-row-option` is one flex row, `.nuiget-row-desc` has `margin-inline-start: auto`, and `.nuiget-row-description` is nowrap with no truncation (primitives.css:90-100, :156-162, :177-183; Row.tsx:93-107). 1.x stacked name / version+meta / authors in a 66px block with a 32px icon. Rewrite MH5 as: root cause = CSS layout of the comfortable Row (one line, right-aligned untruncated description) first, token values (44 vs 66px, 24 vs 32px icon, no weight, no separator/accent) second; drop any "contradicts interaction-model.md" wording (the doc's only comfortable-row requirement, icons/description/badges, is met). Fix shape (≤1 day, but a primitive layout change, not a value tweak): under `.nuiget-row--comfortable` only, stack title over desc (likely a wrapper span in Row.tsx; update ARCHITECTURE.md:277-281 and Row.test.tsx), give the description `overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis` (or allow one wrapped line), add `font-weight: 600` to `.nuiget-row-name` in comfortable, a `--nuiget-color-border` bottom separator and a `--nuiget-color-focus` inline-start accent on `[aria-current]`/`[aria-selected]`, add `--nuiget-icon-size-xl: 32px` for `.nuiget-browse-icon`, and retune `--nuiget-row-height-comfortable` together with `ROW_HEIGHT.comfortable` in Listbox.tsx:8 (the virtualizer measures rows, so a taller row needs only the estimate kept in sync). Keep em font sizes (no px), no colour literals. Gates: `npm test` (webview-tokens, webview-stylesheets, webview-colours lint tests and Row/Listbox tests) and `npm run test:ui` (theme.ui.test.ts contrast/target-size, sidebar.ui.test.ts compact height). Order it after the lists-area data items; the overflow of long descriptions is a CSS-reading inference not verified at runtime and should be checked in the first ExTester screenshot.

### MH6 — High-contrast themes lose the bordered controls 1.x drew

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `css` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:3494-3541 under body.vscode-high-contrast(-light): 1px contrastBorder on tabs with contrastActiveBorder on the active tab, bordered .btn with 2px focus outline, bordered selects and text/password inputs, 2px outline on focused rows, 2px border on the sources modal, verified badge recoloured.
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/tokens.css:116-121 HC block remaps only --nuiget-color-border/focus/shadow. Controls that use no border token stay borderless in HC: .nuiget-button border 1px transparent (primitives.css:374), .nuiget-tab border 0 (:668), .nuiget-icon-button border 0 (:238); --nuiget-color-input-border falls back to transparent (:106) and is remapped only under forced-colors (:162), not under the HC body classes. theme.ui.test.ts:213 runs axe contrast/target-size per theme, which does not assert border presence. Not verified visually (no screenshots available).
- Fix: tokens.css :root add --nuiget-color-control-border: transparent; in the single HC rule (:116-121) set --nuiget-color-control-border: var(--vscode-contrastBorder) and --nuiget-color-input-border: var(--vscode-contrastBorder). primitives.css: .nuiget-button, .nuiget-tab, .nuiget-icon-button, .nuiget-badge borders → 1px solid var(--nuiget-color-control-border) (tab keeps its block-end selection line); .nuiget-dialog border already uses --nuiget-color-border. Keep one HC rule (webview-tokens.test.ts:93).
- Files: `src/webview/shared/tokens.css`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, `src/webview/main/main.css`, `src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts`, `src/test/ui/v2/theme.ui.test.ts`
- Test: webview-tokens lint: new token declared in :root and every colour an alias. theme.ui.test.ts: under the two HC themes assert getComputedStyle(tab).borderTopColor !== 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)' for a tab, a button and the select.
- Gate: `npx vitest run src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts src/test/lint/webview-colours.test.ts src/test/lint/webview-css-baseline.test.ts && npm run lint; npm run test:ui (env-dependent)`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Narrow MH6 to what survives: (a) `.nuiget-button` has no resting border in HC (primitives.css:374 literal `transparent`; 1.x App.css:3504-3506 drew 1px contrastBorder); (b) inactive tabs have no per-tab box in HC (primitives.css:668; 1.x :3494-3501), only the selected tab's 2px contrastActiveBorder underline and the tablist's 1px contrastBorder rule; (c) focus outlines and the dialog border are 1px where 1.x used 2px (primitives.css:225,581 vs App.css:3511,3529,3535); (d) the Verified badge is a text badge whose info-coloured border is not remapped in HC (no 1.x-style recolour, but the text is contrast-measured). Drop from the claim: selects, text/password inputs and the sidebar search (bordered in HC because VS Code defines `input.border`/`dropdown.border` = contrastBorder in both HC themes and exports `--vscode-input-border` to the webview, so the `transparent` fallback at tokens.css:106 never applies there); icon buttons (1.x `.toolbar-icon-btn` was borderless in HC too, App.css:472, outside the HC block); rows and dialog as "borderless" (both are drawn via the HC-remapped tokens, tokens.css:118-119). With inputs and selects at parity the residual reads low (cosmetic thickness plus resting button/tab edges), not medium. Fix hint (≤1 day): add `--nuiget-color-button-border: var(--vscode-button-border, transparent)` to tokens.css `:root` (VS Code registers `button.border` with default `contrastBorder`, so it is non-null exactly in HC) and use it on `.nuiget-button`; optionally remap `--nuiget-color-border` onto `.nuiget-tab` under the HC body classes and bump the focus/dialog widths to 2px there; extend `src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts` REQUIRED list and add a jsdom test asserting `.nuiget-button` resolves a border token; gate `npx vitest run src/test/lint src/webview/shared` and `npm run test:ui` (theme.ui.test.ts) — a border-presence assertion in theme.ui.test.ts via `getComputedStyle(...).borderColor !== 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)'` under the two HC themes would close the "not asserted" point.
  - Keep MH6 at medium but narrow and sharpen it. Scope: (1) `.nuiget-button` (all dialog/details/banner/empty-state buttons) has no border in either HC theme and, in Default High Contrast (dark), no visible fill either (button.background null on VS Code 1.85, black-on-black on 1.141; secondaryBackground null on both) — this item alone is arguably high; (2) `.nuiget-tab` loses the per-tab 1px contrastBorder box (tablist line, selected underline and focus outline remain); (3) sidebar `.nuiget-section-header`/`.nuiget-section-toggle` lose the 1px contrastBorder 1.x drew; (4) 2px→1px on button/row focus outlines and the dialog border (low). Remove from the claim: selects, text/password inputs and the sidebar search (bordered in HC because VS Code sets input.border/dropdown.border/widget.border to contrastBorder in hcDark/hcLight, so the token's `transparent` fallback never applies there), `.nuiget-icon-button` (1.x icon buttons were also borderless and uncovered by the HC block), and the dialog (bordered, 1px). Root cause (still CSS): not "the HC block remaps too few tokens" but (a) `.nuiget-button` uses a literal `transparent` border colour instead of a token, (b) no `--nuiget-color-button-border` token aliasing `--vscode-button-border` (contrastBorder in HC, null elsewhere — the exact 1.x `.btn-primary` pattern at App.css:1359), (c) `--nuiget-color-button-bg`/`-secondary-bg` have no fallback for HC dark where VS Code leaves them null, (d) `.nuiget-tab` and `.nuiget-section-toggle` set `border: 0`. Fix (≤1 day): add `--nuiget-color-button-border: var(--vscode-button-border, transparent)` to tokens.css `:root`, remap it to `var(--vscode-contrastBorder)` in the HC block and to `ButtonText` under forced-colors; give `--nuiget-color-button-bg`/`-secondary-bg` a `var(--vscode-editor-background)`-style fallback or an HC remap; use the new token in `.nuiget-button`; give `.nuiget-tab` and `.nuiget-section-toggle` a `1px solid transparent` border whose colour is `var(--nuiget-color-border)` inside the HC block? No — primitives.css may hold no HC block (STATUS.md:2045), so instead introduce `--nuiget-color-control-border: transparent` in `:root`, remapped to contrastBorder under the HC body classes and CanvasText under forced-colors, and use it on `.nuiget-button`, `.nuiget-tab`, `.nuiget-section-toggle`; add the names to REQUIRED in src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts. Test: extend src/test/ui/v2/theme.ui.test.ts under both HC themes to assert `getComputedStyle(button).borderColor` of a `.nuiget-button`, a `.nuiget-tab` and a `.nuiget-section-toggle` equals the computed `--vscode-contrastBorder` (and is not transparent), plus a jsdom unit test on primitives.css/tokens.css text for the token usage. Gate: `npx vitest run src/test/lint` and `npm run test:ui:v2` (theme file) green. Also fix the claim's wording: "controls that use no border token" should read "controls whose border colour is a literal or `border: 0`".
  - Keep MH6 at medium, but narrow and re-centre it. Severity medium rests on ONE item: `.nuiget-button` has no HC border (primitives.css:374) and, because VS Code's HC dark `button.background` is black and `button.secondaryBackground` is null, every resting action button in the main panel and its dialogs renders as bare text on the page background under Dark High Contrast (secondaries also under Light High Contrast). 1.x boxed them via `var(--vscode-button-border, transparent)` (App.css:1359,1369) plus the HC block. Downgrade to low: per-tab boxes (.nuiget-tab, :668) and sidebar section-header boxes (.nuiget-section-toggle, :339), since the active tab is still told by a 2px contrastActiveBorder underline and colour. Remove from the gap entirely: inputs and selects (VS Code emits `--vscode-input-border`/`--vscode-dropdown-border` = contrastBorder in both HC themes, so tokens.css:106's `transparent` fallback is never hit there), icon buttons (1.x never bordered them in HC), focus outlines and dialog borders (present at 1px, VS Code's own HC width). Path correction: primitives.css is src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css. Fix (≤1 hour, not ≤1 day): add `--nuiget-color-button-border: var(--vscode-button-border, transparent)` to the `:root` block of tokens.css and `border-color: var(--nuiget-color-button-border)` on `.nuiget-button`; no HC-block edit and no lint change needed (REQUIRED at webview-tokens.test.ts:26-37 is a minimum; the exact-match HC assertion at :96 stays valid because VS Code supplies the HC value). Optional low-severity follow-up: a `--nuiget-color-control-border` token (transparent; contrastBorder in the HC block, which then requires adding that key to the array at webview-tokens.test.ts:96) applied to .nuiget-tab and .nuiget-section-toggle. Gate: the lint vitest project (webview-tokens/webview-stylesheets) plus a stylesheet assertion that `.nuiget-button` declares the button-border token; theme.ui.test.ts will still not detect missing borders and should not be cited as the gate.

### MH7 — Header refresh button has no busy/disabled state and no revalidation bar under the search box

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d

The Installed/Updates tabs only mark rows aria-busy (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx:103, features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx:166); the bar exists only in BrowseList.tsx:83 and the sidebar (SidebarView.tsx:164).
