### MH13 — Chosen tab is not kept across a panel reload

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `store-not-kept` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:33 reads vscode.getState(); :42-47 initial tab from data-initial-tab or savedState.activeTab; :129-135 saves activeTab (and selections) with setState.
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:74 chosenTab is plain useState; origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/index.tsx:19 hands acquireVsCodeApi() to the bus only. The 2.0 sidebar keeps its layout through getState/setState (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx:15-46). Both versions use retainContextWhenHidden (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:115; origin/labs/2.0:src/host/MainPanelHost.ts:31) and neither registers a WebviewPanelSerializer, so the loss shows only when the panel is reopened.
- Fix: Mirror the sidebar: index.tsx passes a viewState store ({getState,setState} of the acquired API) to MainShell; MainShell reads { tab } at start (validated against 'installed'|'updates') and writes it in selectTab and on ui/selectProject. Combine with MH2 if the split is also kept locally as a fallback.
- Files: `src/webview/main/index.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx`
- Test: MainShell.test.tsx: a store returning { tab: 'updates' } starts on Updates; choosing Installed writes { tab: 'installed' }; an invalid value falls back to installed.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main && npm run check-types`

**Better in 2.0 (do not regress):**
- Shared context is host-owned with a revision guard and optimistic pending intents (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/session.tsx:26-48, :103-111); 1.x needed skipSave/skipSourceSave/skipProjectSave refs to break echo loops (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:187, :1320-1347). Keep the session slice as the single source for project/source/prerelease/restore when adding the restore checkbox (MH1).
- Search box is a real ARIA combobox shared by both surfaces: role=combobox, aria-activedescendant, listbox options, Enter/Tab pick, Escape closes → clears → blurs (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.tsx:126-149, :83-122); 1.x had hand-rolled divs with no roles (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2765-2806).
- Tabs primitive with tablist/tab/tabpanel roles, one tab stop, ArrowLeft/Right wrap, Home/End, and the badge count part of the tab name (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Tabs.tsx:30-46, :68); 1.x tabs were plain buttons without roles (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2921-2997).
- Sash is keyboard operable (role=separator, arrows, Shift x10, Home/End, Enter reset) and drags with pointer capture, no document listeners or body cursor mutation (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Sash.tsx:43-110) vs 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/DraggableSash.tsx:85-103.
- Dialog primitive: portal, focus trap, Escape, focus restore, stacked layers (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Dialog.tsx:47-95); the 1.x overlay had no focus management or dialog role (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/SourceSettingsOverlay.tsx:84-85).
- Sources dialog: shows the config file that declares each source (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/sources/SourceSettingsDialog.tsx:46), explicit opt-in checkbox for insecure HTTP (:139-147) instead of 1.x's silent allowInsecure (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/SourceSettingsOverlay.tsx:280), %ENV% placeholder never encrypted and explained (:65, :164-171), labelled fields with aria-invalid and role=alert errors (:137, :148), focus returns to Add source after Back (:202-207), in-dialog remove confirmation (:48-56).
- Theming: every colour is a token aliasing --vscode-*; one HC block, one forced-colors block, one reduced-motion block (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/tokens.css:116-176); lint tests forbid colour literals outside tokens.css and CSS newer than Chromium 114 (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/lint/webview-colours.test.ts, webview-css-baseline.test.ts, webview-tokens.test.ts); per-theme axe pass over both surfaces (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/theme.ui.test.ts:213). 1.x had 71 hard-coded colours (ADR-008) and light-theme hljs overrides (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:3274-3366).
- CSP: nonce-only scripts, img-src limited to the webview origin and data:, connect-src none (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/services.ts:111-118) vs the 1.x remote image allow-list (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:1403).
- Updates badge shows a number only when every source answered and none was unavailable (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:133-134), never a misleading 0.
- A selected source that is no longer in the enabled list stays visible in the selector (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/sources/SourcesControl.tsx:43); selectors are disabled until the session and list are known (:39, MainShell.tsx:166).
- One live region announces refresh outcome and every operation's outcome from either surface (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:100-108, :153, :210); perf marks webviewReady/firstUsefulRender (:82-97).
- Shell is 213 lines with features injected as render props (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:38-47) vs the 3220-line App.tsx; themed scrollbars including forced colours (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:20-44).
- Source settings open from the sidebar through ui/openSourceSettings (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/sources/SourcesControl.tsx:27) and navigation fills the box and searches exactly (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/useSearchTyping.ts:85), matching interaction-model §6.

**Notes:**
- Out of scope and not investigated: the CI failure the owner reported, and the data wiring of icons/authors/verified/description into Installed/Updates rows (lists area). MH5 covers only the CSS/density half of 'rows are plain text'; both halves are needed for the owner's complaint.
- MH1 has a documentation conflict the fix phase must resolve explicitly: origin/labs/2.0:README.md:57-58 and CHANGELOG.md:41 describe the restore toggle as sidebar-only; docs/ux/interaction-model.md:14-17 says both surfaces show it. The owner's observation treats its absence as a regression; the recommended reading is the interaction model's.
- Deliberate removals found in the record, listed as gaps so they are not re-litigated by accident: recent searches (CHANGELOG.md:17, legacyState.ts:15, protocol.test.ts:142-145) and the one-time reset of 1.x split/selection state (CHANGELOG.md:16). The split not being remembered at all (MH2) is NOT covered by that entry.
- Typography and density, 1.x px vs 2.0 tokens (default --vscode-font-size 13px): header title 14px/600 vs none; tab 13px (active 500) vs 1em/inherit; search input 13px vs 1em; selectors 13px vs 1em; row name 14px/600 vs 1em/normal; version 12px vs 0.85em (~11px); meta 11px vs 0.85em; row padding 12px 16px + 32px icon (~58px rows) vs 44px min-height + 24px icon; tab badge 11px pill (border-radius 10px) vs 0.85em badge radius 4px. Sources: 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:36-41,70-128,641-706,740-750,792-802; origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/tokens.css:24-52; primitives.css:86-100,150-162,262-274,662-675.
- Unverified (no VS Code or screenshots available in this read-only run): the visual result of the HC token gaps (MH6), whether Chromium renders a native search-cancel button inside the webview (MH8), and whether webview-tokens.test.ts:55 accepts a color-mix() value for a scrim token (MH12).
- Constraints for every CSS fix in this area: no colour literal outside tokens.css (src/test/lint/webview-colours.test.ts); no CSS newer than Chromium 114 (webview-css-baseline.test.ts, N64); tokens.css must stay custom-properties-only with one HC rule, one forced-colors rule and row-height tokens matching Listbox estimates (webview-tokens.test.ts:47-93); any new data-testid must be listed in interaction-model §8 AND rendered (src/test/lint/interaction-model-testids.test.ts; model :170-173).
- Existing tests that pin current shell behaviour and will need touching with the fixes: origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-shell.ui.test.ts:44-45 asserts the split is '50%'; MainShell.test.tsx:58 'renders the header, the search box, the tabs, the split and one live region' and :84 (prerelease intent) will gain the restore checkbox.
- Interaction model §4:59 ('subtle progress bar under the search box' while revalidating) is delivered only by BrowseList and the sidebar, not by MainShell for the Installed/Updates tabs (MH7). §2 (modes, @ dropdown, Escape behaviour) and §6 (openSourceSettings, navigateToPackage, refresh/all) are delivered by MainShell/SearchBox/SourcesControl as written.
- Gate commands available on origin/labs/2.0 (package.json:294-310): npm run check-types, npm run lint, npm test (vitest run), npm run test:frontend, npm run bench, npm run docs:check, npm run test:ui (ExTester, env-dependent; use --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui on macOS per copilot-instructions).

## sidebar

### SB1 — "Update all" button missing from the Updates section header

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: Updates header carried an ArrowUp action button "Update all packages (N)" when totalUpdateCount > 0 (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1910-1919); handleUpdateAll (…:1396-1426) posted bulkUpdatePackages (single project) or bulkUpdateAllProjects (All Projects).
- 2.0: UpdatesSection renders SectionHeader without `actions` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/UpdatesSection.tsx:170-179); `git grep -i 'bulk|update all|updateAll' origin/labs/2.0 -- src/webview/sidebar` hits only outcomes.test.ts. The host already serves ops/bulkUpdate and ops/bulkUpdateAllProjects (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/handlers.ts:394,396) and SectionHeader accepts `actions` (src/webview/shared/primitives/SectionHeader.tsx:23-24).
- Fix: In UpdatesSection pass `actions={count > 0 && !unsupported ? <IconButton label={`Update all (${count})`} data-testid="update-all-button"><ArrowUpIcon/></IconButton> : undefined}`. On click open the existing ConfirmDialog primitive listing the rows (interaction-model §5 requires a Dialog for bulk update); rows with declaration 'central' are listed as excluded (they need the host's CPM modal; route them through the actions menu instead). Confirm → useOpsActions().run('ops/bulkUpdate', { projectPath, packages: rows.map(u => ({ id: u.id, version: u.latestVersion, sourceUrl: u.sourceUrl })) }); in All Projects → 'ops/bulkUpdateAllProjects' built from all.updates.chunks. Announce the outcome via describeOutcome (already wired for bulk in outcomes.ts). Add `update-all-button` to the sidebar rows of §8 (it is listed for the main toolbar only).
- Files: `src/webview/sidebar/UpdatesSection.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/UpdatesSection.test.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: UpdatesSection.test.tsx: header shows the button only when count > 0 and the project is supported; click opens confirm-dialog listing every non-central row; confirm sends exactly one ops/bulkUpdate with id/latestVersion/sourceUrl per row; All Projects sends ops/bulkUpdateAllProjects; cancel sends nothing.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend && npx vitest run src/test/lint/interaction-model-testids.test.ts`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim is correct; two precision notes for the fix writer. (1) The claim's grep summary slightly understates the hits: `BrowseResults.test.tsx:166` also matches (`ops/bulkInstall`), but it is a test assertion of zero bulk installs, not an Update-all render — immaterial. (2) The 2.0 design docs never assign Update all to the sidebar, so this may be a scoping decision rather than an oversight: origin/labs/2.0:docs/ux/interaction-model.md:77 places "Bulk update / bulk uninstall" on the "Updates/Installed toolbar" with an "In-panel Dialog" (main-panel constructs; §1 line 12 gives the sidebar "quick actions" and QuickPicks), ARCHITECTURE.md:594-596 describes Update all for the main panel's All Projects view and :598-602 describes the sidebar `UpdatesSection` without it, and plan row P5.10 (docs/plans/2026-10-11-nuiget-2.0-execution-plan.md:283, "Sidebar parity: Updates section, split sash, browse, context menu, title-bar toggles, Open full view") does not name it, while P5.5 (:278, "Feature `updates` (main + sidebar section): badge, Update All dialog") is ambiguous. The fix item should therefore include a docs decision (interaction-model §1/§5 + ARCHITECTURE sidebar paragraph) and reuse the main panel's confirmation pattern (UpdateAllDialog or a host QuickPick/modal, since 2.0 sidebar choices go through the host). Also note 1.x excluded nothing from Update all, whereas 2.0's main panel excludes `central` CPM rows (N76, AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:152) — a sidebar Update all must apply the same exclusion. Severity "high" stands: the action is missing, not degraded.
  - SB1 is partly right: the gap is real, but its root cause and severity are mislabelled and it is an owner-decision item, not an implementation bug. (1) Root cause is not "feature-not-rendered": the 2.0 interaction model (§1, §5, §8), plan rows P5.5/P5.10, the P5.5 commit and ruling, ARCHITECTURE.md:586-601 and README.md:22-31 consistently scope bulk update to the main panel's toolbar + confirmation Dialog and define the sidebar as per-row quick actions with QuickPicks; the sidebar conforms to its own documented model. However, no document records the removal of the 1.x sidebar Update all as a decision (no D-row, ruling, ledger entry, retirement-map row for SidebarApp.test.tsx:421, or CHANGELOG Changed/Breaking line), so it is an unrecorded scope decision the owner must make: keep bulk update main-panel-only and write the exclusion into interaction-model §2/§5 (and fix CHANGELOG.md:39, which currently implies both surfaces have the Update all dialog), or add it to the sidebar. (2) Severity: medium, not high — the sidebar still updates each row (ProjectUpdates.tsx:118 row-action-update), Update all remains in the main panel one title-bar click away (nuiget.sidebar.openFullView), and 1.x's button also lacked the confirmation §5 now requires. (3) If the owner chooses to add it, the fix is not a verbatim 1.x port: a ≤1-day item = UpdatesSection.tsx SectionHeader actions IconButton (single project → ops/bulkUpdate; All Projects → ops/bulkUpdateAllProjects over the allProjects stream), excluding central rows per §5/N76 and busy rows, with a confirmation through a host modal via a new ui/* request handled in SidebarHost (the sidebar's documented pattern, as confirmCentralChange does for CPM) rather than an in-panel Dialog; a new §8 hook (e.g. sidebar-update-all-button) must be added to docs/ux/interaction-model.md or src/test/lint/interaction-model-testids.test.ts fails; tests in UpdatesSection.test.tsx (count label, payload per mode, central/busy exclusion, cancel sends nothing) and a sidebar-parity.ui.test.ts case; the ui/* request is a protocol contract change (plan D6 sentinel). Gates: npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/sidebar; npm run docs:check; npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui.
  - Severity "high" stands under the task's lens (user would notice; fix is not CSS), but the claim needs four corrections. (1) Root cause is "scoped out by design", not "feature-not-rendered": interaction-model.md sections 1, 5 and 7 (lines 11-12, 77, 142) assign bulk actions to the main panel only, so 2.0 is consistent with its design doc and the item is first an OWNER DECISION (restore sidebar Update all, or accept the main-panel-only scope). Drop any "contradicts interaction-model.md" framing; if restored, the doc must change in the same PR (its lines 4-5 require that). (2) The 1.x behaviour cannot be restored 1:1: 1.x ran the bulk op with no confirmation (NuGetSidebarPanel.ts:898-928) and the sidebar bundle may not hold `Dialog` (STATUS.md:795). Fix shape (still <= 1 day): UpdatesSection passes an `actions` IconButton (ArrowUp, label "Update all (N)", disabled while busy or N = 0, hidden under All Projects when any chunk errored) -> new host request (e.g. `ui/updateAll` carrying projectPath or the All Projects flag) -> SidebarHost builds the package list from the catalog/updates it already serves and confirms with a modal listing the packages (CPM pattern SidebarHost.ts:264-266; exclude `central` rows as the main panel does, AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:152) -> `ops/bulkUpdate` or `ops/bulkUpdateAllProjects`. Files: src/webview/sidebar/UpdatesSection.tsx, src/protocol/messages.ts + guards.ts (new request; frozen-path rules apply), src/host/SidebarHost.ts, src/host/handlers.ts. Tests: UpdatesSection.test.tsx "Update all sends the host request with the project and count", src/host/handlers.test.ts or SidebarHost.test.ts "update all confirms in a modal, cancel sends nothing, confirm runs ops/bulkUpdate with every non-central row", plus a harness scenario; gate `npm test`, `npm run bench`, `NUIGET_V2=1 npm run test:ui:v2 -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui-v2`. (3) Regardless of the decision, add a CHANGELOG `### Changed` line "2.0 sidebar: Update all moved to the main panel's Updates tab" (or the restore entry) and reword CHANGELOG:39 so the Update all dialog is attributed to the main panel only; name the Open Full Manager -> Updates -> Update all route in README if the scope is kept. (4) If the owner declines the restore, downgrade to medium with that route documented: nothing is broken, the per-row update and actions menu remain in the sidebar (ProjectUpdates.tsx:84,118), and the capability is three clicks away with a confirmation 1.x never had.

### SB2 — @installed / @updates filter mode shows an empty collapsed section if the user had collapsed it

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.1 d
- 1.18.9: In @installed / @updates mode the list rendered directly without a section header (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1835-1838, renderInstalledList/renderUpdatesList), so a collapsed section could never hide filter results.
- 2.0: SidebarView passes `expanded={layout.installedExpanded}` / `layout.updatesExpanded` in every non-browse mode (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx:173-180,194-201). With Installed collapsed (kept in view state, SidebarView.tsx:16-21,91-98), typing `@installed foo` renders one collapsed header and no rows. The UI test "@installed and @updates show one section alone" (src/test/ui/v2/sidebar.ui.test.ts:102) passes only because the default is expanded.
- Fix: Force the section open while its filter mode is active: `expanded={mode === 'installed' ? true : layout.installedExpanded}` (same for updates) and pass `onToggle={undefined}` in that mode so the header is not a toggle; leave `layout` untouched so the user's choice returns in default mode.
- Files: `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarLayout.test.tsx`
- Test: SidebarLayout.test.tsx: collapse Installed, type `@installed Seri` → sidebar-installed-list has the matching row; clear the box → Installed is collapsed again.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The claim is substantively correct; three precision fixes: (1) The 1.x citation `SidebarApp.tsx:1835-1838` is off — the mode-conditional renders are at 0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1858 and :1861, with the render functions defined at :1501 and :1656. (2) 1.x never persisted the collapsed state at all (`useState(true)` at :47-48, no getState/setState in src/webview/sidebar/), so in 1.x the scenario could only arise after an in-session collapse and even then filter mode bypassed it; 2.0's regression is wider because the collapse survives a webview reload (SidebarView.tsx:33-46, :91-98; index.tsx:20). (3) It is not a dead end: `onToggle` remains wired in filter mode (SidebarView.tsx:177, :198), so one click on the header reveals the filtered list — this supports "medium" rather than "high". Also, the interaction model (docs/ux/interaction-model.md:27-28) does not explicitly address collapse state, so frame this as a behavioural regression against 1.x with the doc silent, not as a doc contradiction.
  - Claim stands on mechanism and severity; four precision fixes: (1) The 1.x citation `0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1835-1838` points at the @-prefix dropdown block; the filter-mode renders are `:1858` (`searchMode === 'installed' && renderInstalledList()`) and `:1861` (`renderUpdatesList()`), and the header-less `section-content` bodies are `:1501-1502` and `:1656-1657`. (2) Root cause should read: SidebarView.tsx:176/:197 pass the persisted default-mode collapse flag (`layout.installedExpanded`/`updatesExpanded`, kept via getState/setState at :33-46,91-102) into the filter-mode sections, and InstalledSection.tsx:138 / UpdatesSection.tsx:180 render `null` content when `expanded` is false — "not rendered by feature" (view layer), not CSS. (3) 1.x never persisted the collapse state (`useState(true)` at :47-48, no getState/setState in the file), so in 1.x the scenario was session-local and bypassed anyway; 2.0's persistence makes it recur on every reload until the header is clicked — supports medium (visible header, one-click recovery, but sticky). (4) Scope guard: the header's presence in filter mode is documented ("Installed section filtered", interaction-model.md:27-28) and tested (SidebarView.test.tsx:411 asserts "Installed 3" in @installed mode); only the collapsed state leaking into filter mode is the gap. Fix shape: in SidebarView.tsx pass `expanded={mode === 'installed' ? true : layout.installedExpanded}` (and the Updates twin), keep the persisted layout untouched (ARCHITECTURE.md:477, P5.10 ruling line 50), add a SidebarLayout.test.tsx case mounting with `{ layout: { installedExpanded: false } }`, typing `@installed `, asserting `sidebar-installed-list` present and `section-header-installed` aria-expanded="true", then clearing the box and asserting the section is collapsed again; gate `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/sidebar`.
  - Severity medium is correct; the claim needs four corrections. (1) Wrong 1.x line cite: the direct `renderInstalledList()`/`renderUpdatesList()` calls are at 0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1854-1861 (:1858, :1861); lines 1835-1838 are the closing JSX of the @-prefix filter dropdown. (2) Root cause bucket is "not rendered by feature" (SidebarView forwards the persisted collapsed state into filter mode), not "behaviour". (3) Exposure is understated: 1.x collapse was session-only React state (SidebarApp.tsx:47-48), 2.0 persists it in the webview's view state (SidebarView.tsx:33-46), so a forgotten collapse now survives restarts. (4) Not a docs/ux/interaction-model.md contradiction — §2 lines 27-28 are silent on collapse — so frame it as a 1.x parity regression only. Proposed fix (≤1 h): in origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx change :176 to `expanded={mode === 'installed' || layout.installedExpanded}` and :197 to `expanded={mode === 'updates' || layout.updatesExpanded}` (do not write to view state, so leaving filter mode returns the section to the user's stored collapse); add a test in src/webview/sidebar/SidebarLayout.test.tsx that mounts with `storeWith({ layout: { split: 50, installedExpanded: false, updatesExpanded: true } })`, types `@installed `, and expects `sidebar-installed-list` present and `section-header-installed` to have `aria-expanded="true"`; optionally extend src/test/ui/v2/sidebar.ui.test.ts:127 to collapse Installed then type `@installed`. Gate: `npm run test:frontend` (and `npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui` if the ExTester title is added).

### SB3 — Section headers lost the VS Code side-bar section chrome

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `css` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: .section-header used sideBarSectionHeader-background, a border-top in sideBarSectionHeader-border, 11px/700 uppercase text and list-hoverBackground on hover (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.css:207-225); high-contrast rule at :651-654.
- 2.0: .nuiget-section-header / .nuiget-section-toggle have no background, no border and no hover rule (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:316-347); tokens.css aliases no sideBarSectionHeader-* colour (src/webview/shared/tokens.css:56-112). Headers read as plain text lines, which is part of the "plainer" impression.
- Fix: Add tokens `--nuiget-color-section-header-bg: var(--vscode-sideBarSectionHeader-background)`, `--nuiget-color-section-header-border: var(--vscode-sideBarSectionHeader-border, transparent)`, `--nuiget-color-section-header-fg: var(--vscode-sideBarSectionHeader-foreground, var(--vscode-foreground))` (plus the high-contrast block). In sidebar.css style `.nuiget-sidebar-section > .nuiget-section-header` with that background, border-top, foreground and `:hover` list-hover background; scope to the sidebar so the main panel is unchanged. Colour literals are forbidden by the webview-colours lint test, so only tokens.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/tokens.css`, `src/webview/sidebar/sidebar.css`, `src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts`
- Test: webview-tokens / webview-colours lint tests pass; theme.ui.test.ts (ExTester) axe pass still green; optional screenshot in the sidebar UI test.
- Gate: `npx vitest run src/test/lint && npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim is correct; four precision points for the fix item. (1) Line anchor: the block starts at primitives.css:315, not 316. (2) The typographic half of the 1.x chrome survives in 2.0: `.nuiget-section-heading` is `font-size: var(--nuiget-font-size-sm)` (0.85em ≈ 11px) / `font-weight: 600` (primitives.css:322-329, 1.x was 700) and `.nuiget-section-toggle` keeps `text-transform: uppercase` (:331-346); what is lost is exactly background, border-top, hover background and the high-contrast contrastBorder border — "read as plain text lines" slightly overstates it. (3) Partial separator: when both sections are open, `<Sash orientation="horizontal">` renders (src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx:181-191, gated on `bothOpen`) with `background: var(--nuiget-color-border)` (primitives.css:522-527, 543-546) — a line in widget-border, not sideBarSectionHeader-border, and nothing above the first header or around a lone collapsed section; this mitigates but does not refute "no border". A fix adding border-top to headers must suppress it on the header after the sash, as 1.x did (SidebarApp.css:594, 600-602), to avoid a double line. (4) Keep in 2.0 (parity, do not regress): the `:focus-visible` outline on `.nuiget-section-toggle` (primitives.css:220-227 vs 1.x :227-230), the real `<button aria-expanded aria-controls>` inside an h2/h3 replacing 1.x's `role="button"` div with nested buttons (SectionHeader.tsx:35-52), and the `Badge`/`Spinner` count and busy indicators. Suggested fix scope: two tokens in tokens.css (`--nuiget-color-section-header-bg: var(--vscode-sideBarSectionHeader-background)`, `--nuiget-color-section-header-border: var(--vscode-sideBarSectionHeader-border, transparent)`, mapped to Canvas/CanvasText in the forced-colors block), background + border-top on `.nuiget-section-header`, `.nuiget-section-toggle:hover { background: var(--nuiget-color-list-hover-bg) }`, an HC border via `--nuiget-color-border`, the sash-adjacent border suppression in sidebar.css, and the new token names added to the tokens lint's REQUIRED list (src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts).
  - SB3 is correct on background, border-top, hover and the high-contrast outline, but overstates the typography loss and should be sharpened:

1. Not lost / documented intent (do not count these): `text-transform: uppercase` is kept (primitives.css:343). The font size is `--nuiget-font-size-sm` = 0.85em (tokens.css:49), about 11px at VS Code's default 13px; em sizing is a recorded 2.0 decision (ADR-008 "em-based typography on --vscode-font-size"; ledger U5 closed at origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/findings-ledger.md:81). The header height moving from 22px to a 24px minimum is also recorded intent (`--nuiget-target-min`/`--nuiget-row-height-compact` 24px, tokens.css:24-32; ledger U11 at findings-ledger.md:87). Only weight 700→600 and `sideBarSectionHeader-foreground`→`foreground` differ, both minor.

2. The real gap is exactly four CSS properties: no `background: var(--vscode-sideBarSectionHeader-background)`, no `border-top` in `sideBarSectionHeader-border`, no hover background, and no `contrastBorder` outline under the HC body classes. Root cause stays "css" (plus three missing colour tokens); no host, slice or feature change is involved.

3. Cite primitives.css:313-367 for the 2.0 block (header 315-319, heading 322-329, toggle 331-346, chevron 354-361, actions 363-367) rather than 316-347.

4. Scope note for the fix phase: `SectionHeader` is one primitive shared with the main panel (project sections, Transitive, All-Projects updates). Painting `sideBarSectionHeader-*` in the primitive repaints those too. 1.x did the same in the main panel (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:543 uses `sideBarSectionHeader-background` with an `editor-background` fallback; :1205 uses `sideBarSectionHeader-border`), so primitive-wide is defensible, but the work item must say whether it is primitive-wide or sidebar-only via a modifier class.

5. The fix fits ADR-008 without exceptions: add `--nuiget-color-section-header-bg|fg|border` to tokens.css as `var(--vscode-sideBarSectionHeader-*)` aliases (passes the "every colour is an alias" check in src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts), map them to Canvas/CanvasText in the forced-colors block and `--vscode-contrastBorder` in the HC block; in primitives.css give `.nuiget-section-header` the background and border-top and add `.nuiget-section-toggle:hover { background: var(--nuiget-color-list-hover-bg) }`; add the three names to REQUIRED in webview-tokens.test.ts and a SectionHeader.test.tsx assertion; gates `npx vitest run --project backend src/test/lint`, `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/primitives`, and `theme.ui.test.ts` (axe colour-contrast 0 under the four default themes).
  - Keep SB3 but downgrade to LOW (cosmetic). The mechanism is exactly as claimed, but "clearly worse" overstates it: on all four themes VS Code offers new users (Dark/Light Modern, Dark/Light 2026) the section-header background is identical to the sidebar background, so only a low-contrast 1px border-top is lost; the grey band is visible only on Dark+/Light+ and third-party themes, and in both HC themes the contrastBorder box is lost. The missing hover is not a regression (VS Code's own pane headers have no hover background). Typography (≈11px uppercase, 600 vs 700, 24px vs 22px) is imperceptibly different. Proposed fix (≤1 hour): add to origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/sidebar.css, scoped so the shared primitive's four main-panel uses are untouched: `.nuiget-sidebar .nuiget-section-header { background: var(--vscode-sideBarSectionHeader-background); border-top: 1px solid var(--vscode-sideBarSectionHeader-border, transparent); color: var(--vscode-sideBarSectionHeader-foreground, var(--nuiget-color-fg)); }` and `.nuiget-sidebar .nuiget-section-toggle { font-weight: 700; }` — the border variable resolves to contrastBorder in HC per theme.ts, so no separate HC rule is needed; optionally alias the two variables as `--nuiget-color-section-header-bg/-border` in tokens.css for the tokens lint. Do not add a hover rule (native parity). Gate: `npm run test:ui` (src/test/ui/v2/theme.ui.test.ts runs axe color-contrast and target-size under six themes incl. both HC) and `npm test`; no new unit test is warranted for a colour-only CSS change.

### SB4 — All Projects: project names render UPPERCASE, small and bold; no path tooltip; count always shown

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `primitive-lacks` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Project group headers were normal weight, `var(--vscode-font-size)`, no text-transform, with `title={pi.projectPath}` tooltip, and the count badge only while collapsed (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.css:459-472; SidebarApp.tsx render helper: `title={pi.projectPath}`, `{isCollapsed && <span className="project-group-count">…}`).
- 2.0: InstalledSection and UpdatesSection render each project with `SectionHeader level={3}` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/InstalledSection.tsx:57-66, UpdatesSection.tsx:68-77); `.nuiget-section-toggle { text-transform: uppercase }` and `.nuiget-section-heading { font-size: var(--nuiget-font-size-sm); font-weight: 600 }` apply to every level (primitives.css:323-346). SectionHeader has no `title` prop (SectionHeader.tsx:7-25). Project names are case-sensitive identifiers; uppercasing them is a readability loss the owner noticed.
- Fix: SectionHeader: add class `nuiget-section-header--level-3` when level === 3 and an optional `title` prop forwarded to the toggle; primitives.css: `.nuiget-section-header--level-3 .nuiget-section-heading { font-size: var(--nuiget-font-size-md); font-weight: normal }` and `.nuiget-section-header--level-3 .nuiget-section-toggle { text-transform: none }`. Sidebar ProjectSection passes `title={chunk.projectPath}`. The main panel's FolderGroups also uses level 3 (src/webview/shared/allProjects/folderGroups.tsx:33) — verify its All Projects headers still look right.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/SectionHeader.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/SectionHeader.test.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/InstalledSection.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/UpdatesSection.tsx`
- Test: SectionHeader.test.tsx: level 3 carries the modifier class and the title attribute; level 2 does not.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend && npx vitest run src/test/lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Two precision edits, neither overturns the gap. (1) "count always shown" overstates: 2.0 shows the count whether expanded or collapsed, but passes `count={undefined}` for an errored or unsupported-and-empty installed chunk (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/InstalledSection.tsx:65) and for an errored or sources-unavailable updates chunk (UpdatesSection.tsx:76). Reword to "count shown regardless of expanded state, when available" (1.x: only while collapsed). (2) The `.nuiget-section-heading {` rule opens at primitives.css:322, not 323; cite 322-329 for the size/weight rule and 331-346 for the uppercase toggle rule.
  - Keep SB4 at medium for the typography and tooltip parts; drop "count always shown" from the gap (or list it separately as low / owner decision): it is a ruled, recorded choice (STATUS.md:2229 P5.4, STATUS.md:2972 P5.10) with an a11y rationale, and its root cause is feature code, not the primitive. Two sharpenings: (a) level-2 uppercase is NOT a regression — 1.x's own Installed/Updates `.section-header` was uppercase 11px/700 (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.css:207-221, VS Code pane-header convention); the regression is only that the primitive applies the pane-header style to `level={3}` project names, so the fix must add a level-3 rule (e.g. `h3.nuiget-section-heading { font-size: var(--nuiget-font-size-md); font-weight: normal; } h3 > .nuiget-section-toggle { text-transform: none; }`) and a `title`/`description` prop on SectionHeader, not strip uppercase from level 2. (b) The same primitive renders project names in the main panel (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx:162-172, AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:132), where 1.x was weight 600 with no transform (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:512-520), so the primitive fix covers both surfaces and should be noted as cross-surface even though SB4 is sidebar-scoped.
  - Keep SB4 at MEDIUM, but with these corrections: (1) Strike "count always shown" as a regression — 2.0's own Installed/Updates headers show the count always and read it into the toggle's accessible name ("Installed 3", SidebarView.test.tsx:390; SectionHeader.tsx:47-48), consistent with VS Code pane badges; list it under "2.0 does better / preserve", not as a gap. (2) Remove "the owner noticed": the owner's observation says only "The sidebar is also plainer" and never mentions project names or case. (3) Re-state the root cause precisely: the primitive styles every heading level as a pane header and neither surface scopes an override (primitives.css:322-345; no `.nuiget-sidebar-project` rule in sidebar.css; main.css:483-489 is the folder caption, not an override). The medium rests on the h2/h3 indistinguishability and missing indent in the sidebar, not on uppercase per se. (4) Split the fix: typography is one scoped CSS rule (e.g. `.nuiget-sidebar-project .nuiget-section-toggle { text-transform: none; font-size: var(--nuiget-font-size-md); font-weight: normal; padding-inline-start: var(--nuiget-space-5); }` in sidebar.css, or a `level`-aware rule in primitives.css — decide whether the main panel's h2 project headings keep the pane style, since they share the primitive); the path tooltip is a small TS change (new optional prop on SectionHeader rendered as `title` on the button, passed `chunk.projectPath` from InstalledSection/UpdatesSection), not CSS. Folder captions disambiguate same-named projects only when `folders.length > 1` (InstalledSection.tsx:100-109), so the tooltip still has a use. Add one assertion per change (RTL: `toHaveAttribute('title', A)`; CSS: a `getComputedStyle` or stylesheet lint in src/test/lint). Gate: `npm test` (frontend project) and `npm run test:ui` for sidebar.ui.test.ts. Total ≤ 0.5 day.

### SB5 — Rows are not indented under the section chevron and workbench.tree.indent is ignored

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `css` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Rows had `padding: 4px 8px 4px 22px` to sit under the chevron (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.css:324-332) and All Projects rows used `padding-left: calc(var(--tree-indent, 8px) + 30px)` (…:539-541); the host sent `treeIndent` from workbench.tree.indent on change (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetSidebarPanel.ts:78-82).
- 2.0: `.nuiget-row-option { padding: 0 var(--nuiget-space-4) }` = 8px on every row (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:90-97); sidebar.css adds no indent. `ExtensionConfig.treeIndent` is read host-side (src/host/services.ts:97) but `git grep treeIndent origin/labs/2.0 -- src/webview` finds only tests: nothing consumes it.
- Fix: SidebarView reads `useConfig().treeIndent` and sets `--nuiget-tree-indent` on the root via cssVars. sidebar.css: `.nuiget-sidebar-section-content .nuiget-row-option { padding-inline-start: calc(var(--nuiget-space-4) + var(--nuiget-icon-size-md) + var(--nuiget-space-2)) }` (aligns under the chevron) and `.nuiget-sidebar-project .nuiget-row-option { padding-inline-start: calc(… + var(--nuiget-tree-indent, 8px)) }` for All Projects rows.
- Files: `src/webview/sidebar/sidebar.css`, `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.test.tsx`
- Test: SidebarView.test.tsx: after config/changed with treeIndent 16 the root style has `--nuiget-tree-indent: 16px`.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend && npx vitest run src/test/lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; three refinements. (1) Geometry: 1.x rows did not sit "under the chevron" — they aligned with the section title text at 22px (header 2px + chevron 20px), i.e. past the chevron column; in 2.0 the title text starts at 24px (4px padding + 16px chevron + 4px gap) while rows start at 8px, so rows begin 16px left of the title and inside the chevron column — a flatter, inverted hierarchy, not merely "no indent". (2) Root cause is not CSS alone: host, protocol and slice are at parity (services.ts:97, extension.ts:332, SidebarHost.ts:39, config.tsx:26, ConfigProvider mounted via StoreProvider.tsx:30 / sidebar/index.tsx:19); the gap is "not rendered by feature + primitive lacks it + CSS": no sidebar component calls `useConfig`, `RowProps`/`Listbox` have no indent/depth prop, and `.nuiget-row-option` hard-codes 8px. The fix must also call `useConfig().enable()` in the sidebar because the slice is lazy (config.tsx:28-36) and `config` is `undefined` until asked or until the first `config/changed`. (3) All Projects is flatter still: `ProjectSection` reuses `SectionHeader level={3}` with no level-specific CSS and `.nuiget-sidebar-project` has no rule, so nested rows get the same 8px as top-level rows, where 1.x gave them `tree-indent + 30px`. Severity medium is appropriate (ignored user setting plus a feature the released CHANGELOG.md:342 advertised); no UX-doc contradiction should be claimed.
  - Claim stands; two precisions. (1) It is worse than "not indented": 2.0 rows begin 16px to the left of their section's title text (8px vs ~24px), whereas 1.x aligned both at 22px; and the All Projects per-project groups lost their extra `calc(var(--tree-indent) + 30px)` indent entirely. (2) Root cause is CSS plus a missing webview consumer, not CSS alone: host→protocol→event plumbing for `treeIndent` is complete (services.ts:97, domain.ts:347, extension.ts:332) but nothing under src/webview/sidebar imports `useConfig`, and the lazy config slice is never enabled there. Fix is webview-only: sidebar calls `useConfig().enable()`, writes `--nuiget-tree-indent` from `config.treeIndent` (default 8), and sidebar.css adds a row indent under section headers (chevron 4+16+4 = 24px) and `calc(var(--nuiget-tree-indent) + ...)` for rows inside `.nuiget-sidebar-project`. This is an owner decision only in the sense that no doc records the intent; nothing on origin/labs/2.0 documents the flat layout as deliberate.
  - Keep SB5 at medium for the row/header misalignment (rows at 8px outdent the section and project titles at 24px; 1.17.0 advertised the indentation as a feature), but split out and downgrade the "workbench.tree.indent is ignored" part to low (1.x honoured it only for All Projects nested rows, default 8px, undocumented), and drop the "contradicts interaction-model.md" framing (the doc is silent on indentation). Root cause stays CSS. Fix is well under a day: in sidebar.css add `.nuiget-sidebar .nuiget-row-option { padding-inline-start: calc(var(--nuiget-space-2) + var(--nuiget-icon-size-md) + var(--nuiget-space-2)); }` (=24px, aligns with the title) and `.nuiget-sidebar-project .nuiget-section-toggle { padding-inline-start: calc(var(--nuiget-space-2) + var(--nuiget-tree-indent, 8px)); } .nuiget-sidebar-project .nuiget-row-option { padding-inline-start: calc(24px + var(--nuiget-tree-indent, 8px)); }`; optionally set `--nuiget-tree-indent` via `cssVars()` on `<main className="nuiget-sidebar">` (SidebarView.tsx:145) from `useConfig().config?.treeIndent` after `enable()`. Test: SidebarLayout.test.tsx asserting the computed padding-inline-start of a sidebar option vs the section title offset, and that `config/changed { treeIndent: 16 }` updates the CSS variable; gate `npm run test:frontend`. The trivial-CSS fix does not lower user-facing severity under the stated rubric (that rule targets "high").

### SB6 — Search box lost the filter (@) button and the clear button

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Inside the search wrapper: a clear button (ClearAllIcon) that empties the box and refocuses, and a filter button (FilterIcon) that opens the @-prefix dropdown listing every prefix (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1792-1819; CSS :66-116).
- 2.0: SearchBox renders the input alone (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.tsx:126-168); `git grep 'FilterIcon|ClearAllIcon' origin/labs/2.0 -- src/webview/sidebar src/webview/main src/webview/shared/search` → nothing, though both icons exist (src/webview/shared/primitives/icons.tsx:131,239). The prefixes are still reachable by typing `@` and the placeholder names them (SidebarView.tsx:153), so interaction-model §2 is met; this is 1.x parity/discoverability only.
- Fix: Add two pointer buttons inside `.nuiget-search-box` (IconButton, tabIndex -1): Clear (ClearAllIcon) → `change('')` and refocus, shown only when value is non-empty; Filter (FilterIcon) → a `forcedPrefixes` state that lists every prefix as options while the box is empty or not a complete prefix. Both surfaces get it (the main panel's box is the same component); keep the input `type="search"` native clear hidden via `::-webkit-search-cancel-button { display: none }` to avoid two clear buttons.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/search/search.css`, `src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.test.tsx`
- Test: SearchBox.test.tsx: filter click lists @installed and @updates and ArrowDown/Enter picks one; clear click calls onChange('') and focus stays in the input.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim is substantially correct for the filter (@) button: it is gone from 2.0 with no replacement. Narrow the clear-button half: 2.0 has no custom ClearAllIcon button, but keeps a native `type="search"` cancel x (Chromium default, no in-repo appearance reset; VS Code's injected webview CSS not verified) and a keyboard Escape-to-clear that 1.x's tabIndex=-1 buttons never offered. Reframe severity: 1.x's funnel button was the ONLY prefix hint (placeholder "Search NuGet packages"); 2.0 names the prefixes in the placeholder, so prefix discoverability is arguably better, not worse — the residual gap is one-click access for pointer users. Recommend severity low (cosmetic/parity), not medium. Add to the "2.0 does better" list: keyboard Escape clears the box; the dropdown is a proper combobox with aria-activedescendant. Any fix should add pointer-only (`tabIndex={-1}`) buttons inside `.nuiget-search-box` so it does not add a tab stop and regress the §2/§5 keyboard model; also note the claim's "input alone (126-168)" is loose — that range also renders the options `<ul>`.
  - SB6 stands only for the filter (@) button; it is an undocumented omission, not a design decision, and it is pointer-only discoverability (1.x button was tabIndex -1; typing `@` and the placeholder cover the documented path) — low, not medium. The clear-button half is largely wrong: 2.0's `<input type="search">` keeps Chromium's native clear control (no CSS hides it) and Escape clears per §2; what is lost is only the always-visible themed icon (theme rendering of the native control unverified). Re-root-cause as "primitive lacks it": shared SearchBox has no trailing-action slot, so the 2.0 main panel (MainShell.tsx:181-194) lost the same two buttons that 1.x App.tsx:2736-2760 had. Fix once in src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.tsx (+ search.css): optional filter IconButton that opens the prefix list (set a "forced open" flag that bypasses the isCompletePrefix guard) and an explicit clear button if the native one is judged insufficient; add RTL + axe cases in SearchBox.test.tsx; gate `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/search`. If the owner keeps the current behaviour, record it in interaction-model §2 so the lint/doc contract reflects the decision.
  - Downgrade SB6 from medium to LOW, and correct the mechanism: the clear button is not lost — 2.0 uses `<input type="search">` (native × on non-empty text, no CSS suppression found) and Escape clears the box (tested, specified in interaction-model §2). Only the one-click filter (funnel) button is actually gone, and its sole function (listing every prefix) is reproduced by typing `@`, while the 2.0 placeholder names the prefixes outright, which 1.x's did not. Re-scope the item as cross-surface (main panel lost the same buttons; 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2736-2760) with the fix in the shared SearchBox.tsx, and record it as a cosmetic/native-look (VS Code Extensions view) parity item, not "clearly worse".

### SB7 — No arrow-key bridge between the search box and the lists

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: ArrowDown in the search box (dropdown closed) focused the visible list (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:994-1004); ArrowUp on the first row returned focus to the search box (…:1155-1159).
- 2.0: SearchBox handles ArrowDown only `if (open)` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.tsx:85-93); Listbox ArrowUp at index 0 is a no-op (src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx:140-145). Keyboard users must Tab through the section toggles to reach rows.
- Fix: SearchBox: optional `onArrowDown` called when the list is closed; SidebarView focuses the first visible listbox's active option (`[role="listbox"] [role="option"][tabindex="0"]` inside the view, browse list first when in browse mode). Listbox: optional `onExitTop` called on ArrowUp at index 0; the sidebar passes a callback that focuses searchRef. Document the keys in the lists' `hint` sentences.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/search/SearchBox.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.test.tsx`
- Test: SidebarView.test.tsx: ArrowDown in the box moves focus to the first installed option; ArrowUp there returns focus to the box; with the prefix dropdown open ArrowDown still navigates the dropdown.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`

### SB8 — Installed and Updates rows are not sorted alphabetically

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.25 d
- 1.18.9: filteredInstalled and filteredUpdates sorted by `id.localeCompare` (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1019-1033); All Projects groups sorted each project's rows too (render helper `const sorted = [...filtered].sort(...)`).
- 2.0: ProjectPackages filters only (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/ProjectPackages.tsx:40-43); the host emits packages in declaration order, props file first then project file (src/core/project/inspector.ts:128-137). ProjectUpdates filters only (ProjectUpdates.tsx:44); Catalog.computeUpdates keeps mapWithConcurrency input order (src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:231-262, src/core/feeds/updates.ts:38-54) and streamed rows arrive in completion order until the answer lands (src/webview/shared/store/updates.tsx:59,87).
- Fix: In both `items` useMemo calls sort with `a.id.localeCompare(b.id, 'en')` after filtering (AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx:47 already uses this comparator). Consider the same for the main panel's installed/updates lists (other area).
- Files: `src/webview/sidebar/ProjectPackages.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/ProjectUpdates.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.test.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/UpdatesSection.test.tsx`
- Test: ProjectPackages renders Zlib before Autofac when the host lists Zlib first; ProjectUpdates sorts streamed rows as they arrive.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Browse row showed Trash "Uninstall (Del)" when installed, "+" with a "2/5" count badge when partially installed, "+" otherwise (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/components/PackageRow.tsx:67-77,114-118); Delete uninstalled, Enter installed (SidebarApp.tsx:1437-1453); in All Projects the hover button went straight to the project picker (pickProjectForInstall / pickProjectForRemove, SidebarApp.tsx:1120-1180).

Under All Projects the PlusIcon is rendered for every row including one installed in all projects (…:161-184) and clicking it opens the actions QuickPick first, then the project picker (SidebarHost.ts:161-202) — one more step than 1.x.
- Fix: Single project: when installedVersion is defined render `<IconButton label={`Uninstall ${id}`} data-testid="row-action-uninstall"><TrashIcon/></IconButton>` → run('ops/remove'), and pass `onDelete` to the Listbox for the same path. All Projects: TrashIcon when `where.length === listedCount`, PlusIcon otherwise; keep the actions menu as the target (the host's picker flow stays). Update the Listbox `hint` sentences.

- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`

### SB12 — Browse rows no longer show the package description (sanctioned by §1 — owner decision)

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `other` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Browse rows passed `description={pkg.description}` (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1484) and PackageRow rendered a truncated second line (components/PackageRow.tsx:121-125; CSS :402-410).
- 2.0: BrowseResults passes no description (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/sidebar/BrowseResults.tsx:173-185) and compact rows hide it visually anyway (src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:186-193). This complies with interaction-model §1 "compact rows (no icons, no descriptions)" (docs/ux/interaction-model.md:12), so it is a 1.x regression, not a doc contradiction.
- Fix: Owner decides: (a) accept and leave §1 as is, or (b) amend §1 to "no icons; one-line description in browse rows" and pass `description={result.description}` with a sidebar.css rule un-hiding `.nuiget-sidebar-browse .nuiget-row-description` as a second line (row height grows to ~36px; set `estimateSize` on that Listbox).
- Files: `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`, `src/webview/sidebar/BrowseResults.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/sidebar.css`
- Test: If (b): BrowseResults.test.tsx asserts the description text is visible in the row.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend && npm run docs:check`

### SB13 — Section count badge shows "0" and is not replaced by the spinner while loading

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `primitive-lacks` · Estimate: 0.1 d
- 1.18.9: Badge rendered only when `count > 0` and was replaced by a spinner while loading (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/components/SectionHeader.tsx:55-58).
- 2.0: SectionHeader renders the badge whenever `count !== undefined` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/SectionHeader.tsx:48) and a spinner beside it when busy (…:49); UpdatesSection.test.tsx:155 asserts "counts 0" on purpose, so this is a conscious 2.0 choice.
- Fix: If the owner prefers 1.x: render the Badge only when `count > 0`, keep the spinner. Otherwise close as no change.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/SectionHeader.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/SectionHeader.test.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/UpdatesSection.test.tsx`
- Test: SectionHeader.test.tsx: count 0 renders no badge; count 3 renders "3".
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`

### SB14 — Search box is not focused when the view first renders

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.1 d
- 1.18.9: Focused on mount after 100 ms (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:859) and again on every visibility change via `focusSearch` (src/webview/NuGetSidebarPanel.ts:117-120).

- Fix: SidebarView: `useEffect(() => searchRef.current?.focus(), [])` once at mount; SidebarHost: also send `ui/focusSearch` right after `this.attach(...)` in resolveWebviewView when `webviewView.visible`.
- Files: `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.tsx`, `src/host/SidebarHost.ts`, `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.test.tsx`
- Test: SidebarView.test.tsx: the search input is document.activeElement after mount.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`

### SB15 — Rows have no tooltip with the full id and version (truncated ids unreadable in a narrow sidebar)

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `primitive-lacks` · Estimate: 0.1 d
- 1.18.9: `.package-row-header` carried `title={`${packageId} ${displayVersion}`}` (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/components/PackageRow.tsx:110).
- 2.0: Row's title span has no `title` attribute; only the version cell may (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx:98-101) while `.nuiget-row-name` ellipsises (primitives.css:151-154).
- Fix: Add `title={typeof version === 'string' ? `${name} ${version}` : name}` on `.nuiget-row-title` (or a `title` prop the owner passes).
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx`
- Test: Row.test.tsx: the title element has the tooltip text.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`

**Better in 2.0 (do not regress):**
- Virtualized Listbox with roving tabIndex, aria-posinset/aria-setsize, a visually hidden key hint per list and one role=status live region for every outcome (src/webview/shared/primitives/Listbox.tsx, SidebarView.tsx:204) — 1.x had plain divs and no announcements.
- Row actions become visible on :focus-within as well as :hover (primitives.css:208-212); 1.x showed them on hover/selected only.
- Per-row spinner and status text while an operation is pending, also for operations started on the other surface (ProjectPackages.tsx:46-55,108-110; SidebarView.test.tsx:274); rows never stay optimistic after a failure.
- Keyboard-operable sash (ArrowUp/Down, Home/End, Enter resets) plus persisted expanded/collapsed state and split in the view state (Sash.tsx, SidebarView.tsx:16-46); 1.x sash was pointer-only and only the split was persisted.
- Ctrl/Cmd+Enter opens the package in the full view and Shift+F10 / ContextMenu key open the actions menu (Listbox.tsx:149-169, rowRequests.ts); 1.x had no keyboard route to the context menu.
- Typed context-menu actions (pickers.ts:93-129) replace 1.x's label.includes() string matching (NuGetSidebarPanel.ts:1150-1239), closing the documented 'Update to Version' substring trap.
- CPM awareness: CPM badge, central-change confirmation modal listing affected projects, override rows updating in place, bulk install leaving out projects with a central entry and saying which (ProjectUpdates.tsx:61-87, SidebarHost.ts:240-296).
- Unresolved / implicit / props-declared / unsupported-project rows are disabled with a sentence saying why, instead of offering an uninstall that would fail (src/webview/shared/installed/rules.ts, ProjectPackages.tsx:57-71).
- Provisional badge with tooltip when versions come from static parsing, replaced when evaluation lands (InstalledSection.tsx:134-136).
- Sources-unavailable is a warning Banner with Retry, never rendered as "No updates available" (UpdatesSection.tsx:24-29,118-121,145-150); errors are inline banners with Retry instead of silent empty text.
- Revalidate without blanking plus skeleton rows for the first load (SidebarView.tsx:165, InstalledSection.tsx:98,117); 1.x flashed 'Loading...' text and forceRefresh blanked the lists.
- Browse badges 'Installed' / 'Installed in N of M' carry a tooltip listing project and version (BrowseResults.tsx:164-171); 1.x showed a bare '2/5' pill.
- @updates filtering also works in All Projects (ProjectUpdates.tsx:44); the 1.x all-projects updates branch ignored filterText.
- Search box is a proper combobox with aria-activedescendant and Escape semantics per §2 (SearchBox.tsx), and its placeholder advertises @installed / @updates (SidebarView.tsx:153).
- Forced-colours, high-contrast and reduced-motion handled once through tokens.css (tokens.css:115-179) instead of per-class body.vscode-* overrides.
- Workspace-folder captions and per-project collapsible sections with counts in All Projects, in a shared store with chunk streaming (InstalledSection.tsx:100-109, store/allProjects.tsx).
- Title-bar refresh is a non-destructive workspace invalidation (SidebarHost.ts:111-113) rather than 1.x's cache-clearing forceRefresh that emptied the lists.

**Notes:**
- Not gaps (verified): package.json `contributes` for the sidebar are byte-identical on 0457c75 and origin/labs/2.0 — same commands, view/title entries, groups, icons and nuiget.prereleaseEnabled / nuiget.restoreEnabled when-clauses; context menu entries match 1:1 (pickers.ts:99-129 vs NuGetSidebarPanel.ts:1109-1139, wording/ellipsis only); project, source, version and multi-project QuickPicks mirror 1.x (pickers.ts vs NuGetSidebarPanel.ts:405-520,955-1000,1241-1320); view title = project name / 'All Projects (N)' on both; @installed/@updates filtering, 300 ms browse debounce with 2-char minimum, Enter-bypass, collapsible sections with draggable split and hover actions all present.
- Dead path, not user-visible: host `layout/get|set` still stores `nuiget.layout.sidebarSectionSplit` (handlers.ts:241-258) but the sidebar persists layout through the webview's own getState/setState (SidebarView.tsx:16-46); `git grep sidebarSectionSplit origin/labs/2.0 -- src/webview` finds no consumer. Whether WebviewView state survives a window reload was not verified; 1.x's workspaceState split is swept without migration by design (legacyState.ts:13-21, ADR-000).
- Not verified: 1.x refresh cleared in-memory NuGet caches and the dotnet HTTP cache in the background (NuGetSidebarPanel.ts:628-642); 2.0's refresh() only invalidates the workspace scope (SidebarHost.ts:111-113, same as refresh/all at handlers.ts:405-408). What the coordinator's cache policy does on 'manual-refresh' was not read; if stale versions persist after refresh, that is a cross-area behaviour gap.
- Interaction-model §6 says manual refresh 'issues refresh/all once'; the sidebar title-bar command calls services.invalidate directly instead of the request — functionally identical today, cosmetic doc drift.
- Any new data-testid in a fix must be added to docs/ux/interaction-model.md §8: src/test/lint/interaction-model-testids.test.ts fails for listed hooks nobody renders and for unlisted names rendered under a listed pattern.
- New CSS must use tokens only (src/test/lint/webview-colours.test.ts, webview-css-baseline.test.ts, webview-tokens.test.ts); SectionHeader and SearchBox are shared with the main panel, so SB4 and SB6 change both surfaces.
- The CI failure the owner mentions is outside this area and was not investigated; existing 2.0 sidebar gates are `npm run test:frontend` (SidebarView/UpdatesSection/BrowseResults/SidebarLayout tests) and `npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui` (src/test/ui/v2/sidebar.ui.test.ts, sidebar-parity.ui.test.ts). Nothing was run in this read-only pass.

## Metadata/icon pipeline for Installed and Updates: host enrichment (Catalog/enricher/icons) -> protocol -> webview store -> feature rendering

### DP1 — Installed rows (single project and All Projects) never render icon, authors or Verified although the lite metadata is stored

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Phase 2 enrichment: `installedPackagesMetadata` (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:316-325 <- fetchInstalledPackageMetadata 0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:448-479) merged in 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:443-466; All Projects `allProjectsInstalledProjectMetadata` (NuGetPanel.ts:1162-1178) merged App.tsx:975-1010. `PackageRowContent` drew a 32px icon with default fallback, name + badges, a version line and an authors line with the Verified icon (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:183-246; all-projects rows :1343).
- 2.0: Host answers `transitive/metadata` with iconUrl (data URI), verified, authors, description, vulnerabilities (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/handlers.ts:289 -> src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:285-289 -> src/core/feeds/enricher.ts:46-82); stored per lower-cased id (src/webview/shared/store/installed.tsx:66-87; All Projects from the `metadata` chunk Catalog.ts:340-359 into src/webview/shared/store/allProjects.tsx:119-123). `InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136` reads only `description` (:124) and `vulnerabilities` (:131): no `icon` prop, no authors, no Verified badge, although `Row` accepts icon/description/badges (src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx:36-39, 97). Contradicts docs/ux/interaction-model.md:11 ("Comfortable rows (icons, description, badges)").
- Fix: In renderRow: `const lite = metadata.get(entry.id.toLowerCase())`; pass `icon` (DP5's `PackageIcon`, or inline `<img className="nuiget-browse-icon" src=…>` guarded by `startsWith('data:')` as BrowseList.tsx:111 does), `description={[lite?.authors ? `by ${lite.authors}` : '', lite?.description].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')}` (the BrowseList.tsx:109 pattern), and `<Badge label="Verified" tone="info" title="The package id prefix is reserved by its owner" />` when `lite?.verified` (wording of UpdatesTab.tsx:191). The same component serves All Projects, so one change covers both. Keep the two-phase ask as it is (ADR-009 d.1).
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`
- Test: InstalledTab.test.tsx: `it('rows carry the lite metadata: icon, authors, Verified and description, for the single project and for an All Projects section')` — respond to `host.requests('transitive/metadata')[0]` with `{ metadata: [{ id: 'Serilog', version: '3.0.0', iconUrl: 'data:image/png;base64,AAAA', verified: true, authors: 'Serilog Contributors', description: 'Logging' }] }`; assert `querySelector('img')` src, accessible description `/by Serilog Contributors · Logging/` and `/reserved by its owner/`; All Projects via `host.chunk(request, { kind: 'metadata', projectPath: A, packages: [...] })`.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/installed && npx eslint src/webview/main && npx tsc --noEmit`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Verdict stands (not refuted). Two citation fixes to the claim's 1.x evidence:
(1) The All Projects merge for `allProjectsInstalledProjectMetadata` (the message NuGetPanel.ts:1162-1178 actually emits, sole emitter at :1170) is handled at 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:1070-1100, not :975-1010. Lines :975-1010 handle a different message, `allProjectsInstalledMetadata`, which NuGetPanel.ts never emits (legacy handler). Cite :1070.
(2) The row-site attribution is swapped: 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:1248 is the all-projects row (`compositeKey`, `selectedUninstallsAllProjects`), and :1343 is the single-project row (`deferredInstalledPackages`). Both call `PackageRowContent`, so the substance (icon/authors/Verified on both Installed modes in 1.x) is unchanged.
Also note for the fix item: 2.0 already has a precedent for the mapping in BrowseList.tsx:109-114 (data-URI icon guard `iconUrl?.startsWith('data:')`, `by ${authors}` in description, `Verified` Badge) and UpdatesTab.tsx:191 (Verified badge); InstalledProjectSection should reuse the same pattern so Installed, Updates and Browse rows stay consistent.
  - The claim stands (severity high, root cause feature-not-rendered in both single-project and All Projects). Three precisions for the write-up: (1) Reassign the citations: the icon and the Verified badge contradict docs/ux/interaction-model.md:11 ("icons, description, badges"); the authors line is NOT in §1 (authors appear only in §7, the Details panel), so it is a 1.x-parity gap backed by docs/plans/STATUS.md:62 ("two-phase, 1.x parity") and the CHANGELOG [Unreleased] "2.0 source health" entry ("fills in icons, authors and vulnerabilities"), not an interaction-model contradiction. (2) The fix must include the default-icon fallback: docs/adr/006-icons-and-csp.md:18 says "the webview shows the bundled default icon" when resolution fails, but no 2.0 main-panel row renders a default icon (BrowseList.tsx:111 renders nothing unless `iconUrl` is a `data:` URI; InstalledProjectSection passes no icon at all), whereas 1.x always drew one (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:186-191). Wiring `icon={<img src={iconUrl}/>}` alone would still leave blank rows and still contradict ADR-006. (3) The same render-side pattern exists in Updates rows (UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 renders Verified only; AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-122 renders neither icon, authors nor Verified, although docs/plans/STATUS.md:76 says the terminal `updates/get` rows "carry `iconUrl`/`verified`/`authors`"); that is a separate gap, but the fix item can be scoped to every comfortable `Row` at once.
  - Keep DP1 at HIGH for the missing package icon only (promised by interaction-model.md:11, fetched by the host for every row, default-icon URI already injected into the DOM by services.ts:106-108 but never read by any 2.0 webview file, 1.x always showed one). Downgrade the authors line and the Verified badge within DP1 to MEDIUM: authors are shown in the Details tab one click away (DetailsInfo.tsx:25-26) and both are Browse-time discriminators that 2.0 does render in BrowseList; Verified also appears on 2.0 Updates rows (UpdatesTab.tsx:191), so on Installed it is an inconsistency, not a loss of function. Drop the "rows are plain text" framing: 2.0 installed rows carry a description (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124) that 1.x installed rows never had, plus CPM and Vulnerable text badges — list the description as a 2.0 improvement the fix must keep. Note the fix is a one-component, ~8-line TSX change (not CSS) mirroring BrowseList.tsx:109-115, with the default-icon fallback wired from root.dataset.packageIcon as in 1.x App.tsx:22; scope the same work item to UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 and TransitiveSection.tsx:78, which have the identical omission.

### DP2 — Updates rows render no icon or authors (single project) and only the CPM badge in All Projects; the `icons` chunk has no consumer

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Single project: `checkPackageUpdates` attached iconUrl/verified/authors to each update (0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:2080-2095) and streamed it already decorated (`packageUpdateFound`, 0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:1056-1063); rendered as icon + versions + authors with Verified (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:689-711). All Projects: `allProjectsIcons` (NuGetPanel.ts:1087-1097) merged in App.tsx:1241-1252, rendered as the row icon (UpdatesTab.tsx:609-615).
- 2.0: Terminal `updates/get` rows carry iconUrl/verified/authors (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:253-269), stored (src/webview/shared/store/updates.tsx:87); `UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196` renders only the Verified badge (:191), no icon, no authors. All Projects: the `project` chunk rows are the enriched ones and an `icons` chunk follows (Catalog.ts:376-390), both stored (allProjects.tsx:198-206); `AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-122` renders neither icon, authors nor Verified, and `updates.icons` is read nowhere (git grep: allProjects.tsx:205,304 only).
- Fix: Both renderRow sites: `icon` from `update.iconUrl` (data-URI guard or `PackageIcon`), `description={update.authors ? `by ${update.authors}` : undefined}`, Verified badge in AllProjectsUpdates mirroring UpdatesTab.tsx:191. Read the row's `iconUrl`; use `updates.icons.get(id.toLowerCase())` only as a fallback when a row lacks one. Do not remove the chunk (frozen contract) and do not move enrichment back into `check`: decorations arrive in one flip at the terminal answer, after the bare `found` rows (Catalog.ts:227-230).
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/AllProjectsUpdates.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.test.tsx`
- Test: UpdatesTab.test.tsx: `it('rows show the icon, authors and Verified of the terminal answer after the bare found chunks')` — `host.chunk(request, { kind: 'found', update: bare })` then `host.respond(request, { projectPath: A, updates: [enriched] })`; assert no `img` before, `img[src]` and `/by Serilog Contributors/` after. All Projects: `it('All Projects rows carry icon, authors and Verified from the project chunk')` with a `kind: 'project'` chunk whose rows have `iconUrl`.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/updates && npx eslint src/webview/main && npx tsc --noEmit`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands as written. Three scoping precisions for the fix phase: (1) The All Projects parity gap versus 1.x is icon-only — 1.x All Projects used `checkPackageUpdatesMinimal` (0457c75:src/services/NuGetOperations.ts:602) and its row rendered icon + name + versions with no authors/Verified block (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:609-623); 2.0 omitting authors/Verified there is not a regression, though the enriched `project` chunk rows make adding them free. (2) The `icons` chunk is redundant: Catalog.ts:376-388 derives it from the already-enriched `result.updates`, so the fix should render `update.iconUrl` straight from the row in both UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 and AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-122 and may delete the chunk, the `icons` map in allProjects.tsx and the protocol variant at src/protocol/messages.ts:130. (3) No CSP or host work is needed — the enricher's `iconUrl` is already a `data:` URI (icons.ts:276), so the same `startsWith('data:')` guard used in BrowseList.tsx:111 applies.
  - Two sharpenings so the fix targets the right thing:

1. All Projects parity baseline is icon-only. 1.x `checkPackageUpdatesMinimal` returned only id/installedVersion/latestVersion/sourceUrl (0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:2122-2123, 2146-2151) and the 1.x All Projects Updates row rendered icon + name + versions with no authors and no Verified (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:609-623). So in All Projects the regression vs 1.x is the missing icon alone; rendering authors/Verified there would be an improvement over 1.x (2.0 already has the data on the chunk rows), not a parity gap.

2. The `icons` chunk is redundant, not merely unconsumed. `Catalog.allProjectsUpdates` emits the `project` chunk with `result.updates` already enriched with `iconUrl` (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:376-381, enriched at :256-269; Catalog.test.ts:629,639 shows the same URL in both), then an `icons` chunk with the same URLs (:382-390). The fix should read `update.iconUrl` straight off the rows, as BrowseList does (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:109-111: `icon={result.iconUrl?.startsWith('data:') ? <img className="nuiget-browse-icon" .../> : undefined}` and `description` built from authors), in both UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 and AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-122, add the Verified badge in AllProjectsUpdates, and may drop the `icons` chunk from `allProjects/updates` (origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/messages.ts:130, a contract change) rather than wire `updates.icons` to a consumer. Add tests in UpdatesTab.test.tsx asserting the `img[src^="data:"]` and the authors text in both modes; gate `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/updates`. Related but outside DP2: ADR-006:18 promises a bundled default icon when `iconUrl` is undefined and no 2.0 webview renders one (BrowseList included).
  - The claim is right on mechanism and on "high" for the single-project Updates tab, but its All Projects half overstates 1.x. The 1.x All Projects Updates row rendered checkbox + icon + name + versions only (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:595-625); there is no `package-authors` block and no Verified icon there (those exist only in the single-project row at :689-711). So the All Projects parity gap is icon only; missing authors in All Projects is not a regression, and the missing Verified badge in 2.0 All Projects is a 2.0-internal inconsistency with its own single-project tab (UpdatesTab.tsx:191), not a 1.x gap. Also record that 2.0 All Projects adds a CPM badge 1.x never had (AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:118) and must not regress. Implementation constraint for the fix: 2.0's webview CSP is `img-src ${cspSource} data:` only (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/services.ts:113) and icons arrive as data: URIs (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/icons.ts:211-216; CHANGELOG.md:31), so the fix must mirror BrowseList.tsx:111's `iconUrl?.startsWith('data:')` guard rather than a bare `<img src={iconUrl}>`; a remote https iconUrl would be blocked. Fix is well under one day: pass `icon` (guarded data: img) and `description={update.authors ? `by ${update.authors}` : undefined}` in UpdatesTab.tsx; in AllProjectsUpdates.tsx read `all.updates.icons.get(update.id.toLowerCase()) ?? update.iconUrl` for the icon and add the Verified badge; add UpdatesTab.test.tsx assertions mirroring BrowseList.test.tsx:67-81 (img src equals data: ICON, authors text present, no img for https URL); gate with `npm run test:frontend`.

### DP3 — Icon circuit breaker silences a source (nuget.org included) for the rest of the session after five consecutive icon-less packages

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: nuget.org icons came from the search API's `iconUrl` field (0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:2234-2240) or an un-gated HEAD probe (:1203-1210); the miss-count breaker applied to custom sources only (:1215-1223), and the webview drew the default icon when nothing resolved.
- 2.0: `DefaultIconResolver` counts every `missing` (a flat-container 404/410 = "this package has no icon", origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/flatContainer.ts:176-180) against the source (src/core/feeds/icons.ts:218), skips a source at >= 5 consecutive misses without fetching (icons.ts:24, 195-197), clears the counter only on a `found` (:214) or `reset()` (:180), and `reset()` runs only from `FeedContexts.clear()` (src/core/catalog/feedContexts.ts:136-139) on a `sources`/`workspace` invalidation (Catalog.ts:555-562: manual refresh or a source change). Pinned by icons.test.ts:224-255. With 8-way enrichment (enricher.ts:23) five icon-less packages in a row (analyzers, *.Abstractions, build packages) silence icons for every project, both surfaces and Browse under that nuget.config chain until Refresh. Plausible root cause for "no package icons" even where 2.0 renders them (Browse); not reproduced read-only.
- Fix: Never suppress a source that has answered `found` in this resolver (an `everFound: Set<string>` checked in `isSourceSuppressed`), and bound a suppression in time (`ICON_SOURCE_SUPPRESS_MS`, e.g. 10 min, stored as an expiry instead of a count) so it never outlives a session; keep `failed` outside the count (C-feeds-2). ADR-006 is frozen and says nothing about the breaker, so no ADR edit; record the behaviour change in STATUS.md and the ledger.
- Files: `src/core/feeds/icons.ts`, `src/core/feeds/icons.test.ts`, `src/core/feeds/STATUS.md`
- Test: icons.test.ts: `it('a source that has answered an icon is never suppressed by later misses')` and `it('a suppressed source is asked again after the suppression window')`; adjust `suppresses a source after consecutive misses until reset(), and a hit clears the counter` (:224) to the new window.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project backend src/core/feeds/icons.test.ts src/core/feeds/policy.test.ts src/core/catalog/Catalog.test.ts && npx tsc --noEmit`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The claim stands; four precision corrections. (1) Path: the concurrency constant is origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/enricher.ts:23, not src/core/catalog/enricher.ts. (2) "For the rest of the session ... until Refresh" is slightly overstated: `FeedContexts.clear()` also runs on every `workspace`-scope invalidation from the file watchers for `Directory.{Build,Packages}.{props,targets}` (src/extension.ts:321), `global.json` (:323) and a workspace-folder change (:327-329), and on `sources` scope from the nuget.config watcher (:322) or a source add/remove/enable/disable (src/host/handlers.ts:342). However `project` and `metadata` scopes from install/update/remove operations and the .csproj watcher (handlers.ts:228-230, extension.ts:320) never reset it, so during normal package work the breaker stays tripped. (3) Already-resolved icons bypass the breaker: `icon()` returns a cached data URI at icons.ts:151-153 before `resolveFromSources` runs, and `iconFound` is persisted indefinitely (src/core/cache/CachePolicy.ts:37 `ttlMs: 0, persist: true`, wired via `TieredCacheIconCache` at feedClients.ts:113). A suppressed source therefore still shows icons for package versions seen earlier; only not-yet-resolved versions go blank.
