On a fresh install (the owner's second machine) the persistent cache is empty, so the symptom is maximal there. Note a cache hit does not clear the counter (returns before :214), only a fresh fetch `found` does. (4) The claim understates the regression: because `entryIconUrl` (wire.ts:233) has no callers, 2.0 has no breaker-free icon source at all, unlike 1.x's search-API `iconUrl` path (:2233-2242) and un-gated nuget.org probe (:1203-1210). With 8-way concurrency "five in a row" means five `missing` answers for that source with no intervening fresh `found`, in completion order. Whether five icon-less packages actually occur in a given project is not established by code reading; keep the claim's "not reproduced read-only" hedge.
  - The claim stands on its mechanics and on the design-intent lens it is only partly answered by documented decisions, so it is not refuted; three corrections:

1. Two parts ARE documented design and must not be filed as bugs: (a) the counter resets only on a `sources`/`workspace` invalidation (ADR-009 §6; plan P2.8) — reset timing is a decision; (b) nuget.org receives no address-based exemption (ADR-005:29-31; ARCHITECTURE.md:169-171; ledger M2 names the 1.x un-gated nuget.org HEAD as the privacy defect). Consequently "restore the 1.x nuget.org carve-out" is not an available fix; any fix must be address-neutral (raise or remove the threshold, decay it per request batch or time window, or drop the breaker since the per-package 24 h miss cache already bounds fetch cost). A `nuget.org` string check would reopen M2.

2. The breaker itself — threshold 5, per source, in-memory for the session, now applied to every source — is NOT a documented decision. No ADR, no interaction-model section, no ARCHITECTURE line (ARCHITECTURE.md:85 enumerates icons.ts behaviours and omits suppression) and no STATUS ruling records the threshold or the widening from custom-sources-only (1.x :1216-1223) to all sources; STATUS.md:66 says icons.ts was "copied from the quarry working tree", so the widening is a side effect of dropping the address check, not a weighed choice. It works against interaction-model §1 ("comfortable rows (icons...)") and ADR-006's default-icon rule is per icon, not source-wide. This is an owner decision at best and a regression candidate otherwise.

3. Corrections to the claim's own text: "both surfaces" is wrong — §1 gives the sidebar no icons by design, and in the current tree only BrowseList.tsx:111 renders `iconUrl` (Installed/Updates/all-projects rows render none; that is a separate gap), so today's visible blast radius is main-panel Browse only. "Counts every `missing`" is slightly over: icons.ts:150-161 returns before `resolveFromSources` when every pending source has a cached entry, so a cached 24 h miss does not increment — only fresh `missing` answers do, and the counter dies with the session. Scope is per `nuget.config` chain (feedClients.ts:124-127, feedContexts.ts:175), not global. Reset paths include `sources/refresh`, source add/remove/enable/disable and `refresh/all` from either surface (handlers.ts:333-342, :404-407; SidebarHost.ts:112). Whether five consecutive nuget.org 404s occur in practice was not reproduced (read-only).
  - Keep DP3 but downgrade to MEDIUM and correct the trip path. The mechanism is real and a genuine 1.x->2.0 regression (1.x never counted anything against nuget.org), but 'high' overstates it: (a) in 2.0 only Browse rows render icons (BrowseList.tsx:111), so the breaker cannot explain missing icons on Installed/Updates/details/sidebar, which simply do not render iconUrl; (b) the claimed trigger (five icon-less analyzers/Abstractions/build packages) is empirically unlikely on nuget.org: 43/46 typical packages, and every named category, serve an icon from the flat container; (c) the realistic trigger is >= 5 consecutive package ids nuget.org does not know (private-feed packages, nuget.org answers 404) in a workspace without packageSourceMapping, fed by the Installed-list enrichment that shares the resolver with Browse; (d) Browse stays fully functional without icons and one Refresh resets the breaker. Wording for the gap: 'Icon breaker counts nuget.org 404s for unknown (private) ids; with >= 5 consecutive un-mapped private packages, Browse loses nuget.org icons until Refresh. Medium: silent, Browse-only, enterprise-shaped, recoverable.' Fix as proposed (exempt a source from the counter when the miss may mean unknown id, or stop suppressing a source once it has served any icon in the session; add a test in icons.test.ts next to the :224-255 case; gate npm test), est. 1-2 h, not a day. Confidence: high on mechanism and affected surface; medium on how often typical users hit the realistic trigger (no telemetry; probe is 46 + 3 packages).

### DP4 — `liteLookup` caches a transient lookup failure as "unknown" for 5 minutes, persisted

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: `getPackageSearchMetadata` wrote its caches only inside the success branch (0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:2225-2231, 2240-2241); a failed fetch reached the `catch` and returned `{}` with no cache write (:2247-2250), so the next call asked again.
- 2.0: `SearchClient.lookup` collapses `failed` and `missing` into `undefined` (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/search.ts:269-273; the interface warns, src/core/feeds/types.ts:79-83); `liteLookup` caches `found ?? null` under `lite` (src/core/catalog/feedClients.ts:88-104), and `lite` is 5 min, persist: true (src/core/cache/CachePolicy.ts:35). A 429/timeout during a first enrichment (8 concurrent lookups + icons + vulnerability fetch) yields rows without authors/verified/description for 5 minutes, surviving a reload. feedClients.test.ts:11-36 asserts the miss cache without a failed case.
- Fix: Have `liteLookup` call `search.searchById(source, id, true, signal)` and map `found` -> lite (cache), `missing` -> `null` (cache), `failed` -> `undefined` (no cache); keep `SearchClient.lookup` for callers without a cache. Enricher unchanged.
- Files: `src/core/catalog/feedClients.ts`, `src/core/catalog/feedClients.test.ts`
- Test: feedClients.test.ts: `it('liteLookup caches found and missing answers but never a failed one')` — a `failed` answer sets nothing and the next call asks again.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project backend src/core/catalog/feedClients.test.ts src/core/catalog/Catalog.test.ts && npx tsc --noEmit`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Two precision corrections to the 1.x mechanism, neither changing the verdict: (a) in 1.x a failed fetch usually did not reach the `catch` at :2247; `fetchJson`/`fetchJsonHttp1` resolve `null` on non-200 and timeouts (0457c75:src/services/NuGetService.ts:1002-1008, 1038-1043; Http2Client.ts resolves null at :550-707), so `data?.data?.length` at :2222 was falsy and `return {}` at :2250 was reached by fall-through; same outcome, no cache write. (b) 1.x did hold one negative cache on this path: the in-memory, source-level `failedEndpointCache` (120 s, NuGetService.ts:42, :339-343, :441-443). If the nuget.org service-index discovery itself failed, :2216-2217 returned `{}` for two minutes. That is per-source, memory-only and not persisted; a per-package 429/timeout on the search query was never cached, so "the next call asked again" holds for the scenario claimed. Three findings that strengthen the claim: (1) a manual refresh does not recover: `Catalog.invalidate` (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:555-562) and `FeedClients.reset()` (feedClients.ts:139-144) never touch the TieredCache; the only production `cache.clear('network')` is behind the `nuiget.clearHttpCache` command (src/extension.ts:358-363), and `network` is the scope that includes `lite:` (CachePolicy.ts:69-77). `nuiget.refreshPackages` (:347) only invalidates the coordinator and feed contexts, so the rows stay bare for the full 5 minutes. (2) 2.0 already applies the right rule one file over: the icon resolver treats a `failed` fetch as "says nothing", moves to the next source and caches nothing (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/icons.ts:205-209, finding C-feeds-2); the lite path contradicts that precedent. (3) The found/missing/failed distinction already exists in `searchById` (search.ts:256-267); `lookup` throws it away at :271-272. Fix: have `liteLookup` call `searchById` (or add a detailed lookup) and `cache.set` only on `found` and `missing`, never on `failed`; add a `failed` case to feedClients.test.ts asserting `set` is not called and the second lookup re-asks; severity medium stands.
  - The claim stands and understates the impact in three ways; one nuance narrows it.

1. The cached `null` survives a manual refresh/Retry, not only a window reload. `refresh/all` (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/handlers.ts:405-408) and the sidebar refresh (src/host/SidebarHost.ts:112) call `catalog.invalidate({kind:'workspace'})` → origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:555-562 → `feeds.reset()` (feedClients.ts:139-144 clears serviceIndex, credentials, vulnerabilities, readmeImages only). Nothing in production clears the `lite` kind except the Command Palette command `nuiget.clearHttpCache` (origin/labs/2.0:src/extension.ts:358-363 `cache.clear('network')`); `invalidationToCacheScopes` has no non-test caller. So the UX doc's Retry (interaction-model.md:67,69) cannot recover authors/verified/description for 5 minutes.

2. Blast radius crosses surfaces and views: `enricher` and `searchEnricher` share the one `liteLookup` (feedClients.ts:128,135-136), used by updates (Catalog.ts:254), transitive/installed metadata (:288, :352) and search (:443). A 429 during a search poisons installed/updates rows for the same id on both the main panel and the sidebar, and vice versa.

3. Nuance: `enricher.lookup` walks allowed sources in order and moves past a `null`-cached source (enricher.ts:85-93), so with several sources another may still answer; the 5-minute blank is certain in the common single-source (nuget.org) case.

4. Do not regress the real improvement: 1.x cached neither `failed` nor a genuine `missing` (re-asked nuget.org on every call for private-feed ids); 2.0 caching a definitive `missing` as `null` matches the P4.9a ruling and should stay.

Fix (≤1 day): change `liteLookup` (src/core/catalog/feedClients.ts:88-104) to call `search.searchById(source, packageId, true, signal)` (types.ts:194-196) and cache `found.result`-derived lite as the value, `missing` as `null`, and on `failed` return `undefined` with no `set` (mirror `icons.ts:168-172`). Widen the `Pick<SearchClient, 'lookup'>` parameter to include `searchById` (DefaultSearchClient already implements both). Add to src/core/catalog/feedClients.test.ts a case where the fake answers `{kind:'failed', reason:'timeout'}`: assert `set` is not called and a second call re-asks; keep the existing miss assertion (:20-24). Gate: `npm run test:backend` and `npm run check-types`. Severity medium is right; it is a bug for the fix phase, not an owner decision.
  - Medium stands; the claim is right on mechanism but needs three corrections. (1) It overstates the visible loss: a cached null does not strip "authors/verified/description" from Installed/Updates rows — Installed renders only description (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124) and Updates only the Verified badge (UpdatesTab.tsx:191); authors/verified on Installed rows are a separate steady-state gap. (2) It understates recovery: the 5-minute TTL is not the only bound — the Refresh button/command and sidebar refresh never clear `lite:` (Catalog.invalidate touches no TieredCache key; `invalidationToCacheScopes`/`clearPackages` are unwired), so only `nuiget.clearHttpCache` or expiry recovers, whereas 1.x Refresh (clearSourceErrors + re-ask) did; this is the strongest reason it is not low. (3) 1.x was not immune: a service-index discovery failure was negatively cached in memory for 2 min (NuGetService.ts:340,443); "the next call asked again" holds only for failures after the index resolved. Not high: intermittent trigger, self-heals, ~10-line fix (use `searchById` in `liteLookup`, cache only found/missing, add the failed case to feedClients.test.ts). The fix item should also name wiring `invalidationToCacheScopes` into `Catalog.invalidate` as a sibling item so Refresh clears `lite:`/`versions:`.

### DP5 — Default package icon is never rendered: the host plumbs `data-package-icon`, no webview reads it (ADR-006 promises it)

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: NuGetPanel injected `data-package-icon` on `#root` (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:1397,1409); App.tsx:22 read it; every row drew the 32px default when `iconUrl` was absent or failed to load (InstalledTab.tsx:186-191, UpdatesTab.tsx:689-695, App.tsx:3061-3064).
- 2.0: `renderWebviewHtml` still emits `data-package-icon` for `resources/package-icon.png` (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/services.ts:106-108; the file ships, src/test/lint/vsix-contents.test.ts:23), but src/webview/main/index.tsx:16-27 never reads `rootElement.dataset`, no primitive renders a fallback, and BrowseList.tsx:111 passes `icon={undefined}` for anything but a data URI. docs/adr/006-icons-and-csp.md:17-18 says the webview shows the bundled default icon. Once DP1/DP2 land, rows with and without icons misalign.
- Fix: Read `rootElement.dataset.packageIcon` in index.tsx and hand it down (a `StoreProvider` prop or the config slice); `PackageIcon({ src?: string })` renders `<img alt="" src={src?.startsWith('data:') ? src : fallback}>` at `--nuiget-icon-size-lg` with `onError` -> fallback; `webview.cspSource` already admits the resource URI (services.ts:113). Place the component under `src/webview/main/` (not the shared `primitives/index.ts` barrel) or mark it `/* @__PURE__ */`, so `dist/sidebar.js` does not grow (N63 ceiling, `esbuild.mjs`, enforced by the `e2e-v2` build step). Use it in BrowseList and in DP1/DP2.
- Files: `src/webview/main/index.tsx`, `src/webview/main/PackageIcon.tsx`, `src/webview/main/PackageIcon.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/main.css`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.test.tsx`
- Test: PackageIcon.test.tsx: `renders the data URI and falls back to the bundled icon otherwise`; adjust BrowseList.test.tsx:79-80 (a non-data URL now renders the fallback, not nothing).
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview && npx vitest run --project backend src/host/handlers.test.ts && rm -rf dist && node esbuild.mjs --v2 --production --analyze (sidebar bytes unchanged, webview under its ceiling)`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim is correct; only citation precision: the 1.x row files are at src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx and src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx (not a tabs/ folder), with the default-icon img lines at InstalledTab.tsx:188/190 (top-level), 1459/1461 and 1570/1572 (transitive, 24px `.package-icon-small`), and UpdatesTab.tsx:611/613 and 691/693. "32px" holds for top-level and browse rows (App.css:92-104); transitive rows drew it at 24px (App.css:3061-3071). Additional facts the fix phase needs: the 2.0 CSP already permits the cspSource URI (services.ts:114), and BrowseList.test.tsx:80-81 pins the current no-img behaviour and must be updated, not weakened, by the fix.
  - The claim stands; three precision fixes: (1) the 1.x tab files are at src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx and src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx (not tabs/); the cited line ranges are correct, and the App.tsx block is 3060-3065. (2) The misalignment is not conditional on DP1/DP2 landing: it already exists in BrowseList today, because Row.tsx:97 emits the 24px icon span (main.css:200-204, `--nuiget-icon-size-lg`) only for rows whose `iconUrl` is a data: URI, so a result set mixing resolved and unresolved icons has staggered titles now. (3) Scope the gap to main-panel comfortable rows; the sidebar's icon-less compact rows are documented intent (interaction-model.md §1) and must not be "fixed".
  - Claim stands at medium; four corrections. (1) It understates the present effect: rows already misalign TODAY in BrowseList, the one comfortable list that passes `icon` — an icon-less row's name starts 24px plus `--nuiget-space-3` further left than its neighbours' (Row.tsx:97; primitives.css:88-98,195-198). It is not something that only appears "once DP1/DP2 land". (2) `undefined` is systematic per source, not per package: `ICON_SOURCE_MISS_THRESHOLD = 5` (src/core/feeds/icons.ts:24) with a 24h negative cache (icons.ts:22) skips a whole source after five consecutive icon-less packages until `reset()` (icons.ts:191-199), and `resolveFromSources` has no nuget.org exemption — so the fallback is reached far more often than "the package has no icon". (3) 2.0 draws browse icons at `--nuiget-icon-size-lg` = 24px (shared/tokens.css:31; main/main.css:200-204), not 1.x's 32px; the default must be sized 24px. (4) The fix flips `BrowseList.test.tsx:80-81` (currently asserts the non-data row has no `<img>`) — update it to assert the default src, and extend Row.test.tsx:122-128; do not delete. Proposed ≤1-day fix: read `document.getElementById('root')?.dataset.packageIcon` once in a shared module (src/webview/shared/defaultIcon.ts) passed via StoreProvider or imported; in Row.tsx render `.nuiget-row-icon` for every comfortable row with `icon ?? <img src={defaultIcon} alt="" />` and an `onError` swap to the default; give `.nuiget-row-icon` a fixed `inline-size/block-size: var(--nuiget-icon-size-lg)`; drop the `startsWith('data:')` ternary in BrowseList.tsx:111 in favour of `iconUrl ?? default`. Gate: `npm run test:frontend` plus `npm run package:vsix`.

### DP6 — Transitive rows: the single-project section drops the icon/authors/Verified it holds; the All Projects aggregate never asks for lite metadata (no Vulnerable badge either)

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: Single project: `getTransitiveMetadata` -> `fetchTransitivePackageMetadata` (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:369-385) and rows with a small icon, authors and Verified (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:1457-1475). All Projects: `getAllProjectsTransitiveMetadata` for the unique (id, version) pairs after the stream completed (App.tsx:1206-1227), merged (:1158-1189), rendered (InstalledTab.tsx:1562-1590).
- 2.0: Single project: src/webview/shared/store/transitive.tsx:60-90 asks `transitive/metadata` and stores iconUrl/verified/authors/vulnerabilities, but TransitiveSection.tsx:72-82 renders only "Required by" and the Vulnerable badge, in compact density where `Row` renders no icon (Row.tsx:97) and hides the description visually (primitives.css:186-193). All Projects: AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx imports only `useAllProjects` (:5), never requests `transitive/metadata`, and its rows (:122-131) carry no badge at all, although the host answers any package list (handlers.ts:289; Catalog.ts:281-289).
- Fix: Extract the ask of transitive.tsx:60-90 into a `useLiteMetadata(pairs)` hook (abort on change, one request per distinct pair set); call it from the aggregate when expanded with the unique (id, version) pairs of `aggregateTransitive` (1.x dedupe, App.tsx:1209-1220). Render half, decided: switch both transitive sections to `density="comfortable"` (1.x parity: icon + "Required by …" + `by authors`, Vulnerable and Verified badges visible); the Listbox is virtualized so long lists stay cheap. If the owner prefers compact transitive rows, render badges only (Vulnerable + Verified) — a compact row shows no description or icon.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/store/transitive.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/TransitiveSection.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/AllProjectsView.test.tsx`
- Test: InstalledTab.test.tsx:207 extended with authors/Verified/icon on the single-project rows; AllProjectsView.test.tsx: `it('the aggregated transitive section asks transitive/metadata once for the unique pairs and shows Vulnerable and Verified')`.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/installed src/webview/shared/store && npx tsc --noEmit`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; four refinements. (1) Root cause differs per mode: single project is exactly feature-not-rendered plus primitive/CSS (slice holds iconUrl/verified/authors, `TransitiveSection.tsx:72-82` passes none, compact `Row` cannot show an icon per `Row.tsx:97`); All Projects is "not requested / not stored in slice" — `allProjects.tsx` never asks `transitive/metadata` and the `allProjects/transitive` stream has no metadata chunk kind (`messages.ts:131` vs `:129`), so the fix is webview-side (post-stream `transitive/metadata` ask like `transitive.tsx:66-84`, or a new chunk kind); the host already answers (`handlers.ts:289`, `Catalog.ts:285-289`). (2) The "no Vulnerable badge either" point is a 2.0-internal inconsistency, not a 1.x regression: 1.x showed no vulnerability badge on transitive rows (none in `0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx` 1380-1600; the `vulnerability-badge` at `:205` is the top-level installed row). The single-project Vulnerable badge (`TransitiveSection.tsx:79-81`) is a 2.0 improvement to preserve while bringing All Projects to match. (3) Visually the 2.0 single-project row shows only name + version (+ Vulnerable): the "Required by" description is clipped by the compact rule at `primitives.css:186-192`, so the claim's "renders only Required by and Vulnerable" is true at JSX level but the user sees less. (4) Parity target is 1.x's deliberately slim transitive row (`App.css:3022` "Slim row design", 24px icon, 10px authors), not the full comfortable installed row; the work item must decide between `density="comfortable"` on `Row` (matches interaction-model §1 line 11) or a third slim variant that still renders `icon` and a Verified/authors badge.
  - DP6 stands as a regression, not an owner decision, with these precise corrections. (1) Root cause splits: single project = feature-not-rendered PLUS a density choice — the ruling left density open, but compact + the Row primitive defeats both ruled row contents (the icon can never render, Row.tsx:97, and the ruled "Required by" description is visually hidden, primitives.css:186-193); All Projects = data never requested (no `transitive/metadata` ask anywhere in allProjects.tsx or AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx), not "not rendered". (2) Verified is not a density casualty: compact rows do show badges (primitives.css:185 comment) and BrowseList.tsx:114 already renders Verified as a Badge, so its omission is feature-level; authors needs the description slot or comfortable density. Neither authors nor Verified was ruled in or out for 2.0 transitive rows (the P5.4 spec lists id, version, Required-by, icon) — undocumented, hence not a decision either. (3) The claim's "renders only Required by and the Vulnerable badge" overstates what a sighted user sees: in compact density the row shows id, version and (when present) Vulnerable; Required by is AT-only. (4) The gap's status: recorded in the P5.4 handoff as a deviation with a deferral ("descriptions come with P5.7"; icons "until P4") that no later item closed — an unfinished deferral the owner was never asked to accept. (5) 2.0 does better and the fix must not regress it: a Vulnerable badge on single-project transitive rows (1.x rows had none, InstalledTab.tsx:1457-1476); rows appear at once and enrich afterwards (transitive.tsx:65-85) where 1.x replaced the list with "Loading package details..." per framework (InstalledTab.tsx:1430-1434); metadata keyed by id@version with stale answers dropped (transitive.tsx:102). (6) Adjacent gap to file separately, not inside DP6: transitive rows have no onSelect/onActivate (TransitiveSection.tsx:64-84, AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx:114-132) and details.tsx:16 knows no transitive origin, whereas 1.x opened the details pane on click (InstalledTab.tsx:1453-1455, 1556-1565). Suggested fix shape: switch both transitive Listboxes to comfortable (interaction model §1), pass icon/authors/Verified from the slice metadata, add a `transitive/metadata` ask in the allProjects slice for the aggregate's unique (id, version) pairs after the stream completes (1.x App.tsx:1206-1227 pattern), tests in InstalledTab.test.tsx/AllProjectsView.test.tsx answering the ask and asserting the icon/badge, gate `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/installed src/webview/shared/store`.
  - Two corrections, neither changing the severity. (a) "no Vulnerable badge either" in All Projects is not a 1.x parity gap: 1.x transitive rows never showed a vulnerability badge (the only vulnerability badge in 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx is line 205-215 inside PackageRowContent, the top-level row; the transitive blocks at 1457-1475 and 1562-1590 have none). It is a 2.0-internal inconsistency: single-project TransitiveSection.tsx:79-81 shows Vulnerable (an improvement over 1.x the fix must keep), AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx:122-131 does not. (b) Root cause is split: single project = feature-not-rendered (metadata is in the slice, Row gates icon on comfortable density); All Projects = data never requested by the slice/feature (no `transitive/metadata` call), so the fix is TSX + store work with a new test, not CSS. Additional support for medium, not in the claim: because compact rows clip `.nuiget-row-description` and the sections pass no onSelect, 2.0 shows "Required by"/"N projects" only to screen readers and offers no transitive details view, whereas 1.x had a clickable details panel.

### DP7 — Comfortable Row is a single line: authors/description would share one line with the version, right-aligned, nowrap, no ellipsis (shared with the row-rendering area)

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `primitive-lacks` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: `.package-item` = 32px icon + `.package-info` with stacked name/badges, version and authors lines at 12px/16px padding (0457c75:src/webview/app/App.css:70-125, 559-563).
- 2.0: `.nuiget-row-option` is one flex line (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:90-100); `.nuiget-row-desc` is pushed to the right with `margin-inline-start: auto` (:177-183); `.nuiget-row-description` is `white-space: nowrap` without an overflow rule (:156-162); the row is 44px (src/webview/shared/tokens.css:26) and the icon 24px (src/webview/main/main.css:200-204). After DP1/DP2 the extra text is clipped or overflows in the ~50% list pane; a plausible reason the owner also saw no description where it is rendered.
- Fix: For `.nuiget-row--comfortable` only, lay the option out as a two-row grid: the icon spans both rows, row 1 = title + badges, row 2 = description/status with `overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis`; compact rows unchanged; no Row API change (classes only). Coordinate with the row-rendering area, which owns the rest of the layout.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx`, `src/test/ui/v2/theme.ui.test.ts`
- Test: Row.test.tsx structural assertion for the comfortable layout; `src/test/lint/webview-css-baseline.test.ts` and `webview-tokens.test.ts` stay green; theme UI test re-run.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/primitives src/test/lint && NUIGET_V2=1 npm run test:ui:v2 -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui-v2 (environment-dependent)`

### DP8 — Vulnerable badge carries no severity or count

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.25 d
- 1.18.9: Badge class `vuln-<maxSeverity>` with a count in the title (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:205-215).
- 2.0: `<Badge label="Vulnerable" tone="error" title="Has known vulnerabilities" />` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx:131; TransitiveSection.tsx:80) although `vulnerabilities[].severity` is stored (src/protocol/domain.ts:136-141).
- Fix: `maxSeverity(vulnerabilities)` in rules.ts; label `Vulnerable (Critical)`, title `2 known vulnerabilities, highest Critical`; tone error for High/Critical, warning otherwise.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/installed/rules.ts`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/TransitiveSection.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`
- Test: Extend the `@vulnerable` test (InstalledTab.test.tsx:229) to assert the severity in the accessible description.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/installed`

### DP9 — Details pane lost the Verified mark next to Authors

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `feature-not-rendered` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: `Author(s):` row with the Verified icon from the row's search data (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/PackageDetailsPanel.tsx:429-437).
- 2.0: `PackageMetadata` has no `verified`/`iconUrl` (origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/domain.ts:116-134); `searchResultToMetadata` drops them (src/core/feeds/metadataResolver.ts:35-44); `DetailsSelection` carries no lite data (src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx:9-19); DetailsInfo.tsx:25-27 lists Authors only and DetailsPane.tsx:189 shows the bare id.
- Fix: Add an optional `lite?: Pick<PackageMetadataLite, 'iconUrl' | 'verified' | 'authors'>` to the webview-local `DetailsSelection` (no protocol change), fill it at every `select(...)` call site from the row, render icon + Verified badge in the header. Do not add fields to `PackageMetadata` (src/protocol/** is frozen).
- Files: `src/webview/shared/store/details.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/details/DetailsPane.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/AllProjectsUpdates.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx`
- Test: DetailsPane.test.tsx: `it('the header shows the icon and Verified of the row that selected the package')`.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/features/details && npx tsc --noEmit`

### DP10 — The sidebar pays for a lite enrichment it never renders

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Sidebar used `liteMode` and skipped enrichment (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:317; 0457c75:src/services/NuGetOperations.ts:582, 621).
- 2.0: `InstalledProvider` sits in the shared `StoreProvider` (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/StoreProvider.tsx:26) mounted by src/webview/sidebar/index.tsx:19; its effect asks `transitive/metadata` for every installed list change (src/webview/shared/store/installed.tsx:70-87) while no sidebar component reads `metadata` (git grep over src/webview/sidebar: none). The `lite`, icon and vulnerability caches make the second pass cheap, but it is a full enrich pass per surface.
- Fix: Make the ask opt-in (`enableMetadata()` as transitive.tsx does with `enable`), called once from the main panel's InstalledTab mount; measure first (Output channel `[perf]` lines) — if the cached second pass is negligible, close as no-op.
- Files: `src/webview/shared/store/installed.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/store/installed.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.tsx`, `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarView.test.tsx`
- Test: installed.test.tsx: `asks transitive/metadata only once a consumer enabled it`; SidebarView.test.tsx: `the sidebar never asks transitive/metadata`.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared/store src/webview/sidebar`

**Better in 2.0 (do not regress):**
- Icons are host-fetched `data:` URIs with auth, outbound policy, a 256 KB cap and image-type sniffing (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/feeds/icons.ts:1-5, 98-121, 268-277; CSP src/host/services.ts:111-118): private-feed icons work and the webview opens no connection. 1.x's badge-host allow-list blocked them (ledger S6; 0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:1403).
- Lite lookups go only to the sources the package-source mapping allows, in order (src/core/feeds/enricher.ts:84-93; src/core/feeds/search.ts:256-267; policy.test.ts); 1.x hard-wired `https://api.nuget.org/v3/index.json` for every installed package (0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:2215-2220; ledger M2). The render fixes must not reintroduce a nuget.org default.
- Installed rows receive a `description` and live `vulnerabilities` from every resolved source (enricher.ts:54-73; types.ts:127-131); 1.x `InstalledPackage` had no description at all (0457c75:src/services/NuGetTypes.ts:36-54).
- All Projects Updates rows already carry verified/authors (src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:376-381 over computeUpdates) where 1.x's `checkPackageUpdatesMinimal` carried none (0457c75:src/services/NuGetPackageService.ts:2114-2122); DP2 is webview-only.
- `updates/get` streams bare `found` rows before enrichment and answers the enriched rows once (Catalog.ts:227-230, 253-269; src/webview/shared/store/updates.tsx:59, 87), so the list appears before the lookups finish and `check` stays feed-cheap (src/core/feeds/updates.ts:4-6). Trade-off to keep in mind: 1.x streamed each update already decorated (`packageUpdateFound`, 0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:1056-1063 forwarding NuGetPackageService.ts:2087-2096), so in 2.0 icons/authors arrive in one flip at the terminal answer — DP2's test asserts exactly that; do not move enrichment back into `check`.
- Lite metadata and icons live in the TieredCache with declared lifetimes (`lite` 5 min persisted, `iconFound` forever, `iconMissing` 24 h; src/core/cache/CachePolicy.ts:35-38; feedClients.ts:88-104; icons.ts:80-96), and a transient feed failure never caches a 24 h icon miss (icons.ts:205-209; flatContainer.ts:176-181).
- Per-package isolation: one failing enrichment leaves that row bare (enricher.ts:74-79); a failed All Projects enrichment logs and the list stays (Catalog.ts:356-358); the lite ask is aborted when the rows change (installed.tsx:76-86; enricher.ts:49-52).
- Package metadata is cached per source (`resourceKeys.metadata(id, version, source.url)`, Catalog.ts:490-516), closing the 1.x cross-feed bleed (ledger F6).
- One icon resolution per (id, version, source set) in flight shared by every caller (icons.ts:163-176, 236-262), and one enrichment pass per project in All Projects (Catalog.ts:340-359), instead of 1.x's separate `resolveAllProjectsIcons` round (0457c75:src/services/NuGetOperations.ts:728-756).

**Notes:**
- FIELD TABLE — iconUrl. 1.x Installed (single): sent in Phase 2 `installedPackagesMetadata` (0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.ts:316-325 <- NuGetPackageService.ts:448-479), merged App.tsx:443-466, rendered InstalledTab.tsx:186-191. 1.x Installed (all): sent `allProjectsInstalledProjectMetadata` (NuGetPanel.ts:1162-1178), merged App.tsx:975-1010, rendered InstalledTab.tsx:1343. 1.x Updates (single): sent (NuGetPackageService.ts:2080-2095), rendered UpdatesTab.tsx:689-695. 1.x Updates (all): sent `allProjectsIcons` (NuGetPanel.ts:1087-1097), merged App.tsx:1241-1252, rendered UpdatesTab.tsx:609-615. 2.0 Installed (single): SENT as a data URI by `transitive/metadata` (handlers.ts:289 -> Catalog.ts:285-289 -> enricher.ts:56,68-70), STORED installed.tsx:77-84, DROPPED at InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136 (no `icon` prop). 2.0 Installed (all): SENT as the `metadata` chunk (Catalog.ts:340-359), STORED allProjects.tsx:119-123, DROPPED same place. 2.0 Updates (single): SENT on the terminal rows (Catalog.ts:259-261), STORED updates.tsx:87, DROPPED at UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196. 2.0 Updates (all): SENT in the `project` chunk rows and again as the `icons` chunk (Catalog.ts:381-390), STORED allProjects.tsx:203-205, DROPPED at AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-122 (and the `icons` map is unread). Upstream, DP3 can make the host send `undefined` for every icon.
- FIELD TABLE — verified. 1.x: sent for Installed single/all and Updates single (same sites as iconUrl), rendered as the Verified icon beside authors (InstalledTab.tsx:237-239, :1471-1473; UpdatesTab.tsx:705-707); not sent for Updates (all) (`checkPackageUpdatesMinimal`, NuGetPackageService.ts:2114-2122). 2.0: sent and stored everywhere (enricher.ts:59-61 via search.ts:274-278; Catalog.ts:262-264); RENDERED only on single-project Updates rows (UpdatesTab.tsx:191); DROPPED at InstalledProjectSection.tsx:129-132 and AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:118.
- FIELD TABLE — authors. 1.x: sent for Installed single/all and Updates single, rendered as the authors line (InstalledTab.tsx:235-241; UpdatesTab.tsx:703-709); Updates (all) had none (UpdatesTab.tsx:616-623). 2.0: sent and stored everywhere (enricher.ts:62-64; Catalog.ts:265-267); DROPPED at InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136, UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 and AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-122 — never rendered on an Installed or Updates row.
- FIELD TABLE — description. 1.x: not on `InstalledPackage` or `PackageUpdate` (0457c75:src/services/NuGetTypes.ts:36-54, 67-75), never on a row (details only). 2.0 Installed: SENT (search.ts:274-275 via `lookup`; enricher.ts:65-67), STORED, RENDERED (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124) — better than 1.x when it arrives. 2.0 Updates: not on `PackageUpdate` (domain.ts:96-104) — parity. See the runtime note below for why the owner may still have seen none.
- FIELD TABLE — vulnerabilities. 1.x Installed: sent (NuGetPackageService.ts:514-523), rendered with max severity and count (InstalledTab.tsx:205-215). 2.0 Installed: sent with live data from every resolved source (enricher.ts:57,71-73), stored, rendered as a severity-less badge (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:131; DP8). Updates: n/a in both.
- FIELD TABLE — totalDownloads. Neither version carries it on Installed/Updates rows (1.x NuGetTypes.ts:36-54, 67-75; 2.0 domain.ts:47-67, 96-104, and `PackageMetadataLite` domain.ts:189-197 has none); Browse only in both. No gap.
- FIELD TABLE — default icon. 1.x: `data-package-icon` (NuGetPanel.ts:1397,1409) -> App.tsx:22 -> every row (InstalledTab.tsx:186-191). 2.0: emitted by services.ts:106-108, read by nothing (main/index.tsx:16-27) — DP5.
- Runtime note on the owner's 'no description' for Installed rows: no code drop explains it — description is wired end to end (handlers.ts:289 -> enricher.ts:65-67 -> installed.tsx:77-84 -> InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124). Candidates: DP4 (a failed first lookup cached as `null` for 5 min, persisted), every source excluded/unhealthy so `enrich` gets `resolved = []` (Catalog.ts:287-288), or DP7 clipping the right-aligned nowrap text. To check on the second machine: Output channel lines `[Enrich] … failed` / `Lite metadata of … could not be read` and the `transitive/metadata` response in the webview developer tools. Not reproducible read-only.
- Dead contract fields: `InstalledPackage.iconUrl/verified/authors/vulnerabilities` (origin/labs/2.0:src/protocol/domain.ts:63-66) and `TransitivePackage.iconUrl/verified/authors` (domain.ts:163-165) are filled by nothing in 2.0 (git grep over src/core/project, Catalog.ts, updates.ts: no writer); lite data travels in `PackageMetadataLite` instead. `src/protocol/**` is frozen (contract-change sentinel) — leave them, record in the ledger.
- The `allProjects/updates` `icons` chunk duplicates the `iconUrl` already on each row (Catalog.ts:381-390). Harmless and frozen; the render fix should read the rows and treat the map as a fallback.
- No P5 ruling dropped icons/authors from Installed/Updates rows: the P5.4 row and ruling (docs/plans/STATUS.md:62, 2206-2223) and the P5.5 row (:76, 'the terminal rows carry iconUrl/verified/authors') speak of data, never of rendering it; the feature tests assert only the CPM and Vulnerable descriptions (InstalledTab.test.tsx:67, 238; UpdatesTab.test.tsx:66) while BrowseList.test.tsx:67-82 asserts icon/authors/Verified. Omission, not a decision.
- Sidebar rows (compact: no icons, no descriptions) match interaction-model.md:12 and 1.x's sidebar Installed/Updates rows, which passed no description (0457c75:src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1526-1531, 1596-1601, 1682-1688). 1.x sidebar Browse rows did show a description (SidebarApp.tsx:1484; PackageRow.tsx:121-125) and 2.0 BrowseResults.tsx:173-185 does not — Browse area, not this one.
- Enrichment cost is comparable: 1.x one nuget.org `packageid:` search per package plus a HEAD probe at concurrency 16 (NuGetPackageService.ts:448-512); 2.0 one lookup per allowed source until found, one icon fetch and the vulnerability data at concurrency 8 (enricher.ts:23, 54-58), all cached. Both deliver Installed metadata as one answer after every package finished (installed.tsx:77; NuGetPanel.ts:316-325).
- Bundle ceilings are a CI gate (N63; `esbuild.mjs`, checked by the `e2e-v2` build step): any new shared primitive for DP1/DP2/DP5 must stay out of `dist/sidebar.js` (place it under `src/webview/main/` or mark it `/* @__PURE__ */`) and the implementer pastes `node esbuild.mjs --v2 --production --analyze` sizes as the P-items did.
- The owner's CI failure was not investigated here (outside this area; no CI logs are readable from the repository). Draft of this answer is at /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-mattias-github-claude-worktrees-nuiget-audit-modernization-plan-review-bca33d/be213251-34a1-4e7c-9c88-7ce5a7cbe53a/scratchpad/dp-gaps-draft.json (scratch copy, not a deliverable file).

## Gates and tests: why the 2.0 UI regression passed every gate, plus the All Projects ExTester flake (CI run 38914547552)

### TF1 — ExTester main-panel suites assert ids, roles and text only; no row content (icon, authors, Verified, description) is checked, so bare rows pass

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `other` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: 1.x rendered icon + authors + Verified in Installed and Updates rows (0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:181-240; 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/UpdatesTab.tsx:689-708). Its ExTester suite never asserted that content either: a grep of 0457c75:src/test/ui for icon/author/verified matches only the Activity Bar icon (0457c75:src/test/ui/activation.ui.test.ts:5-36); 0457c75:src/test/ui/search-flow.ui.test.ts:27-31 slept 5 s and asserted only that the app was alive.
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-installed.ui.test.ts:49-54 checks the listbox role, aria-multiselectable and that package-row-serilog / package-row-newtonsoft.json exist; origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-updates.ui.test.ts:45-46 checks textContent contains '13.0.2 ->'; origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-search.ui.test.ts:72 checks textContent contains the id; origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-details.ui.test.ts:28-35 waits for text. Nothing reads an icon, an authors line or a badge. The product passes no icon, authors or Verified to installed rows (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136: description only) and no icon/authors to update rows (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196: Verified badge only), although the installed store holds lite metadata with iconUrl/verified/authors (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/installed.tsx:77-93) and the host enriches updates with iconUrl/verified/authors (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:255-268). Only BrowseList wires icon/authors/Verified (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:109-116) and even its test asserts text only. Result: 32 passing on every run (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/STATUS.md). The tracker scoped each feature's ExTester test to 'happy path over the §8 ids' (origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/STATUS.md:76).
- Fix: (1) Row.tsx: add data-testid="row-icon" on the icon span (Row.tsx:97) and data-testid="row-description" on the description span (Row.tsx:105); add an authors slot or keep authors inside description, but give the authors text its own span data-testid="row-authors". Badge.tsx: data-testid={`badge-${label.toLowerCase()}`}. (2) interaction-model.md §8: rows for row-icon, row-authors, row-description, badge-<name> (verified, cpm, vulnerable, installed). (3) main-installed: after package-row-newtonsoft.json appears, assert within it: row-icon contains an img (executeScript: complete && naturalWidth > 0, which also catches a CSP-blocked icon under services.ts:113), row-authors text non-empty, badge-verified present. (4) main-updates: same three checks on the update row. (5) main-search: same on the browse row (currently only textContent). Run the suite before the feature fix: the new assertions must fail (negative control), then pass after the installed/updates features pass icon/authors/verified to Row.
- Files: `src/test/ui/v2/main-installed.ui.test.ts`, `src/test/ui/v2/main-updates.ui.test.ts`, `src/test/ui/v2/main-search.ui.test.ts`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Badge.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: New assertions in the three ExTester files; a negative control run on the current labs/2.0 build must show them red.
- Gate: `rm -rf dist && npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui (CI job UI green; the new assertions red on the unfixed build)`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Claim stands; three precision fixes. (1) "even its test asserts text only" is true only of the ExTester test (main-search.ui.test.ts:72). The jsdom suite does assert BrowseList row content: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.test.tsx:67-73 ("lists the results with the metadata that came first: icon, authors, downloads, Verified and Installed"), and origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx:125-128 asserts the icon slot renders in comfortable density and not in compact. Store tests also prove authors/verified arrive (shared/store/updates.test.tsx:20,75; shared/store/search.test.tsx:23,77-79). No jsdom test under features/installed or features/updates asserts icon/authors/Verified (grep: zero hits), consistent with those features not rendering them. So the gap is "no ExTester assertion anywhere + no assertion of any kind for Installed/Updates rows", not "no row-content test at all". (2) Line drift: main-installed.ui.test.ts assertions are at 44-48, not 49-54 (49-54 is the start of the selection test); the updates assertion text is '13.0.2 →' (arrow glyph), not '13.0.2 ->'; 1.x blocks span InstalledTab.tsx:181-246 and UpdatesTab.tsx:689-710. (3) BrowseList renders an icon only when `iconUrl` starts with `data:` (BrowseList.tsx:111), so even browse rows with an https icon URL show none; a fix for Installed/Updates should not copy that guard blindly.
  - The claim stands, with the finding stratified and the root cause relabelled.

1. §1 contradictions — regressions, not owner decisions: no icon on Installed, Updates or Transitive rows (interaction-model §1 "icons"; Row.tsx:37 accepts `icon`; the P5.4 ruling at STATUS.md:2229 dictated "icon from the slice's metadata when present" for transitive rows and TransitiveSection.tsx:72-78 never passes it); no description on Updates rows (§1 "description"). The model was never amended (one commit touches that line, b8a97a8) and no Deviations section or ruling records dropping them.

2. 1.x parity items NOT in §1 — owner decides: a separate authors line (the model lists authors only under §7 Details; BrowseList folds authors into `description`, one acceptable reading), and a Verified badge on Installed rows (§1 says "badges" generically; Installed already renders CPM/Vulnerable). Report these as medium parity gaps for the owner, not as design violations.

3. Root cause: not "other". Primary = "not rendered by feature": the host sends iconUrl/verified/authors/description on both `transitive/metadata` (Catalog.ts:288 → enricher.ts:54-69) and `updates/get` (Catalog.ts:254-269); the installed slice stores it (installed.tsx:77-84); InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136, UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 and TransitiveSection.tsx:72-78 simply do not pass it to Row. Secondary = the documented ExTester scope ("happy path over the §8 ids", STATUS.md:76-82; plan P5.12; ADR-008:40; P5.12b decision 7), which let the omission ship. The fix is a scope extension, not a reversal of that decision.

4. Two factual corrections to the claim's own text: InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136 renders description + CPM + Vulnerable badges, not "description only"; and BrowseList's RTL test (BrowseList.test.tsx:67-81) does assert `img src`, authors and Verified — only its ExTester test (main-search.ui.test.ts:72) is text-only. Do not say CI captures "only failure screenshots" (ExTester's default was not verified); say no v2 suite takes a screenshot and nothing in the tracker records a visual review standing in for the assertion.

5. ≤1-day fix item (files, tests, gates): in InstalledProjectSection.tsx pass `icon` (data: URI pattern of BrowseList.tsx:111) and a Verified badge from `metadata.get(id)`; in UpdatesTab.tsx pass `icon={update.iconUrl…}` and `description` from `update.authors`; in TransitiveSection.tsx pass `icon` per the P5.4 ruling. RTL tests mirroring BrowseList.test.tsx:67 in InstalledTab.test.tsx and UpdatesTab.test.tsx ("lists the rows with icon, Verified and description from the lite metadata"). ExTester: one `img` presence + description/Verified textContent assertion on `package-row-newtonsoft.json` in main-installed.ui.test.ts and main-updates.ui.test.ts. Gates: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main`; `npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui`. Severity high stays: the test-fidelity gap let a §1 violation reach the release.
  - Severity: medium, not high — and file it as a guardrail note under the Installed/Updates row-content product gap, not as a standalone parity gap. It is not a regression: 1.x asserted icon/authors/Verified in no ExTester test and in no InstalledTab/UpdatesTab jsdom test either; 2.0 coverage is strictly stronger at both layers (roles, aria, version arrows, csproj round-trips; BrowseList icon/authors/Verified assertions). Record under "2.0 does better": main-installed/main-updates ExTester assertions and BrowseList.test.tsx:67-80. Fix (≤1 h, not CSS): wire icon/authors/verified from the metadata already in the store into InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136 and UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196, then add two jsdom assertions mirroring BrowseList.test.tsx:67-80 (img src = data: ICON, accessible description contains 'by <authors>', badge 'Verified') to InstalledTab.test.tsx and UpdatesTab.test.tsx using the existing hostSays scripted-host pattern; gate: `npm run test:frontend`. Do not add icon/authors assertions to the ExTester suites (live-feed dependent, flaky); optionally add a §8 hook `package-row-icon` so interaction-model-testids.test.ts enforces that comfortable rows render it.

### TF2 — jsdom proves the Row primitive and BrowseList carry metadata, but no Installed, Updates or All Projects test asserts icon, authors or Verified reach the row

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `other` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: Row content lived inline in 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:181-240; whether 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.test.tsx asserted it was not checked.
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx:39-41 proves description and badges render when given; origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.test.tsx:67-82 asserts icon, authors, downloads, Verified and Installed for browse rows. For the lists the owner saw: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx:67 and :238 assert only 'Centrally managed' and 'Has known vulnerabilities'; origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.test.tsx:66 only 'Centrally managed'; origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/AllProjectsView.test.tsx:81 only 'required by'. Dropping icon/authors from InstalledProjectSection or UpdatesTab breaks no unit test.
- Fix: Mirror BrowseList.test.tsx:67-82 for each list: answer transitive/metadata (installed.tsx:77) with { id, version, iconUrl: 'data:image/png;base64,...', verified: true, authors: 'James Newton-King', description: 'Json.NET' } and updates/get with enriched rows (iconUrl, verified, authors); assert the option has an accessible description matching /by James Newton-King/ and /reserved/ (Badge title), and that the row wrapper contains .nuiget-row-icon img with the data: src. Add the All Projects variants (allProjects/installed metadata chunk, allProjects/updates icons chunk). Keep the existing CPM/vulnerable assertions.
- Files: `src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/updates/UpdatesTab.test.tsx`, `src/webview/main/features/installed/AllProjectsView.test.tsx`
- Test: One 'metadata reaches the row' test per list (installed single, installed all-projects, updates single, updates all-projects); each red on the current labs/2.0 tree.
- Gate: `npm run test:frontend`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The claim is right that no 2.0 Installed, Updates or All Projects test (jsdom, ExTester UI, or e2e) asserts icon, authors or Verified reach a row, while Row.test.tsx and BrowseList.test.tsx do prove the primitive and browse rows carry them. Four corrections: (1) "Dropping icon/authors from InstalledProjectSection or UpdatesTab breaks no unit test" is vacuous — neither component renders icon or authors at all (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:119-135, UpdatesTab.tsx:181-193, AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-118 pass no Row icon prop and no authors). The rendered-but-untested items are the Verified badge at UpdatesTab.tsx:191 and the description at InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124; dropping those breaks no test. Icon/authors not reaching those rows is a rendering gap for a separate claim, not a coverage gap. (2) The unchecked 1.x half resolves against the claim's framing: 1.x InstalledTab.test.tsx and UpdatesTab.test.tsx (0457c75) assert none of iconUrl/authors/verified/package-icon/verified-badge either, and App.test.tsx:194-199/663-668 feed those fields but assert only side effects — so this is an inherited hole, not a 2.0 regression. (3) Slice tests do cover the data path: updates.test.tsx:20,75-76 asserts authors on store rows and transitive.test.tsx:104-105 asserts iconUrl on transitive metadata; only the render step is unasserted. (4) Severity 'high' does not fit the task's definitions (high = a user would call it broken or missing): a missing test assertion is not user-visible and 1.x had the identical gap. Reclassify as a fix-phase work item: add Verified and description assertions to UpdatesTab.test.tsx and InstalledTab.test.tsx now, and add icon/authors assertions in the same files once those features render them.
  - The claim stands under the design-intent lens: the documented design (interaction-model §1, ADR-006, ARCHITECTURE.md:430, CHANGELOG.md:35, STATUS.md P4.8/P4.9 rulings) requires icons, description and badges on main-panel rows and enriches installed/updates rows with icon/verified/authors for that purpose; nothing records a decision to omit them, so the missing rendering and the missing tests are a gap for the fix phase, not an owner decision. Sharpen the claim by field: (1) Icons: mandated by two docs; no main-panel Installed/Updates row passes `icon`, and the host-injected default icon (`data-package-icon`, src/host/services.ts:106-108) is read by no 2.0 webview, contradicting ADR-006:18. (2) Verified: UpdatesTab.tsx:191 renders it (unasserted); AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:118 and InstalledProjectSection.tsx:129-132 do not — an undocumented inconsistency. (3) Description: InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124 does render it from the lite-metadata map, but no test feeds a description (InstalledTab.test.tsx:236 feeds vulnerabilities only), so the claim's "description never reaches Installed rows" framing in the task context is wrong for Installed; the test gap remains. (4) Authors: interaction-model §1 lists icons/description/badges only and §7 places authors in the Details tab; 1.x showed authors on rows (InstalledTab.tsx:230-237, UpdatesTab.tsx:703-708). Silence is not a documented decision, so this does not refute, but authors-on-rows is the one field that needs an explicit owner call (and a Row primitive slot — BrowseList.tsx:109 works around its absence by concatenating authors into `description`). Root cause is "not rendered by feature" (data is in installed.tsx:17-18 and updates.tsx:16 with slice tests proving arrival), plus "primitive lacks it" for authors — not "other". The 1.x test question is closed: 1.x InstalledTab/UpdatesTab tests never asserted icon/authors/verified on rows either (only a `defaultPackageIcon` fixture), so the coverage gap is inherited rather than newly introduced — which is exactly why the 2.0 render regression went undetected.
  - Severity: LOW as a standalone item (invisible to users; identical gap in 1.x), not high. Fold TF2 into the "test to add" line of the rendering-gap work item(s) for Installed/Updates/All Projects rows rather than listing it separately, and sequence it after the rendering fix. Factual corrections to the claim text: (a) replace "Dropping icon/authors from InstalledProjectSection or UpdatesTab breaks no unit test" with "InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-132 and UpdatesTab.tsx:181-194 never pass icon or authors to Row, so nothing can be dropped; the rendered-but-untested items are the Updates rows' Verified badge (UpdatesTab.tsx:191, no `verified` in the UpdatesTab.test.tsx:17 fixture) and the Installed rows' description (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124, InstalledTab.test.tsx:235 feeds metadata without description)"; (b) add "1.x baseline: 0457c75 InstalledTab.test.tsx/UpdatesTab.test.tsx assert none of icon/authors/verified; App.test.tsx:198-199,668 feed them as fixtures and assert only badge count/no crash; src/test/ui and src/test/e2e have zero such assertions - the gap is parity-neutral"; (c) root cause "other" -> "not rendered by feature (data present in PackageMetadataLite/PackageUpdate and in the installed/allProjects slices); test gap is a consequence". Work item: in InstalledProjectSection.tsx, UpdatesTab.tsx, AllProjectsUpdates.tsx pass `icon` (data: URI only, as BrowseList.tsx:111), prepend `by <authors>` to description (as :109) and add the Verified badge (as :114); in InstalledTab.test.tsx extend the transitive/metadata answer at :235 with iconUrl/authors/verified/description and assert `querySelector('img')` src plus accessible description /by .* · Prefix reserved/; in UpdatesTab.test.tsx give `update()` verified/authors/iconUrl and assert the same; gate `npm run test:frontend`.

### TF3 — Density contract is measured for the sidebar (compact token) but not for the main panel (comfortable rows with an icon slot)

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `other` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: 1.x had no density tokens; row layout was not measured by any UI test (0457c75:src/test/ui/*).
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/sidebar.ui.test.ts:63-81 asserts every sidebar row equals --nuiget-row-height-compact (24px, origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/tokens.css:25) and the virtual offsets; origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-shell.ui.test.ts:27-46 measures only the split and the root colour. Nothing asserts a main row reaches --nuiget-row-height-comfortable (44px, tokens.css:26) or has an icon slot; Row.tsx:97 renders .nuiget-row-icon only when density is comfortable AND an icon is passed, so a comfortable list without icons renders the same DOM as a compact one apart from min-height (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css:82-87). §1 promises comfortable rows with icons, description and badges (origin/labs/2.0:docs/ux/interaction-model.md:11).
- Fix: Add a layout probe modelled on sidebar.ui.test.ts:63-81: read the comfortable token from tokens.css, executeScript over [data-testid="installed-list"] [data-index] (and updates-list) returning height, presence of .nuiget-row-icon img, img.complete and naturalWidth; assert height >= token, icon present and loaded for every row, and that the description span is visible (getComputedStyle display !== 'none').
- Files: `src/test/ui/v2/main-installed.ui.test.ts`, `src/test/ui/v2/main-updates.ui.test.ts`
- Test: 'lays the rows out as comfortable rows with loaded icons' in main-installed and main-updates.
- Gate: `npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The claim's "measured" thesis stands as written: no test anywhere renders a main-panel row and measures its height against `--nuiget-row-height-comfortable`; the only comfortable-height check is the text-level lint origin/labs/2.0:src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts:69-77 comparing Listbox.tsx's JS constant to the token string. One sentence is overstated when read absolutely: "Nothing asserts a main row ... has an icon slot". origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.test.tsx:79-81 asserts, in jsdom, that the main browse row `package-row-serilog.sinks.console` contains an `<img src=data:...>` and that a row without a data: icon has none. Read it as: no UI test under the CSP, and no Installed/Updates feature test, asserts an icon slot or the comfortable class on a main row; only the Browse list is covered, and only in jsdom. (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx:122-128 tests the primitive's gate at Row.tsx:97 and so confirms rather than softens the claim.) Two trivial imprecisions: main-shell.ui.test.ts:27-46 also measures `display` and `rootHeight`, not "only the split and the root colour"; and a comfortable list without icons differs from compact in the class name (Row.tsx:63), with min-height being the CSS consequence. Root cause is better stated as a test-coverage gap in src/test/ui/v2 (no `tokenPx('--nuiget-row-height-comfortable')` assertion over `.nuiget-main-list [data-index]`) compounded by Installed/Updates features never passing `icon` to Row (only BrowseList.tsx:111 does), which is the sibling gap about icons/authors/verified not reaching those rows.
  - Not refuted: no document on origin/labs/2.0 records the missing main-row measurement as an intended decision, and §1 of the interaction model cuts against 2.0 here. Four corrections to the claim as written:

1. Framing: the sidebar test is not a "density contract". The P3.5 ruling (STATUS.md:2089) defined sidebar.ui.test.ts:58-82 as the proof that the stylesheet loads and tokens/CSSOM offsets resolve under the webview CSP, using the compact token as its probe; main-shell.ui.test.ts:27-46 is the same CSP proof for the main entry (P5.3 ruling, STATUS.md:2162) using --nuiget-split. The asymmetry is which CSS property each test probed, not a decision to verify one surface's density and not the other's.

2. Root cause is item scoping, not "other": main-shell.ui.test.ts was written at P5.3 when the shell had no rows ("panes are props for P5.4+", STATUS.md:61); the P5.4/P5.5/P5.6 ExTester tests were scoped to "happy path over the §8 ids" (STATUS.md:76-77), and §8 has no density or icon hook. Nothing later re-opened the question.

3. "Nothing asserts" is overstated. webview-tokens.test.ts:69-77 ties ROW_HEIGHT.comfortable to the 44px token, Row.test.tsx:122-128 asserts the comfortable icon slot in jsdom, and theme.ui.test.ts:52-54 runs axe target-size (24px) in the main panel under six themes. What is missing is specifically a browser-under-CSP measurement of a main row (height = --nuiget-row-height-comfortable, .nuiget-row-icon present) — that gap is real.

4. "Same DOM as a compact one apart from min-height" needs a CSS correction: primitives.css:185-193 also visually hides .nuiget-row-description/.nuiget-row-status in compact rows, so a comfortable Installed row with a description (InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124) is visibly distinct from compact; an Updates row (UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196: no description, no icon) is not.

Severity medium stands, and the proposed test would fail today for a reason outside the test area: Installed and Updates rows pass no icon although the host sends iconUrl (domain.ts:63,90,100,163,192) and the slices store it (installed.tsx:17, updates.tsx:16); the P5.4 ruling's "icon from the slice's metadata when present" for transitive rows (STATUS.md:2229) is dead under TransitiveSection.tsx:69,77 density="compact" (Row.tsx:97). That is a §1 rendering gap for the main-panel claims, masked by this test gap; it is a defect, not an owner decision, because no ADR or ruling says main rows go without icons.
  - Mechanism stands; severity should be LOW, not medium. By the task's scale (high = user calls it broken; medium = clearly worse; low = cosmetic) a missing UI assertion is invisible to users, and 2.0 cannot be "clearly worse" than 1.x here because 1.x had zero layout assertions while 2.0 has the sidebar probe, a target-size axe gate on BOTH surfaces (theme.ui.test.ts:171), the ROW_HEIGHT-vs-token lint (webview-tokens.test.ts:69-77) and the Row icon-slot unit test (Row.test.tsx:122-129). The assertion the claim wants would pass on today's code (all main lists are comfortable; min-height 44px applies; stylesheet proven loaded by main-shell.ui.test.ts:40-46), so it is a regression guard, not an unenforced contract. Root cause should read "test coverage", not "other". Also correct the DOM statement: comfortable vs compact without an icon differ in min-height AND in description/status visibility (primitives.css:185-193). Reclassify as a low test-hygiene item and fold its fix into the icon-wiring gap's gate: one executeScript block in main-installed.ui.test.ts mirroring sidebar.ui.test.ts:63-78 against --nuiget-row-height-comfortable and testId installed-list (~15 lines), plus, once icons are wired, an assertion that .nuiget-row-icon exists on a row whose metadata carries an icon; gate: NUIGET_V2=1 npm run test:ui:v2 -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui-v2. Do-not-regress list: sidebar.ui.test.ts:58-82, theme.ui.test.ts target-size/contrast gates, webview-tokens.test.ts:69-77, Row.test.tsx:122-129.

### TF4 — The doc-to-code lint is one-directional and §8 has no hooks for the §1 promises, so a feature rendering less than the interaction model says is invisible to every lint

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `other` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: 1.x had no interaction model or doc lint.
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/test/lint/interaction-model-testids.test.ts:125-135 fails only when a §8 hook is rendered by no file; §8 (origin/labs/2.0:docs/ux/interaction-model.md:127-166) lists no hook for a row's icon, authors, description or badges, nor for the header's prerelease/restore toggles or the refresh button, so §1 (interaction-model.md:11-17: comfortable rows with icons/description/badges; restore toggle visible in both surfaces) has no machine check. restoreEnabled is referenced by no product file under src/webview/main (git grep over origin/labs/2.0 -- src/webview/main hits only test snapshot helpers); MainShell.tsx:174-178 renders 'Include prerelease' without a test id.
- Fix: (1) §8: add row-icon, row-authors, row-description, badge-<name> (verified, cpm, vulnerable, installed), prerelease-toggle, restore-toggle, refresh-button; the existing lint then fails until each is rendered (TF1 adds the Row/Badge ids; MainShell gets prerelease-toggle on :175, refresh-button on :178; restore-toggle lands with the header fix). (2) New lint webview-density.test.ts in the style of interaction-model-testids: for every .tsx under src/webview/main that renders <Listbox ... density="comfortable">, the <Row ...> inside its renderRow must pass icon= and description= (regex over the file without comments); seed the lint with a planted-source self-test like interaction-model-testids.test.ts:137-161.
- Files: `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx`, `src/webview/shared/primitives/Badge.tsx`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `src/test/lint/webview-density.test.ts`
- Test: Lint red on current labs/2.0 for InstalledProjectSection.tsx:118-136, UpdatesTab.tsx:181-196 and AllProjectsUpdates.tsx:110-118; green after the feature fix.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project backend src/test/lint`
- Refuter corrections:
  - Three narrowings; the claim otherwise stands.

(a) "§1 has no machine check" is too strong. §1 has no doc-driven check, and no check of any kind for Installed/Updates row icons or a main-header restore control, but parts of §1 are machine-checked by non-lint tests: origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.test.tsx:67-73 asserts icon, authors, downloads, Verified and Installed on browse rows; src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.test.tsx:123-128 asserts the primitive shows the icon at comfortable density and hides it at compact; src/test/ui/v2/sidebar.ui.test.ts:71 reads --nuiget-row-height-compact for the sidebar; src/test/e2e/v2-commands.e2e.test.ts:46-47 flips nuiget.sidebar.toggleRestore/togglePrerelease. The uncovered promises are specifically: icon on Installed/Updates rows, and the restore toggle/Restore action in the main header (§1:14, §5:80).

(b) The unrendered list-row gap is the icon, not "icons/description/badges" wholesale: InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124 passes description from metadata and :129-131 renders CPM/Vulnerable badges; UpdatesTab.tsx:189-191 renders a Verified badge. Only BrowseList.tsx:111 passes icon. A fix item should add the icon (and authors) to Installed/Updates rows, not re-add description/badges.

(c) Line range: MainShell.tsx:174-177 is the 'Include prerelease' label/checkbox; :178 is the Refresh IconButton. Both lack a data-testid.

(d) The lint is doc→code in two forms, not one: it also fails for an enumerated pattern name (e.g. row-action-install) that nothing renders (:132-135). Still no code→doc or doc→behaviour check.
  - TF4 as framed (a lint defect, root cause "other") should be dropped: the one-directional §8 ⊆ rendered check and the §8 admission rule are the plan's prescribed design (plan:285; STATUS.md:3064-3074, :724; interaction-model.md:170-173; ADR-008:39), 1.x had no lint, and an inverse "§1 → rendered" lint was never specified — adding one is a new owner decision, not a fix. The "Include prerelease has no test id" point is not a gap under the admission rule (read by role in MainShell.test.tsx:74,90; no external test needs it). What survives of TF4 is one concrete item that belongs to the header area, not the lint: the main panel renders no "Restore after operations" toggle, contradicting interaction-model.md:14 and regressing 1.x App.tsx:2414-2417, with no ADR, ruling or doc amendment deciding it (ARCHITECTURE.md:541-547 and STATUS.md:61 describe the as-built header silently without restore). Re-file it as a high-severity header gap with root cause "not rendered by feature (MainShell)": add a `restoreEnabled` checkbox bound to `session/set { restoreEnabled }` beside the prerelease one in src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx, a MainShell.test.tsx case mirroring lines 84-92 for restore, an ARCHITECTURE.md header-table row, and an interaction-model §1 line-17 clarification; gate `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx` plus `npm run docs:check`. If the owner instead wants the main panel without the toggle, amend interaction-model.md:14 and record the ruling.
  - Severity should be LOW, and TF4 should be folded into the "test to add" column of the Installed/Updates row-parity gap and the header-toggle gap rather than listed as its own parity gap: it has no user-visible effect, and 1.x had nothing comparable. The lint itself needs no redesign — it does what interaction-model.md:170-173 says. The guard that is actually missing is content assertions, not hooks: (i) one RTL assertion per list in origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/features/installed/InstalledTab.test.tsx and features/updates/UpdatesTab.test.tsx that a comfortable row renders its icon (`.nuiget-row-icon`) and includes authors/description in the option's accessible description, mirroring BrowseList.test.tsx:67-82; (ii) once a restore control exists in the main header, a `restore-toggle` (and `prerelease-toggle`) hook added to §8 and to the hook list at src/test/ui/v2/main-shell.ui.test.ts:20. Adding hooks alone would not catch the owner's complaint, since BrowseList already renders an icon and would satisfy a presence-somewhere `row-icon` hook. Gate: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/main` then `npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui`. Estimated effort: under half a day, and it ships no change a user can see.

### TF5 — No test covers the header toggles or the cross-surface context of §1 (prerelease and restore visible and in sync in both surfaces)

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **KILLED** · Root cause: `other` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:2413-2425 rendered both checkboxes 'Restore after operations' and 'Include prerelease'; the 1.x panel-open UI test checked only the selectors (origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/test-retirement-map.md, panel-open row).
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-shell.ui.test.ts:20 lists nuiget-app, search-input, project-selector, source-selector, tab-bar, installed-tab, updates-tab, sash, package-details-panel, status-live: no toggle. origin/labs/2.0:src/test/e2e/v2-sidebar.e2e.test.ts:45-51 flips togglePrerelease/togglePrereleaseOff without reading any surface. origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:174-178 has prerelease only; no restore control exists in the main webview (see TF4).
- Fix: After prerelease-toggle and restore-toggle exist (TF4 ids; restore control from the header area): in main-shell add 'the header shows the shared context and follows the sidebar commands': assert both toggles present and enabled once the session arrived; leaveWebview, run 'nUIget: Skip restore after operations' (origin/labs/2.0:package.json:122) via the palette, enterMainWebview, poll restore-toggle unchecked; run 'nUIget: Restore after operations' (:117) and poll checked; same pair for 'nUIget: Include Pre-release' / 'Exclude Pre-release' (:104, :109) on prerelease-toggle.
- Files: `src/test/ui/v2/main-shell.ui.test.ts`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`
- Test: One new ExTester test in main-shell.ui.test.ts, red until the restore control exists in the header.
- Gate: `npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The claim is wrong as stated; the residual, narrower gaps are: (1) no harness scenario exercises `h.toggle(…, 'restoreEnabled')` — origin/labs/2.0:src/test/harness/harness.ts:28 types `Toggle = 'includePrerelease' | 'restoreEnabled'` but scenarios.test.ts only toggles `includePrerelease`, so restore cross-surface propagation is covered at host/extension level (extension.test.ts:440-447, SessionStore.test.ts:162-166), not through two FakeWebviews; (2) no ExTester UI test clicks the main-panel prerelease checkbox in VS Code or inspects the sidebar title-bar toggle icons (main-shell.ui.test.ts:20 lists no toggle testId; no `src/test/ui/v2/*` file mentions prerelease) — same as 1.x, whose panel-open UI test also checked only selectors; (3) the absence of a restore control in the main webview (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:174-178 has only 'Include prerelease') is a product gap (TF4), not a test gap, and cannot be "tested" until the control exists. If a TF5-style item is kept, it should be rescoped to: add a `restoreEnabled` scenario to scenarios.test.ts mirroring :720-757, and (optionally) a `main-shell.ui.test.ts` assertion on a `prerelease-toggle` testId once TF4 adds the restore control.
  - Narrow TF5 to the restore toggle and the sidebar's visible context-key state, and lower it to low-medium. Prerelease is covered at every layer the plan documented: header checkbox (MainShell.test.tsx:74,84-93), store reconciliation from the other surface (session.test.tsx:105-127,172-180), host-level broadcast and persistence (SessionStore.test.ts:368-384), and a two-surface harness scenario that is the retirement map's named 2.0 proof (scenarios.test.ts:720-758; test-retirement-map.md:53). The real gaps are: no harness scenario calls toggle(surface,'restoreEnabled') although the Toggle type offers it (harness.ts:28,241); no test asserts the setContext calls in SidebarHost.ts:35-36,43-44 that drive the title-bar icons (1.x had NuGetSidebarPanel.test.ts:169-276); the e2e command tests flip without reading. The absence of an ExTester toggle test is not a regression (the 1.x panel-open test never checked toggles, test-retirement-map.md:19) and follows the documented "§8 ids" scope, but §1 and plan row P5.10 ("title-bar toggles | tests") show the gap is unplanned, not a design decision. Proposed fix (≤1 day): add scenario 'restore toggle reaches both surfaces and ops honour it' next to scenarios.test.ts:720 (toggle on sidebar, assert both webviews' session.restoreEnabled flips and a following ops/update passes skipRestore:true, cf. handlers.test.ts:481); add a SidebarHost unit test asserting setContext('nuiget.prereleaseEnabled'|'nuiget.restoreEnabled') on construction and on session.onDidChange; gate: npx vitest run --project integration src/test/harness -t "restore toggle" and npx vitest run src/host. Main-panel restore visibility is TF4's fix, not TF5's.
  - Downgrade to low and reframe as a narrow coverage follow-up, not a parity gap. What is genuinely untested in 2.0 (verified by grep): (1) no host-level assertion that restoreEnabled:false yields skipRestore:true — every snapshot in src/host/handlers.test.ts (:55) and src/webview tests uses restoreEnabled:true, and `git grep "restoreEnabled: false" origin/labs/2.0 -- src/host src/webview src/test` returns nothing; 1.x had this at 0457c75:src/webview/NuGetPanel.test.ts:876. (2) The harness driver accepts the 'restoreEnabled' toggle (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/harness/harness.ts:28,241-249) but no scenario calls it (scenarios.test.ts has only the two includePrerelease calls at :739,:749). (3) origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.test.tsx has no `session/changed` → checkbox-flips test (grep: no hits); the equivalent exists at store level (session.test.tsx:118-124) and 1.x's was App.test.tsx:367. Fix (≤2h, low): add one handlers.test.ts case with a restoreEnabled:false snapshot asserting {skipRestore:true}; add one harness scenario `h.toggle('sidebar','restoreEnabled')` then `operate(...)` asserting the op's skipRestore; optionally one MainShell.test.tsx case broadcasting session/changed {includePrerelease:true, origin:'sidebar'} and asserting the header checkbox is checked. Gate: `npm test`. An ExTester restore toggle test stays out by the recorded STATUS.md:85 ruling (palette-hidden commands); if the fix phase adds a main-panel restore control (TF4), add its testid to §8 and extend main-shell.ui.test.ts then.

### TF6 — A green UI run leaves no visual evidence: ExTester screenshots only failed tests, so the uploaded screenshot artifact is empty on success and nobody saw the rows

- Severity: **medium** · Verification: **OK** · Root cause: `other` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: Same in 1.x (same runner, same workflow pattern): no screenshot on success.
- 2.0: node_modules/vscode-extension-tester/out/suite/runner.js:114-119 takes a screenshot only when currentTest.state === 'failed'; origin/labs/2.0:.github/workflows/ci.yml:290-298 uploads /tmp/nuiget-ui/screenshots with if-no-files-found: ignore, so a green run uploads nothing and says nothing.
