No plan gate names a screenshot or a visual review (grep of origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/RUN.md for screenshot/visual: none; RUN.md:66-68 makes the row's Gate column the acceptance test); the STATUS notes record '32 passing' as the UI proof (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/STATUS.md). The owner's stated visual channel (CI screenshots or mockups) therefore produced no image across P5-P8.
- Fix: (1) New file visual-record.ui.test.ts (sorts after theme.ui.test.ts, so it runs last): open the main panel, select package-row-newtonsoft.json, wait for the details, VSBrowser.instance.takeScreenshot('main-installed-details'); click updates-tab, screenshot 'main-updates'; browse 'Serilog', screenshot 'main-browse'; open the sidebar, screenshot 'sidebar'. takeScreenshot writes <storage>/screenshots/<timestamp>/<name>.png (node_modules/vscode-extension-tester/out/browser.js:410-414), which ci.yml:297 already uploads. (2) ci.yml UI job: after 'Run UI tests' add a step that fails when find /tmp/nuiget-ui/screenshots -name '*.png' | wc -l is below 4 and lists the names in $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY; rename the artifact ui-log-and-screenshots. (3) RUN.md: a feature row that changes a webview must link the four PNGs of its CI run in its STATUS note for the owner.
- Files: `src/test/ui/v2/visual-record.ui.test.ts`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`
- Test: Local run produces 4 PNGs under /tmp/nuiget-ui/screenshots/*/; CI artifact contains them; the count step is red when the suite is pointed at an empty directory (negative control).
- Gate: `rm -rf dist && npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui && test $(ls /tmp/nuiget-ui/screenshots/*/*.png | wc -l) -ge 4`
- Refuter corrections:
  - The core claim stands (a green 2.0 UI run yields a log-only artifact, no image, no gate asked for one), but three statements need correcting. (1) "1.x: same workflow pattern" is false: the released 1.x CI has no UI job at all. 0457c75:.github/workflows/ci.yml jobs are lint:18, test:60, e2e:94, build:149, benchmark:181 (origin/main = 16831f4 has the identical job list); `test:ui` existed only as a local script (0457c75:package.json:311) and 1.x src/test/ui/** has no screenshot call. The `ui` CI job was added on labs/2.0 at 98bcdd1 (2026-10-12, P0.8) and the screenshot upload at 3b6f987 / 779a6cd. So 1.x shares only the runner's failed-only screenshot behaviour; it produced zero CI UI evidence of any kind, and 2.0's pipeline is strictly more than 1.x here — the fix phase must not treat the 1.x pipeline as the parity target. (2) "Produced no image across P5-P8" is overstated: failure screenshots were produced and read during local runs at P5.3 and P5.10 — origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/findings-ledger.md:425 (N66, "the failure screenshot shows the Serilog row gone and Newtonsoft.Json listed") and :432 (N73, "the failure screenshot shows Newtonsoft.Json alone in the sidebar"). These are sidebar failure images seen by the session, not by the owner; no image of a passing main panel exists. The failed-only screenshot artifact was also a designed choice, recorded in docs/plans/STATUS.md:2094 and ARCHITECTURE.md:818 ("log and screenshot artifact"), not an omission. (3) "Nobody saw the rows" should be scoped: the green suite does machine-check some visual facts — origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-shell.ui.test.ts:27-46 (root display flex, height > 200, `--nuiget-split` 50%, list ≈ half the split, non-transparent computed color), sidebar.ui.test.ts:69 (computed color, root height), theme.ui.test.ts:52-63 (axe color-contrast/target-size plus body/html backgroundColor) — so stylesheet loading under CSP, layout and theme colours were verified; what no test or screenshot ever checked is row richness (icons, description, authors, verified badge). Minor wording: the artifact `ui-log` is not empty on success — it carries ui-output.txt; only the screenshots component is absent.
  - The claim's 2.0 finding stands (medium severity; root cause is a process/tooling gap, not "other": every gate is DOM/test-title level, the CI step passively uploads a directory the runner populates only on failure, and no doc, gate or step produces or reviews an image on green). Three corrections: (1) "Same in 1.x (same runner, same workflow pattern)" is wrong — 0457c75:.github/workflows/ci.yml has no UI job at all (jobs lint:18, test:60, e2e:94, build:149, benchmark:181; no `test:ui`/ExTester/screenshot); plan ledger T2/N2 (plan:396,407) "ExTester UI suite never runs in CI"; 1.x `test:ui` (0457c75:package.json:311) ran only locally. 2.0 added the CI UI job, the log upload and failure-screenshot upload — list this under "2.0 does better" so the fix does not regress it. (2) "the uploaded screenshot artifact is empty … uploads nothing and says nothing" overstates: there is one combined `ui-log` artifact that on a green run contains `ui-output.txt` (the `tee` log with "32 passing"); only the screenshots path is absent, and the `ui` job lacks a step summary. (3) Runner lines are :113-124 with the failed-state guard at :116 and `takeScreenshot` at :119 (not 114-119). Fix pointer (≤1 day): add explicit `VSBrowser.instance.takeScreenshot('<surface>-<state>')` calls at key states in the v2 suites (main Installed rows, browse results, details pane, sidebar sections), split the screenshots into their own upload step with `if-no-files-found: error`, add a UI step summary listing the PNGs, and add an owner visual-review gate for UI items in RUN.md; gate: `xvfb-run -a npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui && ls /tmp/nuiget-ui/screenshots/*/*.png | wc -l` ≥ the number of named states.
  - Downgrade TF6 from medium to LOW and reclassify it from "parity gap" to "process root-cause note" for the design regression. What survives: ExTester screenshots only failed tests; no v2 UI test or plan gate produces or reviews a success-path image; "32 passing" was the only UI proof; the owner's expected visual channel ("CI ExTester screenshots or mockups") therefore never yielded a success image of the main-panel rows. What must be struck: (a) "Same in 1.x" — 1.x CI (0457c75) had no UI job, no screenshot upload and no artifact upload at all, so 2.0 improved on 1.x here; the fix phase must keep the new `ui` job, log tee and screenshot upload (ci.yml:261-298). (b) "No image across P5-P8" — failure screenshots were taken and examined in P5.3 and P5.10 (findings-ledger N66, N73). Proposed fix (≤1 hour, not ≤1 day): add `captureScreenshot(name)` to src/test/ui/v2/mainWebview.ts / sidebarWebview.ts wrapping `VSBrowser.instance.takeScreenshot(sanitizedName)`, call it in each suite's existing afterEach (or once per key state: installed rows, updates rows, details panel, sidebar sections), optionally gate on `process.env.CI`; no ci.yml change needed since /tmp/nuiget-ui/screenshots is already uploaded. Add a RUN.md gate line for UI items: "ui-log artifact contains ≥1 PNG per surface; owner reviews before the item is `done`". Gate: `npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui && ls /tmp/nuiget-ui/screenshots/*/*.png | wc -l` ≥ number of suites.

### TF7 — The two-surface harness compares id@version only; enrichment (authors, icon, description) is never asserted end to end

- Severity: **low** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `other` · Estimate: 0.5 d
- 1.18.9: 1.x integration tests asserted service results directly (test-retirement-map.md, NuGetPackageService row); no end-to-end check of enrichment either.
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/test/harness/harness.ts:56 packagesOf reduces a result to id@version; origin/labs/2.0:src/test/harness/scenarios.test.ts:63,99 compare installed: ['Serilog@3.0.0']; origin/labs/2.0:src/test/harness/fakeFeed.ts:60,71 serve authors 'Harness' and descriptions, yet no scenario asserts that a transitive/metadata or updates/get answer carries them.
- Fix: Add a scenario 'enrichment reaches both surfaces': the main surface asks transitive/metadata for the installed rows (as installed.tsx:77 does) and updates/get; assert each answer carries authors 'Harness' and a description, and iconUrl when the fake feed serves an icon (add a small data: icon to fakeFeed); extend expectState with an optional enriched flag.
- Files: `src/test/harness/scenarios.test.ts`, `src/test/harness/fakeFeed.ts`, `src/test/harness/harness.ts`
- Test: The new scenario, red if the host stops enriching.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project integration src/test/harness`

### TF8 — All Projects ExTester test depends on a .csproj create event the host may never receive; run 38914547552 timed out twice (120 s each) while the PR run on the same SHA passed

- Severity: **high** · Verification: **unverified** · Root cause: `behaviour` · Estimate: 1 d
- 1.18.9: No 1.x ExTester test covered All Projects (0457c75:src/test/ui holds 11 files, none for it).
- 2.0: origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-all-projects.ui.test.ts:45-50 writes Utility/Utility.csproj in before, then :78-86 and :88-92 poll option[value="__all_projects__"] for 120 s with no other trigger. Host: the project list is a cached coordinator resource (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/catalog/Catalog.ts:120-122); rediscovery runs only on an invalidation of scope workspace/project/sources (origin/labs/2.0:src/extension.ts:249-259), fed by the .csproj watcher (extension.ts:320, 1500 ms debounce), workspace-folder changes (:327-330) and the refresh commands (:347; origin/labs/2.0:src/host/handlers.ts:405-408). The option renders from projects.length (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:170) fed by projects/list then projects/changed (origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/shared/store/projects.tsx:43-49; origin/labs/2.0:src/host/MainPanelHost.ts:84). Logs (gh run view --log, read-only): failing run 38914547552 job 117020231790: 'Launching browser' 14:21:55.49 -> 'Launching tests' 14:21:58.28 (2.8 s); both tests failed at exactly 120 s with 'Timed out after 120000 ms waiting for the All Projects option' and no '(last error ...)' suffix (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/sidebarWebview.ts:27 appends one when the probe threw), so the select was live and never gained the option; the following 30 tests passed. Passing run 38914554505 (same SHA b1ad903): 14:23:02.02 -> 14:23:09.36 (7.3 s) and the first test passed with no duration printed (the option was already in the DOM when the panel opened). Ruled out: session normalization (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/session/SessionStore.ts:124-126) only downgrades the committed selection, not the option; the webview does subscribe to projects/changed; the debounce is 1.5 s against 240 s of waiting. Most likely: on the fast start the activation's discovery cached one project before the write, and the create event never reached the extension's watcher (VS Code's recursive workspace watcher subscribes asynchronously after the window opens; inferred from the 2.8 s vs 7.3 s gap and the no-rediscovery evidence, not verified against VS Code source), so the projects key was never invalidated.
- Fix: (1) MainShell.tsx:178: data-testid="refresh-button" on the Refresh IconButton; §8 row. (2) Test: after the write in before, open the panel and, inside the 120 s poll for the option, click refresh-button whenever ~10 s pass without it (refresh/all -> handlers.ts:405-408 workspace invalidation -> scopes.ts:103-104 bumps projects -> extension.ts:256-258 rediscovers -> projects/changed). Do not use Workbench.executeCommand('nUIget: Refresh Packages'): origin/labs/2.0:package.json:147-148 hides nuiget.refreshPackages with when: "false". (3) after (lines 52-65): after rmSync, click refresh-button too, so the downgrade (SessionStore.ts:124-126) does not depend on a delete event; a lost one would leave ALL_PROJECTS selected for every following file. (4) Harness: add files.addProject(path, xml) beside addReference/deleteProject (harness.ts:301-305) that writes without firing the fake watcher; scenario 'a project added without a watcher event is listed after a refresh': expectState projects unchanged, refresh('main'), expectState projects [A, B, C] and projects/changed received by both surfaces. (5) Optional host hardening for the host area to decide: on a surface's first session/get, services.invalidate({ kind: 'project', projectPaths: [] }, 'manual-refresh') re-lists projects only (origin/labs/2.0:src/core/queries/scopes.ts:65-67 matches projects and allProjects:* for any path list, no installed list recomputed). Do not add a tracked second-project fixture: rejected by the P5.9 ruling (origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/STATUS.md:2875). (6) Record run 38914547552 in the P8.3 tracker row (grep for it over origin/labs/2.0 finds nothing) and apply the N44 policy: one re-run, a second sighting on one SHA is a stop-and-ask.
- Files: `src/test/ui/v2/main-all-projects.ui.test.ts`, `src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx`, `docs/ux/interaction-model.md`, `src/test/harness/harness.ts`, `src/test/harness/scenarios.test.ts`
- Test: Harness scenario (red if refresh/all stops re-listing projects) plus three local ExTester runs (rm -rf dist before each) all 32 passing; CI UI job green on the pushed SHA.
- Gate: `npx vitest run --project integration src/test/harness && rm -rf dist && npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui`

**Better in 2.0 (do not regress):**
- Bounded polling instead of fixed sleeps everywhere in the UI suite (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/sidebarWebview.ts:13-30); 1.x slept 5 s and asserted only that the app was alive (0457c75:src/test/ui/search-flow.ui.test.ts:27-31). Keep the poll helper and its last-error reporting.
- Real ARIA and keyboard assertions on the real host: role=listbox, aria-multiselectable, aria-current vs aria-selected, Space/Enter/Escape, roving focus into the tabs (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-installed.ui.test.ts:49-66; main-shell.ui.test.ts:49-62; sidebar-parity.ui.test.ts:47-61). Nothing in 1.x checked semantics.
- Theme pass measures contrast and 24 px target size with axe under six built-in themes in both webviews, with 'rule did not run' guarded (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/theme.ui.test.ts:157-174); 1.x theme-switching only checked that the panel rendered (test-retirement-map.md).
- Layout proof from tokens under the CSP for the sidebar (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/sidebar.ui.test.ts:63-81) and for the main split (main-shell.ui.test.ts:27-46): extend to main rows (TF3), do not remove.
- Lint suite over the webviews: Chromium 114 CSS baseline from MDN data (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/lint/webview-css-baseline.test.ts), tokens, colours, stylesheets, import boundaries, and the §8 hook lint (interaction-model-testids.test.ts:125-135). TF4 adds the reverse direction; keep the existing one.
- Two-surface harness with a fake dotnet and fake feed proving convergence, one-response-per-request, queue saturation and project deletion mid-request (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/harness/scenarios.test.ts:53-238); 1.x had no equivalent.
- Extension-host e2e ported without fixed sleeps, on VS Code 1.85.0 and stable, with the fixture written back (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/e2e/v2Support.ts; .github/workflows/ci.yml:166-257).
- CI retries only when no test ran, never a red count (origin/labs/2.0:.github/workflows/ci.yml:203-216); UI job uploads the log and failure screenshots and runs check:vsix on the packaged build (ci.yml:284-298).
- Fixture hygiene: every suite restores TestProject.csproj and waits for the list to settle before the next file (origin/labs/2.0:src/test/ui/v2/main-search.ui.test.ts:34-47; sidebar-parity.ui.test.ts:23-41).
- Perf timing lines in the UI log ([P5.6] cold search, [P7.2] warm search: main-search.ui.test.ts:69,85) give a per-run latency record; keep them.

**Notes:**
- Be exact about what is and is not wired before the fix phase: UpdatesTab.tsx:191 already renders the Verified badge and InstalledProjectSection.tsx:124 already passes the description; what is missing everywhere except BrowseList.tsx:109-116 is icon and authors, and Installed also lacks Verified. The data is available: updates are enriched host-side (Catalog.ts:255-268) and installed lite metadata is fetched by the store (installed.tsx:77-93), so the product root cause is feature-not-rendered, not host-not-sent.
- The sidebar's plainer rows are by design: §1 says compact rows, no icons, no descriptions (interaction-model.md:12), and sidebar.ui.test.ts:71-77 holds it to the compact token. A test cannot flag an intended change; if the owner wants icons in the sidebar that is a design decision, not a gate miss.
- Why the gates passed, in one sentence: every UI gate was defined as the §8 ids (docs/plans/STATUS.md:76), §8 has no id for what 1.x rows showed, the doc lint only checks that §8 ids exist in code, screenshots exist only on failure, and the 1.x suite that was ported never asserted this content either (test-retirement-map.md ports what the legacy files proved).
- The restore toggle is absent from the main webview altogether (restoreEnabled appears in no product file under src/webview/main); that is a header-area gap. TF5 covers only the missing test.
- The failing CI run 38914547552 (push on labs/2.0, 'docs(changelog): collapse a double blank line', SHA b1ad903) and the passing run 38914554505 (pull_request, same SHA) were read with gh run view --log; no artifact was downloaded. The run is not recorded in docs/plans (grep over origin/labs/2.0 finds nothing).
- Unverified: that VS Code's recursive workspace watcher was not yet subscribed when the test wrote Utility.csproj 2.8 s after launch is inferred from the logs and the code paths, not from VS Code internals. The proposed fix (explicit refresh in the test, harness scenario for the refresh path) does not depend on that inference being right.
- nuiget.refreshPackages is hidden from the Command Palette (package.json:147-148, when: "false"), so the ExTester fix must click the panel's Refresh button, which has no test id today (MainShell.tsx:178).
- The theme test's 'incomplete color-contrast' lines in the UI log are axe nodes inside the details pane it could not decide, allowed by the P5.12b ruling (theme.ui.test.ts:162-169); they are not a content check and said nothing about the rows.
- UI test file order is alphabetical because the npm script passes an unquoted shell glob (package.json:309) that the shell expands sorted before extest sees it; main-all-projects therefore runs first, right after VS Code starts, which is why its before hook races activation.

## Critic

### Missing from the audit
- Single-operation notifications and progress (host/ops had no audit area owner). 1.x wrapped every install/update/remove and every bulk in `vscode.window.withProgress` (0457c75:src/services/NuGetOperations.ts:52,118,192,266,357,461 — 6 sites) and had ~44 `show{Information,Warning,Error}Message` sites (NuGetCliService.ts 15, NuGetOperations.ts 16, NuGetPanel.ts 5, NuGetSidebarPanel.ts 3, others 5). 2.0 has zero `withProgress` and 8 `show*Message` sites (origin/labs/2.0:src/extension.ts:362,369 cache cleared; src/host/SidebarHost.ts:120,153,261,293; src/host/pickers.ts:12,54), none keyed on an `ops/*` outcome. This contradicts docs/ux/interaction-model.md §4:64 ('Partial / failed op → Toast (VS Code notification)') and §5:75-76 ('progress toast', 'outcome toast') for EVERY single install/update/uninstall, not only Update All (MU4). A failed single op is announced only in the visually hidden live region (src/webview/shared/store/outcomes.ts:51; LiveStatus is `.nuiget-visually-hidden`); a sighted user sees the row spinner stop and nothing else. Severity high; fix is one subscriber in extension.ts on operation outcomes (cf. MU4 correction 3).
- Output channel never revealed. 1.x `setupOutputChannel()` called `outputChannel.show(true)` before every operation (0457c75:src/services/NuGetLogger.ts:14-20), which doubled as visible dotnet progress and error detail. 2.0 creates a LogOutputChannel (origin/labs/2.0:src/extension.ts:65, src/host/services.ts:148-149) and never calls `.show` on it — the only `.show(` calls in non-test src are `statusBar.show()` (extension.ts:278) and `picker.show()` (pickers.ts:35). §4's Error row ('details also logged to the output channel') is met by a handful of `logger.error` calls, but nothing surfaces the channel to the user. Not examined by any area.
- No webview ErrorBoundary in 2.0. 1.x mounted one in 0457c75:src/webview/app/index.tsx; `git grep -l 'ErrorBoundary\|componentDidCatch' origin/labs/2.0 -- src/webview` is empty and src/webview/main/index.tsx renders StrictMode → StoreProvider → MainShell bare. A render exception blanks the whole webview silently. This matters for the owner's report: from outside, a runtime exception and a rendering trim look identical, and the entire audit (this critique included) is code-reading with zero rendered evidence. Wave 0 must start with a screenshot plus the Webview Developer Tools console from the second machine.
- Marketplace listing content. README.md's demo gif is the identical blob in both refs (`docs/images/nuiget.gif` = 86da6ac2709afd7ba1c09b9395a216d03e93d550 at 0457c75 and origin/labs/2.0; README.md:8 links it from `main`), and all 3 image references are unchanged. The 2.0.0 listing would advertise the 1.x rows with icons/authors/verified that 2.0 does not render. Not examined.
- Undocumented removals as a cross-cutting item. CHANGELOG 2.0.0 'Breaking changes' (origin/labs/2.0:CHANGELOG.md:14-17) lists four items (1.x replaced, SDK 10 floor, persisted-state reset, noRestore migration and recent searches gone) and none of the UI removals the audit surfaced (header restore checkbox, sidebar Update all, Ctrl+Enter quick install on rows and dropdown, operation toasts/progress, hover prefetch, source-health marker). `scripts/check-docs-drift.mjs` (lines 2-8) checks message names, settings/commands/context keys and paths against the tree, not behaviour removals, so nothing forced these to be written down. Individual corrections mention single cases; the results have no item requiring a Changed/Breaking line per deliberate removal.
- Details-pane contrast is never measured. theme.ui.test.ts:164-167 tolerates axe `incomplete` color-contrast nodes when `insideDetails` is true; the failed CI run's log prints exactly those nodes (`.nuiget-details-group:nth-child(7) > ul > li:nth-child(4)`, `:nth-child(8) > h4/p`, `:nth-child(9) > h4/p`) under every theme while the suite passes. The pane the owner called 'sparser' has no measured contrast gate and no area examined its typography beyond MB10 (README) and MB9/MB12 (facts).
- Why the red CI run did not stop the release (answers the user's 'also the CI failed'). Run 38914547552 (push, b1ad903) failed only in job UI — 30 passing, 2 failing, both `Timed out after 120000 ms waiting for the All Projects option` in main-all-projects.ui.test.ts (TF8); Lint, Test ×2, E2E ×2, Build & Package, Benchmark, Perf, SDK probe were green. The pull_request run 38914554505 on the same SHA passed. Ruleset 12524593 on `main` requires only `Test`, `Lint`, `Build & Package` (origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/STATUS.md:980), so UI and E2E are not required checks and a red UI job cannot block the release PR. The results carry TF8 but not this consequence or the remedy (fix TF8, then add UI/E2E to the required checks). Note the UI job has no retry step (ci.yml:258-298), unlike E2E (ci.yml:203-216); that is correct, since a red count must never be retried.
- Header 'Restore project(s)' action: interaction-model §5:80 lists 'Transitive banner, header', but origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:160-178 renders selectors, SourcesControl, the prerelease checkbox and Refresh only, and `ops/restore` is requested from the transitive banners alone (TransitiveSection.tsx:51, AllProjectsTransitiveSection.tsx:105). 1.x had no header Restore either (only the transitive prompt at 0457c75:src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.tsx:1379 and the all-projects batch restore, App.tsx:271), so this is a 2.0 doc-vs-code mismatch to amend (drop 'header' from §5 or add the action), not a parity gap. No area examined it.
- Unexamined, no gap claimed: (a) focus-ring visuals — 2.0 has 13 `focus-visible` rules (primitives.css 9, main.css 3, sidebar.css 1) vs 31 `:focus`/`focus-visible` rules in 1.x App.css+SidebarApp.css; counts only, nobody looked at a focused row, and MH6 covered only outline width in HC; (b) light-theme LOOK beyond contrast — contrast IS measured under Light Modern and Light 2026 (theme.ui.test.ts:22-27), so do not overstate, but no claim examined light rendering of rows, badges or section headers; (c) narrow-width behaviour of either surface (sidebar at 200 px, panel at a half-screen split); (d) sidebar context-menu item parity — the results only assert typed actions are 'better'; no comparison of 1.x `_showContextMenu` labels to 2.0 pickers.ts actions; (e) main-panel row context menu (Shift+F10 route exists per §5; content unexamined); (f) P7.4, the only owner-run visual/a11y pass (VoiceOver/NVDA, narrow sidebar, light/HC) is `skipped` → 2.0.1 (STATUS.md:106, 'no VS Code or Windows machine available'), so no human has looked at light or HC rendering of 2.0 at all.
- Examined here and at parity, so the results' silence is acceptable: `contributes.*` is identical between refs (12 commands, same menus/when-clauses, same view, same activationEvents) except `nuiget.recentSearchesLimit` removed (documented, MH9); all four remaining settings are read by the 2.0 host (origin/labs/2.0:src/host/services.ts:90-96; handlers.ts:300,306,424); status bar present in both (extension.ts:269-278 vs 0457c75:src/services/OperationQueue.ts:78); panel tab icon (MainPanelHost.ts:61), `openManager` explorer-context argument (extension.ts:345 `projectPathFromCommandArg`), `initialTab`, sidebar view title (SidebarHost.ts:304-308) all carried over; legacy state keys are cleared on purpose (src/host/legacyState.ts:14-24, documented Breaking line). Correction to an MH1 verifier: `nuiget.sidebar.toggleRestore` was palette-hidden (`commandPalette when: false`) in 1.x too — losing the main-panel checkbox removes the only non-sidebar path, which is the real point, but it is not a 2.0 change to package.json.
- Zero rendered evidence anywhere in the audit. Every one of the ~90 entries, including all horizontal-scrollbar/crushed-name claims (MI3, MB1, MU11, DP7, MH5), is a code-reading inference; no reviewer rendered a row, opened the VSIX or looked at a screenshot. The audit therefore shares the blind spot of the gates it criticises, and Wave 0 must produce images before Wave 1 is sized.

### Overclaims and severity corrections
- Spot-checked and CONFIRMED (not overclaimed), five claims: MH1 — origin/labs/2.0:src/webview/main/MainShell.tsx:174-178 renders only the 'Include prerelease' checkbox and Refresh; no `restoreEnabled` binding anywhere in the file. DP3 — src/core/feeds/icons.ts:24 `ICON_SOURCE_MISS_THRESHOLD = 5`, :129 `consecutiveMisses` per source, :180-186 `reset()` and the suppression check, :214-218 the counter; no `nuget.org` string in the file, so no exemption. TF6 — zero `takeScreenshot`/`screenshot` calls under origin/labs/2.0 src/test; ci.yml:290-298 uploads `/tmp/nuiget-ui/screenshots` with `if-no-files-found: ignore`, so a green run yields a log-only artifact. TF8 — run 38914547552 UI job: 30 passing / 2 failing, both 'Timed out after 120000 ms waiting for the All Projects option' (main-all-projects.ui.test.ts:81-90 polling after `fs.writeFileSync` of Utility.csproj at :49); PR run 38914554505 green on the same SHA b1ad903; all other jobs green. DP5/MI1 — `icon={` appears only at src/webview/main/features/search/BrowseList.tsx:111; `data-package-icon` is emitted at src/host/services.ts:106-108 and read by no file under src/webview (1.x read it at 0457c75:src/webview/app/App.tsx:22 and used it in 8 row sites).
- Structural double counting inflates the headline. The icon gap is filed seven times (MI1, MU1, MU2, MB2, DP1, DP2, DP5 — five of them 'high'); the comfortable-row layout five times (MI3, MU11, MB1, MH5, DP7 — three 'high'); search-box buttons twice (MH8, SB6); the quick-search dropdown three times (MH10, MB6, MB7); the revalidation bar twice (MU5, MH7); the installed count twice (MI8, MH3); transitive rows twice (MI4, DP6); the vulnerability badge twice (MI7, DP8). About 90 entries collapse to roughly a dozen root fixes; the raw count of highs overstates the work by 3-4×. The fix phase should be planned from the collapsed list.
- Test-coverage notes rated as user-facing severities. TF1 (high), TF2 (high), TF3 (medium), TF4 (medium) and TF6 (medium) are gate weaknesses, not parity gaps a user would notice; by the task's own scale TF2 and TF3 are mis-scaled (their own verifiers say low), TF5 was refuted by all three verifiers (prerelease is covered at every layer; only the `restoreEnabled` harness toggle is unused), and TF6's 'same workflow pattern in 1.x' is false — 1.x CI had no UI job, no screenshot upload and no artifact at all (0457c75:.github/workflows/ci.yml jobs lint/test/e2e/build/benchmark). Keep them, but as the 'test to add' column of the product items, not as gaps.
- Documented design scope presented as regressions. MB4 (multi-install offered only under All Projects) is written in interaction-model §3 from the 2026-10-11 design commit, before the code; MB5 (no Ctrl+Enter install on main rows) follows §5, which defines Enter = details on the main panel and names no secondary action there; SB1 (no sidebar Update all) follows §1/§5, which place bulk actions on the main toolbars with an in-panel dialog. All three are owner decisions that lack a CHANGELOG line, not defects; SB1 'high' should read medium with the three-click route named. MH9 (recent searches) and SB12 (sidebar browse description) are documented decisions listed as gaps.
- 2.0-internal consistency items labelled 1.x parity gaps: MI5's `declaration: 'props'` badge (1.x had no `declaration` field); SB4's 'count always shown' (a ruled choice, and 2.0's own Installed/Updates headers do the same); the 'no Vulnerable badge on All Projects transitive' part of MI4/DP6 (1.x transitive rows had none; the single-project badge is a 2.0 improvement); MU2's 'icons chunk never consumed' (redundant with `update.iconUrl` on the rows, not a data-loss bug); the sidebar halves of MI5 and SB10 (the 1.x sidebar never marked implicit rows).
- Rendering inferences stated as facts. MI3, MB1, MU11, DP7 and MH5 assert horizontal scrollbars, crushed names and a 'single 44 px line' from CSS reading; their verifiers repeatedly note 'not rendered-verified'. 44 px is a `min-height` and the Listbox measures real rows (Listbox.tsx:96), so the one-line claim depends on `nowrap` behaviour nobody observed. Keep as 'likely' and size after a screenshot.
- Severity inflation already flagged by the verifiers and worth adopting: MH1 high → medium plausible (the sidebar toggle still governs main-panel ops; only the non-sidebar path is lost); MB1/MB2 high → medium (icons present at first paint; a ragged left edge, not missing function); MH5 high → medium once the data items land (the 'plain text' impression is the data gap, not the tokens); DP3 high → medium (Browse-only today since no other list renders icons; needs ≥5 consecutive ids the source does not know); MH6 medium → narrow to the `.nuiget-button` resting border (the inputs/selects half is wrong: VS Code supplies `--vscode-input-border`/`--vscode-dropdown-border` in both HC themes); SB3 medium → low (Dark/Light Modern and 2026 themes give the section header the same background as the sidebar); SB6 medium → low (native `type=search` clear control and Escape-to-clear exist); MB3's 'largest latency regression in this area' has no 1.x number and sits behind the ruled P7.2d deferral.
- Provenance slips. Several entries say 'the owner noticed/named X' (MH1 'owner-confirmed regression', SB4, MI1 corrections) where X is the harness-computed task text ('a gear icon and a single checkbox', 'rows are plain text'), not the user's relayed words, which were only 'the design is completely broken in 2.0, also the CI failed'. Severity should not borrow authority from the computed brief.
- The 1.x baseline is overstated in places, which inflates the delta: MB3 (1.x lite mode blanked verified/authors/description for every API row and Phase 2 queried all rows, hard-wired to nuget.org); MU5 (a 1.x manual Refresh showed no Updates spinner either; the content-compare short-circuited it); MI4 (1.x never showed 'Required by' inline on the row); MI3/MB1/MU11 (1.x main-panel rows had no description at all — the description is a 2.0 addition that overflows, so the fix must keep it, not 'restore' 1.x).

### Why the gates missed it
- Every plan gate was a count or a boolean, never a content assertion. P5 rows (origin/labs/2.0:docs/plans/2026-10-11-nuiget-2.0-execution-plan.md:274-286) gate on 'axe 0', 'tests green', 'line count check', one named harness scenario, 'hex-outside-tokens count 0'; phase gates P0-P6 (plan:201-300) are 'suites green in CI', 'coverage ≥ 85 %', 'advisor review recorded'. A list of text-only rows satisfies all of them. Richness was never a gate dimension, so no gate could fail on it.
- The design contract has no hook for its own §1. `src/test/lint/interaction-model-testids.test.ts` is doc→code over the §8 ids only (interaction-model.md:170-173), and §1's 'Comfortable rows (icons, description, badges)' has no testid or assertion behind it. BrowseList alone renders icon+description+badges, so §1 reads as satisfied for the whole main panel while Installed, Updates and Transitive render none of the icon and only part of the rest.
- The item specs omitted it, so conforming code shipped the gap. P5.4/P5.5 (plan:277-278) name All Projects grouping, transitive section, bulk dialogs, badge, CPM placeholder and the `sourcesUnavailable` banner — never icons/authors on rows; the P5.4 ruling asked for 'icon from the slice's metadata when present' only on TransitiveSection rows, which run `density="compact"` where Row.tsx:97 discards the icon; the P3.2 ruling fixed Row's DOM as one flex line and the P3.1 ruling set 44 px as a 'starting value' nobody retuned. The host did its part (iconUrl/verified/authors reach every slice); the feature files were never told to render it.
- Unit and UI tests inherited 1.x's blind spot. 0457c75 InstalledTab/UpdatesTab tests and all eleven 1.x ExTester files assert ids, roles and text, never icon/authors/verified; the retirement map (docs/plans/test-retirement-map.md:15-25) mapped each 1.x suite to a 2.0 namesake by behaviour ('Installed tab content', 'the panel renders under dark, light and high-contrast'), so parity of row content was never a test target. The only 2.0 test that asserts an icon is BrowseList.test.tsx:67-81 in jsdom; main-installed/main-updates ExTester suites assert testids, `aria-*` and version strings.
- The e2e gate cannot see rendering by construction. The nine `src/test/e2e/v2-*.e2e.test.ts` suites drive the extension host (commands registered, panel opens and reopens, install/update edit the fixture csproj, sidebar hidden/shown, sources) and never read webview DOM; they prove behaviour, and a webview that renders plain text passes them unchanged.
- No success-path image exists anywhere in the pipeline or the tracker. Zero `takeScreenshot` calls in src/test; ExTester screenshots only failed tests; ci.yml:290-298 uploads the screenshots directory with `if-no-files-found: ignore`, so a green UI run produced `ui-output.txt` only. STATUS.md mentions 'screenshot' once (a workflow note), 'visual review' and 'mockup' never. '32 passing' was the only UI evidence from P5 to P8.
- The one human visual gate was skipped. P7.4 — owner-run checklist with VoiceOver/NVDA, narrow sidebar, light and HC — is `skipped` → 2.0.1 (STATUS.md:106, 'no VS Code or Windows machine available'). The owner's working rule is that visual checks run via CI ExTester screenshots or mockups; neither was produced, so the first human to see 2.0 rendered was the owner on the second machine after the release PR was opened.
- Phase advisor reviews reviewed diffs, not renderings. RUN.md:102-107 hands the `architecture-advisor` a commit list and gate output; a reviewer reading diffs sees `Row` accept `icon`/`description`/`badges` and `InstalledPackage` carry `iconUrl`/`verified`/`authors` and reasonably assumes they are joined. Nothing in the brief could reveal that only BrowseList passes `icon`.
- Doc tooling checks names, not behaviours. `scripts/check-docs-drift.mjs` (lines 2-8) compares message names, settings, commands, context keys and paths; the P7.1 CHANGELOG gate required only the storage-reset/SDK-floor/noRestore wording. Removing the header restore checkbox, the toasts, the quick install or the sidebar Update all never had to be written down, so no reader of CHANGELOG/README could have noticed.
- The theme gate is blind to 'plain'. axe color-contrast and target-size (theme.ui.test.ts:53-54) pass on a text-only list; `incomplete` contrast nodes inside the details pane are tolerated by design (:164-167), and the CI log shows them under every theme. 'axe 0' across six themes was read as 'the UI is fine in every theme'.
- CI could not stop it even when red. UI and E2E are not required checks on ruleset 12524593 (STATUS.md:980: `Test`, `Lint`, `Build & Package` only), so TF8's red UI job on the push run (38914547552) had no effect on the release PR run (38914554505) that passed on the same SHA.
- Two mechanical gates that would catch it next time. (A) Success-path screenshot gate: call `VSBrowser.instance.takeScreenshot('<surface>-<state>')` at named states in each v2 suite (installed rows, updates rows, browse results with details, sidebar sections, one light and one HC theme), flip ci.yml:298 to `if-no-files-found: error`, list the PNGs in `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY`, and add a RUN.md line 'an item touching src/webview is not `done` until the owner has seen the PNGs in the ui-log artifact'. ≤1 hour, no new tooling. (B) §1 content assertion in ExTester, kept deterministic so it does not inherit TF8-style flake: on `installed-list`, `updates-list` and `browse-list`, assert every `[role=option]` in a comfortable list has an `img.nuiget-row-icon` with non-empty `src` (true once the default fallback lands, independent of nuget.org), assert `scrollWidth <= clientWidth` on every `[role=listbox]`, and only behind the existing `poll` helper assert the Newtonsoft.Json row's icon `src` starts with `data:`; add `package-row-icon` to §8 so the testid lint enforces presence. Optional third (host): a handlers/extension test asserting one `show*Message` per `OperationOutcome`/`BulkOutcome` status, which pins the §4/§5 toast contract.

### Recommended phase shape (adopted as P9)
- Wave 0 — evidence and CI (½ day, before any fix; the release PR `build!: release nUIget 2.0.0` already exists and must NOT be merged until Waves 0-2 land and the owner has seen the PNGs). (1) Ask the owner for a screenshot and the Webview Developer Tools console from the second machine: with no ErrorBoundary, a runtime exception and a rendering trim look identical from outside, and the whole audit is code-reading. (2) Fix or quarantine TF8 (main-all-projects.ui.test.ts waits 120 s for a .csproj create event the host may never receive) so the push run is green; a red count must never be retried. (3) Land mechanical gate A (success-path screenshots) and run it once on labs/2.0 so Wave 1 is sized from images, not CSS inference. (4) After TF8 is fixed, add `UI` and the two `E2E` jobs to ruleset 12524593's required checks — the direct answer to 'also the CI failed'.
- Owner decisions that gate Wave 1 scope — collect in one message, recommended default first with the trade-off: (a) authors line on Installed/Updates rows: yes (1.x parity; BrowseList already folds `by <authors>` into description) vs keep authors in Details only (less dense rows); (b) comfortable row layout: stacked two-line (name+badges / description+authors, like 1.x) vs one line with ellipsis (cheaper, keeps 44 px) — the missing `overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis` is a bug under either choice; (c) sidebar Update all: defer to 2.0.1 with a CHANGELOG Changed line (three-click route via Open Full Manager) vs add it now (~1 day, new `ui/*` request under the D6 contract rule); (d) Ctrl+Enter quick install on main rows and the suggestions dropdown: defer and record as Breaking vs wire BrowseList `onSecondaryActivate` to the details pane's primary action; (e) multi-install from a single-project browse selection: defer and record as Changed vs add an 'Install in several projects…' secondary action. Sessions execute; the owner decides and merges.
- Wave 1 — rows (≈2 days, merge before release; one green commit per item, tests per item, gate `npm test` then `npm run test:ui -- --storage /tmp/nuiget-ui` with the PNGs attached to the handoff). F1: shared `packageIcon(iconUrl?)` helper in src/webview/shared that reads `#root[data-package-icon]` once, returns `<img className="nuiget-package-icon" src={data: URI ?? default} alt="" onError→default>`, a reserved fixed-width icon slot in Row for comfortable density; wire into InstalledProjectSection, UpdatesTab, AllProjectsUpdates, TransitiveSection and BrowseList; update BrowseList.test.tsx:80-81 to expect the default (closes MI1, MU1, MU2, MB2, DP1, DP2, DP5). F2: `by <authors>` composed into `description` and a `<Badge label="Verified" tone="info">` on the same lists per decision (a) (MI2, MU1, DP1, DP2, DP6a); tests extend the existing `transitive/metadata`/`updates/get` fixtures with iconUrl/authors/verified and assert img src, 'by …' and the badge. F3: comfortable Row layout per decision (b) in primitives.css (+ Row.tsx if stacked): `.nuiget-row-title { flex-shrink: 0 }` or a floor, `.nuiget-row-description { min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis }`, two-line stack under `.nuiget-row--comfortable` only, retune `--nuiget-row-height-comfortable` together with `ROW_HEIGHT` in Listbox.tsx:8 (webview-tokens lint), compact untouched (MI3, MB1, MU11, MH5, DP7). F4: TransitiveSection and AllProjectsTransitiveSection to comfortable density with onSelect; allProjects slice asks `transitive/metadata` for unique id@version pairs after the stream (MI4, DP6). F5: host — exempt or decay the icon source breaker without an address check (DP3) and make `liteLookup` cache only found/missing, never failed (DP4). Mechanical gate B lands with F1-F3.
- Wave 2 — feedback and chrome (≈1.5 days, merge before release). F6: extension.ts subscriber on operation outcomes → `showWarningMessage` on failed/partial/rejected, `showInformationMessage` on success, `withProgress` fed by `ops/progress` current/total for bulk kinds, reveal the output channel on failure; handlers/extension test asserting one toast per status (closes MU4 and the single-op notification gap; satisfies §4:64 and §5:75-76). F7: header 'Restore after operations' checkbox in MainShell bound to `session.restoreEnabled` with a MainShell.test.tsx case mirroring :84-92 (MH1); amend interaction-model §1, README.md:57-58, CHANGELOG. F8: MainShell `ProgressBar` under the search box on any revalidating slice (MU5, MH7); Installed tab count from `result.packages.length` (MH3a, MI8); `layout/get`/`layout/set` for the split via `Sash.onChangeEnd` (MH2); header source-health marker from `useSources().failed` (MH4); MU3 reducer union of `packageIds`/`projectPaths` in ops.tsx:72; `--nuiget-color-button-border` token on `.nuiget-button` (MH6 narrowed); a webview ErrorBoundary rendering an EmptyState with Reload. F9: docs — interaction-model §1 (authors, layout, header restore), §4 (Floating/Range row, SDK-implicit row), §5 (drop 'header' from Restore or add the action); CHANGELOG Breaking/Changed line for every deliberate removal from decisions (c)-(e); replace the README demo gif with a 2.0 recording before publishing.
- Wave 3 — sidebar (≈1 day, before release if Waves 0-2 are green, otherwise first in 2.0.1): SB2 filter-mode expanded override (0.1 d), SB5 row indent aligned with the section title plus `--nuiget-tree-indent` (0.5 d), SB4 level-3 project typography and path tooltip (0.5 d), SB3 header background/border scoped to `.nuiget-sidebar` (low, ≤1 h).
- Defer to 2.0.1 with a CHANGELOG line each: MB3 (ruled P7.2d bare-row chunk, protocol change), MB4/MB5/MB6/MB7/MH10 and SB1 per decisions (c)-(e), MB8 package size, all remaining lows (MI9-MI12, MU6-MU10, MB9-MB19, MH11-MH13, SB6-SB15, DP8-DP10), the P7.4 manual pass. Sizing: Waves 0-2 ≈ 4-4.5 days on the one labs/2.0 branch; do not publish 2.0.0 until the owner has seen the Wave 2 PNGs and the push run on labs/2.0 is green with UI/E2E required.
